  const uniq=[...new Set(targets||[])];
  const fullLines=_frontComponents(uniq).filter(line=>line&&line.length);
  const coverage=(divs.length<uniq.length)?_evenCoverageTargets(fullLines,divs.length):uniq;
  const lines=_frontComponents(coverage).filter(line=>line&&line.length),validTargets=new Set(coverage);
  if(!lines.length){for(const d of divs)if(d._botPlanRole===role){d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;d._botPlanRole='';}return;}

  const lineOf=new Map();lines.forEach((line,i)=>line.forEach(pid=>lineOf.set(pid,i)));
  const lineLoad=new Float64Array(lines.length),targetLoad=new Map();for(const pid of validTargets)targetLoad.set(pid,0);
  const quotas=_proportionalLineQuotas(lines,divs.length);

  for(const d of divs){
    if(d._botPlanRole===role&&validTargets.has(d._botPlanTargetPid)){
      const li=lineOf.get(d._botPlanTargetPid);if(li!==undefined)lineLoad[li]+=d.size||1;
      targetLoad.set(d._botPlanTargetPid,(targetLoad.get(d._botPlanTargetPid)||0)+(d.size||1));
    }else{d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;d._botPlanRole=role;}
  }

  // Repair inherited clumps. A valid old target is not sacred when one slot carries
  // several times the strength of an empty slot. Prefer moving settled stacks first.
  if(coverage.length>1){
    const totalPower=divs.reduce((z,d)=>z+(d.size||1),0),ideal=totalPower/coverage.length;
    const overloaded=[...divs].filter(d=>d._botPlanTargetPid>=0&&!d.path.length&&d.provId===d._botPlanTargetPid).sort((a,b)=>{
      const as=(a.path.length?1:0),bs=(b.path.length?1:0);if(as!==bs)return as-bs;
      return (targetLoad.get(b._botPlanTargetPid)||0)-(targetLoad.get(a._botPlanTargetPid)||0);
    });
    for(const d of overloaded){const pid=d._botPlanTargetPid,p=targetLoad.get(pid)||0;if(p<=ideal*1.35+(d.size||1)*0.25)continue;
      let min=Infinity;for(const v of targetLoad.values())if(v<min)min=v;if(min>=ideal*0.85)break;
      const li=lineOf.get(pid);if(li!==undefined)lineLoad[li]=Math.max(0,lineLoad[li]-(d.size||1));targetLoad.set(pid,Math.max(0,p-(d.size||1)));
      d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;if(!d.path.length){d.moveProgress=0;}
    }
  }

  for(const d of divs){
    if(provinces[d.provId]?.isSea)continue;
    const existing=d._botPlanTargetPid;
    if(existing>=0){if(d.provId===existing)continue;if(d.path.length&&d.path[d.path.length-1]===existing)continue;}
    let chosen=existing,chosenPath=null,chosenLine=existing>=0?lineOf.get(existing):-1;
    if(chosen>=0){const path=_botStrictPath(d.provId,chosen,civ.id);if(path&&path.length>1)chosenPath=path;else if(d.provId!==chosen)chosen=-1;}
    if(chosen<0){
      const lineOrder=lines.map((line,i)=>{let near=1e12;for(const pid of line)near=Math.min(near,_provPlanDist(d.provId,pid));const desiredPower=(quotas[i]||0)*Math.max(1,DIV_TIERS[0]?.size||1);return{i,deficit:desiredPower-lineLoad[i],density:lineLoad[i]/Math.max(1,line.length),near};})
        .filter(x=>(quotas[x.i]||0)>0).sort((a,b)=>b.deficit-a.deficit||a.density-b.density||a.near-b.near||a.i-b.i);
      for(const lo of lineOrder){
        const candidates=[...lines[lo.i]].sort((a,b)=>((targetLoad.get(a)||0)-(targetLoad.get(b)||0))*1e9+_provPlanDist(d.provId,a)-_provPlanDist(d.provId,b));
        for(const pid of candidates){if(d.provId===pid){chosen=pid;chosenPath=[pid];chosenLine=lo.i;break;}const path=_botStrictPath(d.provId,pid,civ.id);if(path&&path.length>1){chosen=pid;chosenPath=path;chosenLine=lo.i;break;}}if(chosen>=0)break;
      }
    }
    if(chosen<0)continue;d._botPlanRole=role;d._botPlanTargetPid=chosen;
    if(chosenPath&&chosenPath.length>1){d.moveProgress=0;d.path=chosenPath.slice(1);}else if(d.provId===chosen){d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;}
    if(chosenLine!==undefined&&chosenLine>=0)lineLoad[chosenLine]+=d.size||1;targetLoad.set(chosen,(targetLoad.get(chosen)||0)+(d.size||1));
  }
}

// Massive-army bot routing ------------------------------------------------------
// Above this threshold, never run one BFS / target-sort per division. Build a small
// set of reverse BFS lanes once for the whole civ, then every fox reads one next hop.
// Wartime mass armies are intentionally offensive: no quiet-border reserve is carved
// out here; all available land forces flow toward the active war front and spread by lane.
const BOT_MASS_FLOW_THRESHOLD=512,BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES=32;
let _botMassQ=new Int32Array(0),_botMassSeen=new Uint32Array(0),_botMassSeenStamp=1;
function _ensureBotMassScratch(){const n=provinces.length;if(_botMassQ.length!==n){_botMassQ=new Int32Array(n);_botMassSeen=new Uint32Array(n);_botMassSeenStamp=1;}}
function _botMassNodeLegal(pid,civId,role,allowSea=false){const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)return false;if(p.isSea)return allowSea;const o=provOwner[pid];if(o===civId||(o>=0&&areAllied(civId,o)))return true;return role==='attack'&&o>=0&&areAtWar(civId,o);}
function _buildOneBotMassFlow(out,seeds,civId,role,allowSea=false,distOut=null,targetOut=null){
  out.fill(-1);if(distOut)distOut.fill(distOut.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT===2?65535:0xffffffff);if(targetOut)targetOut.fill(-1);if(!seeds.length)return;_ensureBotMassScratch();let st=(++_botMassSeenStamp)>>>0;if(st===0){_botMassSeen.fill(0);_botMassSeenStamp=st=1;}let h=0,t=0;
  for(let i=0;i<seeds.length;i++){
    const pid=seeds[i]|0;if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||!_botMassNodeLegal(pid,civId,role,allowSea)||_botMassSeen[pid]===st)continue;
    _botMassSeen[pid]=st;out[pid]=pid;if(distOut)distOut[pid]=0;if(targetOut)targetOut[pid]=pid;_botMassQ[t++]=pid;
  }
  while(h<t){const cur=_botMassQ[h++];for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
    const n=_adjList[ai];if(n<0||n>=provinces.length||_botMassSeen[n]===st||!_botMassNodeLegal(n,civId,role,allowSea))continue;
    _botMassSeen[n]=st;out[n]=cur;if(distOut)distOut[n]=distOut[cur]+1;if(targetOut)targetOut[n]=targetOut[cur];_botMassQ[t++]=n;
  }}
}
function _buildBotMassFlow(civ,targets,role){
  if(!civ)return false;const uniq=[...new Set(targets||[])].filter(pid=>pid>=0&&pid<provinces.length).sort((a,b)=>a-b),n=provinces.length;
  const laneCount=uniq.length?Math.min(BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES,uniq.length):0;
  const signature=role+'|'+n+'|'+civ.foxes.length+'|'+_routeLegalStamp(civ.id)+'|'+uniq.join(',');
  if(civ._botMassFlowActive&&civ._botMassFlowSignature===signature)return true;
  civ._botMassFlowSignature=signature;
  let lanes=civ._botMassFlow;if(!lanes||lanes.length!==BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES||lanes[0]?.length!==n)lanes=civ._botMassFlow=Array.from({length:BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES},()=>new Int32Array(n));
  let sea=civ._botMassSeaFlow;if(!sea||sea.length!==BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES||sea[0]?.length!==n)sea=civ._botMassSeaFlow=Array.from({length:BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES},()=>new Int32Array(n));
  let fb=civ._botMassFallbackTarget;if(!fb||fb.length!==BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES){fb=civ._botMassFallbackTarget=new Int32Array(BOT_MASS_FLOW_LANES);fb.fill(-1);}
  civ._botMassLaneCount=laneCount;civ._botMassFlowRole=role;civ._botMassFlowActive=!!uniq.length;civ._botMassTargets=new Set(uniq);
  if(!uniq.length){civ._autoCoverage=null;return false;}
  const seedLanes=_stableFlowSeedLanes(uniq,laneCount,(0x63d83595^Math.imul(civ.id+1,0x9e3779b1))>>>0,fb);
  const alive=[];for(let i=0;i<civ.foxes.length;i++){const d=civ.foxes[i];if(d.hp>0)alive.push(d);}
  civ._autoCoverage=_newAutoCoverage(civ.id,role==='attack'?'war':'friendly',uniq,seedLanes,lanes.slice(0,laneCount),sea.slice(0,laneCount),
    (out,seeds,allowSea,dist,target)=>{if(role==='attack'||!allowSea)_buildOneBotMassFlow(out,seeds,civ.id,role,allowSea,dist,target);else{out.fill(-1);if(dist)dist.fill(dist.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT===2?65535:0xffffffff);if(target)target.fill(-1);}},alive);
  return true;
}
function _botMassCommittedRoute(div,civ){
  const state=civ?._autoCoverage;if(!div||!civ?._botMassFlowActive||!state)return null;
  if(state.mode==='friendly'&&div._autoCoverageState===state&&div._autoCoveragePid===div.provId&&state.targets.has(div.provId))return null;
  _releaseAutoCoverage(div);return _pickAutoCoverageRoute(state,div);
}
function _botMassNextPid(div,civ){const r=_botMassCommittedRoute(div,civ);return r?.path?.[0]??-1;}
function _botMassKick(div,civ){
  if(civ?._armyPlanDirty)return false;
  if(!div||div.hp<=0||!civ||div.civId!==civ.id||div.path.length||div._blockedParked||div.combatCooldown>0)return false;
  const route=_botMassCommittedRoute(div,civ);if(!route||!route.path.length)return false;
  _reserveAutoCoverage(civ._autoCoverage,div,route.target);div._botPlanRole=civ._botMassFlowRole||'front';div._botPlanTargetPid=route.target;div.moveProgress=0;div.path=route.path;return true;
}
function _botMassAssignFlow(civ,divs,targets,role){
  if(!_buildBotMassFlow(civ,targets,role))return false;
  const state=civ._autoCoverage;
  for(const d of divs){
    if(role!=='attack'&&provinces[d.provId]?.isSea)continue;
    if(d._autoCoverageState!==state)_retargetAutoCoverage(d,state);
    else if(!d.path.length)_botMassKick(d,civ);
  }
  return true;
}

function _botMassAttackTargets(civ,enemies,warFront){
  const out=[],seen=new Set(),enemyStamp=_markBotEnemyIds(enemies||[]);
  // Attack the ACTIVE ENEMY EDGE, not every deep enemy province. This keeps the wave
  // distributed across the frontline and prevents route targets from jumping around whenever
  // ownership changes in the interior.
  for(const pid of warFront||[])for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){
    const n=_adjList[ai],o=provOwner[n];if(o>=0&&_botEnemyMarks[o]===enemyStamp&&!seen.has(n)){seen.add(n);out.push(n);}
  }
  if(out.length)return out.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  // No direct land contact (island/overseas war): keep one stable reachable beachhead per enemy.
  for(const eid of enemies||[]){const nearest=_botNearestEnemyTarget(civ,eid);if(nearest>=0&&!seen.has(nearest)){seen.add(nearest);out.push(nearest);}}
  return out.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
}

function _botPlanArmy(civ,enemies,force=false){
  if(!civ||!civ.foxes.length) return {warFront:[],warFrontSet:new Set(),allBorders:[],frontDivs:[],guardDivs:[]};

  const enemySig=[...(enemies||[])].sort((a,b)=>a-b).join(',');
  if(civ._armyPlanEnemySig!==enemySig){
    force=true;
    const enteringWar=!!enemySig&&!civ._armyPlanEnemySig;
    civ._armyPlanEnemySig=enemySig;
    // No war-time stack split is needed: live units are never logically merged.
    if(enteringWar)civ._botGroupDirty=true;
  }
  if(!force&&civ._armyPlanCache&&!civ._armyPlanDirty&&(_simTick<(civ._armyPlanNextTick||0))) return civ._armyPlanCache;
  civ._armyPlanDirty=false;
  civ._armyPlanNextTick=_simTick+30+(civ.id%17); // same logical cooldown, no per-tick decrement/polling

  const allBorders=botBorderProvs(civ,null);
  const warFront=enemies&&enemies.length ? botBorderProvs(civ,enemies) : [];
  const divs=[];for(let i=0;i<civ.foxes.length;i++){const d=civ.foxes[i];if(d.hp<=0)continue;if(!enemies?.length&&provinces[d.provId]?.isSea)continue;divs.push(d);}
  let result;

  // ALL bot auto-move uses the shared reverse-flow planner, not only mass armies.
  // This removes the old low-count per-division BFS/sort path while preserving the same
  // peacetime border coverage and wartime broad-front objectives. One planner now scales
  // from a single bot division to 100K+ without switching algorithms or losing behavior.
  if(divs.length){
    const wartime=!!(enemies&&enemies.length),targets=wartime?_botMassAttackTargets(civ,enemies,warFront):allBorders,role=wartime?'attack':'border';
    if(targets.length)_botMassAssignFlow(civ,divs,targets,role);
    else{civ._botMassFlowActive=false;for(const d of divs){d._botPlanRole=role;d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;}}
    result={warFront,warFrontSet:new Set(warFront),allBorders,frontDivs:wartime?divs:[],guardDivs:wartime?[]:divs};
    civ._armyPlanCache=result;return result;
  }

  if(!enemies||!enemies.length){
    civ._botMassFlowActive=false;
    // PEACETIME: every army belongs to the national-border plan.
    for(const d of divs){
      if(d._botPlanRole!=='border'){d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;}
      d._botPlanRole='border';
    }
    _botAssignBalancedPlan(civ,divs,allBorders,'border');
    result={warFront,warFrontSet:new Set(warFront),allBorders,frontDivs:[],guardDivs:divs};
    civ._armyPlanCache=result;
    return result;
  }


}

let _botEnemyMarks=new Uint32Array(0),_botEnemyMarkStamp=1;
function _markBotEnemyIds(ids){
  const n=civs.length;if(_botEnemyMarks.length<n)_botEnemyMarks=new Uint32Array(Math.max(n,_botEnemyMarks.length*2||256));
  let st=(++_botEnemyMarkStamp)>>>0;if(st===0){_botEnemyMarks.fill(0);_botEnemyMarkStamp=st=1;}
  for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){const id=ids[i];if(id>=0&&id<n)_botEnemyMarks[id]=st;}
  return st;
}
function botTickDivisions(civ,enemies=[],active=null){
  if(civ.id===playerCivId||!civ.foxes.length) return;

  // Movement/cooldowns remain exact every simulation tick, but stationary armies disappear
  // from the hot loop entirely.
  active=active||_ensureActiveDivSet(civ);_tickActiveDivisions(civ,active);

  // Rescue only after an arrival/state change can have created a stranded idle sea unit.
  if(!enemies.length&&(civ._botRescueInit!==true||civ._botRescueDirty)){
    civ._botRescueInit=true;civ._botRescueDirty=false;
    for(const div of civ.foxes){
      if(div.path.length>0||!(provinces[div.provId]&&provinces[div.provId].isSea))continue;
      const rescue=_botNearestOwnedLand(div.provId,civ.id);
      if(rescue!==null){const path=_botStrictPath(div.provId,rescue,civ.id);if(path&&path.length>1){div.path=path.slice(1);div._botPlanRole='rescue';div._botPlanTargetPid=rescue;}}
    }
  }

  botAutoGroup(civ,enemies.length>0);

  // Validate only currently moving/cooldown divisions, not the whole army.
  if(active&&(civ._activeDivCount||0))for(const div of active){
    if(!div||!div._simActive||!div.path.length)continue;
    const dest=div.path[div.path.length-1],plannedOwnDest=(div._botPlanRole==='border'||div._botPlanRole==='front'||div._botPlanRole==='rescue');
    if(provOwner[dest]===civ.id&&!plannedOwnDest&&!civ._botMassFlowActive){div.path=[];div.moveProgress=0;continue;}
    if(provinces[dest]&&provinces[dest].isSea&&div._botPlanRole!=='rescue'&&!(enemies.length&&civ._botMassFlowActive)){div.path=[];div.moveProgress=0;continue;}
    const next=div.path[0],nOwner=provOwner[next];
    if(nOwner>=0&&nOwner!==civ.id&&!areAtWar(civ.id,nOwner)&&!areAllied(civ.id,nOwner)&&!(provinces[next]&&provinces[next].isSea)){div.path=[];div.moveProgress=0;div._botPlanTargetPid=-1;}
  }

  let plan=civ._armyPlanCache;
  const enemySig=_botWarEnemySig[civ.id]||'';
  if(civ._armyPlanEnemySig!==enemySig){const enteringWar=!!enemySig&&!civ._armyPlanEnemySig;civ._armyPlanEnemySig=enemySig;civ._armyPlanDirty=true;if(enteringWar)civ._botGroupDirty=true;}
  if(!plan||civ._armyPlanDirty||_simTick>=(civ._armyPlanNextTick||0))plan=_botPlanArmy(civ,enemies,true);

  if(enemies.length>0){
    // Massive armies attack through the shared offensive field itself. Do not rescan
    // tens of thousands of foxes every few logical ticks for individual retargeting.
    if(civ._botMassFlowActive)return;
    // Individual retarget timers in the old loop all used a 6-frame countdown. Process the
    // tactical idle scan once per seven logical ticks, plus immediately after arrivals/front changes.
    if(!civ._botTacticalDirty&&_simTick<(civ._botNextTacticalTick||0))return;
    civ._botTacticalDirty=false;civ._botNextTacticalTick=_simTick+4;
    const warFrontSet=plan.warFrontSet||(plan.warFrontSet=new Set(plan.warFront));
    const enemyStamp=_markBotEnemyIds(enemies),attackClaims=new Map();
    let tacticalDivs=civ.foxes;
    if(civ._botMassFlowActive&&plan.warFront?.length){
      tacticalDivs=[];if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();
      for(const pid of plan.warFront){const a=_provDivIndex.get(pid)?.get(civ.id);if(a?.length)for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)tacticalDivs.push(a[i]);}
    }
    for(const div of tacticalDivs){
      if(div.path.length>0||provinces[div.provId]?.isSea||div._botPlanRole==='border'||div._botPlanRole==='rescue')continue;
      div._retargetIn=4;
      if(div._botPlanRole==='front'&&warFrontSet.has(div.provId)){
        let best=-1,bestScore=1e18,bestDist=1e18;
        for(let ai=_adjBegin(div.provId),ae=_adjEnd(div.provId);ai<ae;ai++){
          const n=_adjList[ai],own=provOwner[n];if(own<0||_botEnemyMarks[own]!==enemyStamp)continue;
          const defenders=allDivsInProv(n,own).reduce((z,x)=>z+(x.size||1),0),claimed=attackClaims.get(n)||0,dist=_provPlanDist(div.provId,n);
          // Assigned-attack power dominates defender weakness: spread the wave first,
          // then prefer softer targets. This prevents every unit drilling one corridor.
          const score=claimed*100000+defenders*1000+dist;
          if(score<bestScore){best=n;bestScore=score;bestDist=dist;}
        }
        if(best>=0){attackClaims.set(best,(attackClaims.get(best)||0)+(div.size||1));div.path=[best];div._botPlanRole='attack';div._botPlanTargetPid=-1;div._retargetIn=0;}
      }else if(div._botPlanRole==='attack'||!plan.warFront.length){
        if(plan.warFront.length){civ._armyPlanNextTick=0;civ._armyPlanDirty=true;civ._armyPlanCache=null;div._retargetIn=0;}
        else{for(const eid of enemies){botLaunchAttack(civ,eid);if(div.path.length>0){div._retargetIn=0;break;}}}
      }
    }
  }
}

// ── DIVISION DRAWING ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Draws movement arrows FIRST (behind foxes), then fox sprites on top.
// Arrows animate a travelling "pulse" dot along the path.
// When movement is blocked by combat, the arrowhead freezes before the border.

// _drawMovementArrowsBatched: replaces the old per-arrow _drawMovementArrow.
// All arrows are grouped into style buckets and drawn with minimal ctx state changes:
//   • One save/restore wrapping the entire pass
//   • setLineDash set once (on) / once (off)
//   • Lines batched by (isPlayer, selected) → ≤2 player strokes + one per bot-civ
//   • Arrowheads batched by (blocked, color) bucket → ≤4 fill passes
//   • Pulse dots batched into one arc pass
//   • Combat icons in one font-set pass
// Pre-allocated arrow rendering buffers — cleared each frame (avoids GC pressure)
const _arrowLnSelPl=[];
const _arrowLnUnselPl=[];
const _arrowLnBotBuckets=[]; // civId -> persistent {color,segs[]}
const _arrowLnBotActive=[];
const _arrowAhFree=[];
const _arrowAhBlocked=[];
const _arrowDots=[];
const _arrowSwords=[];
const _arrowPtsPool=[];let _arrowPtsUsed=0;
// Route arrays are replaced or shortened with shift(); their world-space province centers
// therefore stay stable between route changes. Cache those centers once per path array so
// rendering movement arrows does not repeatedly chase province objects on every frame.
const _arrowPathWorldCache=new WeakMap();
function _arrowPathWorld(path){
  let rec=_arrowPathWorldCache.get(path),n=path.length,first=n?path[0]:-1,last=n?path[n-1]:-1;
  if(rec&&rec.n===n&&rec.first===first&&rec.last===last)return rec;
  const xy=new Float32Array(n*2);let hasSea=false,allValid=true,k=0,valid=0,fx=NaN,fy=NaN,lx=NaN,ly=NaN,px=NaN,py=NaN;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const p=provinces[path[i]];if(p){const x=p.centerTx+0.5,y=p.centerTy+0.5;xy[k++]=x;xy[k++]=y;if(p.isSea)hasSea=true;if(valid===0){fx=x;fy=y;}else{px=lx;py=ly;}lx=x;ly=y;valid++;}else{allValid=false;xy[k++]=NaN;xy[k++]=NaN;}}
  rec={n,first,last,xy,hasSea,allValid,valid,fx,fy,lx,ly,px,py};_arrowPathWorldCache.set(path,rec);return rec;
}
const _arrowHeadPool=[];let _arrowHeadUsed=0;
const _arrowDotPool=[];let _arrowDotUsed=0;
const _arrowSwordPool=[];let _arrowSwordUsed=0;
function _pooledArrowPts(){let a=_arrowPtsPool[_arrowPtsUsed];if(!a)a=_arrowPtsPool[_arrowPtsUsed]=[];_arrowPtsUsed++;a.length=0;return a;}
function _pushArrowHead(dst,x,y,ang,ar,color,alpha){let o=_arrowHeadPool[_arrowHeadUsed];if(!o)o=_arrowHeadPool[_arrowHeadUsed]={};_arrowHeadUsed++;o.x=x;o.y=y;o.ang=ang;o.ar=ar;o.color=color;o.alpha=alpha;dst.push(o);}
function _pushArrowDot(dst,x,y,r,alpha){let o=_arrowDotPool[_arrowDotUsed];if(!o)o=_arrowDotPool[_arrowDotUsed]={};_arrowDotUsed++;o.x=x;o.y=y;o.r=r;o.alpha=alpha;dst.push(o);}
function _pushArrowSword(dst,x,y,size,alpha){let o=_arrowSwordPool[_arrowSwordUsed];if(!o)o=_arrowSwordPool[_arrowSwordUsed]={};_arrowSwordUsed++;o.x=x;o.y=y;o.size=size;o.alpha=alpha;dst.push(o);}

function _drawMovementArrowsBatched(tpx,renderDivs=_renderMovingDivs){
  const sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height;
  if(!renderDivs.length)return;
  const playerColor=playerCivId>=0&&civs[playerCivId]?civs[playerCivId].color:'#fff';
  let any=false;
  ctx.save();ctx.setLineDash([7,5]);

  // Draw a pre-grouped civ array directly. _renderByCiv already preserves the same
  // per-civ encounter order as _renderDivs, so this removes redundant global rescans
  // without changing line geometry, culling, styles or layering.
  function emitArray(arr,selectionMode=-1){
    if(!arr?.length)return;let begun=false;
    for(let di=0,n=arr.length;di<n;di++){
      const d=arr[di];if(!d||d.path.length===0)continue;
      if(selectionMode===1&&!d._renderSelected)continue;
      if(selectionMode===0&&d._renderSelected)continue;
      const hp=provinces[d.provId];if(!hp)continue;
      const sx0=(hp.centerTx+0.5-camX)*tpx,sy0=(hp.centerTy+0.5-camY)*tpx;
      if(sx0<-600||sx0>sw+600||sy0<-400||sy0>sh+400)continue;
      const route=_arrowPathWorld(d.path),rxy=route.xy;if(rxy.length<2)continue;
      let drew=false;if(!begun){ctx.beginPath();begun=true;}ctx.moveTo(sx0,sy0);
      if(route.allValid){for(let ri=0;ri<rxy.length;ri+=2){ctx.lineTo((rxy[ri]-camX)*tpx,(rxy[ri+1]-camY)*tpx);drew=true;}}
      else for(let ri=0;ri<rxy.length;ri+=2){const wx=rxy[ri],wy=rxy[ri+1];if(!Number.isFinite(wx)||!Number.isFinite(wy))continue;ctx.lineTo((wx-camX)*tpx,(wy-camY)*tpx);drew=true;}
      if(drew)any=true;
    }
    if(begun)ctx.stroke();
  }

  const playerArr=playerCivId>=0?_renderMovingByCiv[playerCivId]:null;
  ctx.strokeStyle=playerColor;ctx.globalAlpha=.82;ctx.lineWidth=2.5;emitArray(playerArr,1);
  ctx.globalAlpha=.45;ctx.lineWidth=1.4;emitArray(playerArr,0);
  ctx.globalAlpha=.22;ctx.lineWidth=1.4;
  // Moving bot civs were recorded in exact first-encounter order while _renderDivs was built.
  for(let i=0;i<_arrowLnBotActive.length;i++){const cid=_arrowLnBotActive[i],c=civs[cid];if(!c)continue;ctx.strokeStyle=c.color;emitArray(_renderMovingByCiv[cid],-1);}
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  if(!any){ctx.restore();return;}

  // Decorations are streamed in render order. No persistent 100k-object pools.
  // Visual formulas are intentionally identical to the previous renderer.
  let dotBegun=false;ctx.fillStyle='#fff8d0';
  for(let di=0;di<renderDivs.length;di++){
    const d=renderDivs[di];if(!d||d.path.length===0)continue;
    const hp=provinces[d.provId],civ=civs[d.civId];if(!hp||!civ)continue;
    const sx0=(hp.centerTx+0.5-camX)*tpx,sy0=(hp.centerTy+0.5-camY)*tpx;
    if(sx0<-600||sx0>sw+600||sy0<-400||sy0>sh+400)continue;
    const route=_arrowPathWorld(d.path);if(route.valid<1)continue;
    const fx=(route.fx-camX)*tpx,fy=(route.fy-camY)*tpx,lx=(route.lx-camX)*tpx,ly=(route.ly-camY)*tpx;
    const px=route.valid>1?(route.px-camX)*tpx:sx0,py=route.valid>1?(route.py-camY)*tpx:sy0;
    const isPlayer=d.civId===playerCivId,blocked=d._nextIsBlocked(),prog=d.moveProgress;
    const routeColor=(!!hp.isSea||route.hasSea)?'#4ac8ff':civ.color;
    const x=blocked?sx0+(fx-sx0)*prog:lx,y=blocked?sy0+(fy-sy0)*prog:ly;
    const ang=blocked?Math.atan2(fy-sy0,fx-sx0):Math.atan2(ly-py,lx-px);
    const ar=d._renderSelected?10:7,alpha=isPlayer?(d._renderSelected?.92:.55):.28;
    ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=blocked?'#e06030':routeColor;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x-ar*Math.cos(ang-.38),y-ar*Math.sin(ang-.38));ctx.lineTo(x-ar*Math.cos(ang+.38),y-ar*Math.sin(ang+.38));ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
    if(!blocked){const dx=sx0+(fx-sx0)*prog,dy=sy0+(fy-sy0)*prog,r=d._renderSelected?4:2.5;ctx.globalAlpha=isPlayer?.9:.4;ctx.fillStyle='#fff8d0';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(dx,dy,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
    if(blocked){const pulse=isPlayer?_playerFoxBlockPulse(d._animSeed||0):(.7+.3*Math.sin(d.frame*.15));ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillStyle='#ff8040';ctx.font=`${d._renderSelected?13:9}px serif`;ctx.globalAlpha=isPlayer?pulse*.9:pulse*.4;ctx.fillText('⚔',sx0+(fx-sx0)*.5,sy0+(fy-sy0)*.5);}
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.restore();
}

// Legacy stub kept so any external call sites don't break
function _drawMovementArrow(d, civColor){ /* replaced by _drawMovementArrowsBatched */ }

// ── Province stacking layout ──────────────────────────────────────
// Each frame, assigns tileX/Y to every division based on its slot
// index within its province, forming a compact grid so units are
// visible side-by-side rather than piled on the same pixel.
//
// Grid grows right then up:
//   col 0..N-1  →  row 0 (bottom)
//   col 0..N-1  →  row 1 (one step up)
//   …
// The whole block is centred on the province centre.

const _STACK_COLS = 4;          // max divisions per row
const _STACK_SX   = 1.85;       // horizontal spacing  (tile units)
const _STACK_SY   = 1.55;       // vertical spacing    (tile units, upward)

// ── ULTRA-LIGHT UNIT RENDER INDEX ─────────────────────────────────
// Rendering is province/viewport driven: off-screen armies no longer participate in
// stacking, sprite selection, arrow building, HP queues, or label/badge work.
// Gameplay arrays remain untouched — this is render-only and loses no mechanics.
let _provRenderMinX=null,_provRenderMaxX=null,_provRenderMinY=null,_provRenderMaxY=null;
// Coarse province buckets avoid scanning every occupied province every render frame.
// Provinces are inserted into every 64-tile cell touched by their AABB, so large/irregular
// provinces still appear as soon as any part of them reaches the viewport.
const _PROV_RENDER_BUCKET=64;
let _provRenderBuckets=null,_provRenderBucketCols=0,_provRenderBucketRows=0;
// Dynamic companion index: only provinces that currently contain at least one division.
// Static province geometry stays untouched; occupancy transitions update a handful of coarse
// buckets so each render frame never scans empty provinces just to discover they have no army.
let _occupiedRenderBuckets=null,_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty=true;
let _renderProvStamp=null,_renderProvStampId=1;
const _renderProvCandidates=[];
function _markOccupiedRenderBucketsDirty(){_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty=true;}
function _forProvRenderBucketRange(pid,fn){
  if(!_provRenderMinX||pid<0||pid>=provinces.length)return;
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,(_provRenderMinX[pid]/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
  const bx1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,(_provRenderMaxX[pid]/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
  const by0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,(_provRenderMinY[pid]/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
  const by1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,(_provRenderMaxY[pid]/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++)fn(by*_provRenderBucketCols+bx);
}
function _ensureOccupiedRenderBuckets(){
  if(!_provRenderBuckets||!_provRenderMinX||_provRenderMinX.length!==provinces.length)_buildProvinceRenderBounds();
  const need=_provRenderBucketCols*_provRenderBucketRows;
  if(!_occupiedRenderBuckets||_occupiedRenderBuckets.length!==need){_occupiedRenderBuckets=Array.from({length:need},()=>new Set());_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty=true;}
  if(!_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty)return;
  for(const b of _occupiedRenderBuckets)b.clear();
  if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();
  for(const pid of _provDivIndex.keys())_forProvRenderBucketRange(pid,bi=>_occupiedRenderBuckets[bi].add(pid));
  _occupiedRenderBucketsDirty=false;
}
function _occupiedRenderAdd(pid){
  if(_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty||!_occupiedRenderBuckets||!_provRenderMinX)return;
  _forProvRenderBucketRange(pid,bi=>_occupiedRenderBuckets[bi].add(pid));
}
function _occupiedRenderRemove(pid){
  if(_occupiedRenderBucketsDirty||!_occupiedRenderBuckets||!_provRenderMinX)return;
  _forProvRenderBucketRange(pid,bi=>_occupiedRenderBuckets[bi].delete(pid));
}
function _buildProvinceRenderBounds(){
  const n=provinces.length;
  _provRenderMinX=new Int16Array(n); _provRenderMaxX=new Int16Array(n);
  _provRenderMinY=new Int16Array(n); _provRenderMaxY=new Int16Array(n);
  _provRenderBucketCols=Math.ceil(WORLD_W/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET);
  _provRenderBucketRows=Math.ceil(WORLD_H/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET);
  _provRenderBuckets=Array.from({length:_provRenderBucketCols*_provRenderBucketRows},()=>[]);
  _renderProvStamp=new Uint32Array(n);_renderProvStampId=1;
  for(let pid=0;pid<n;pid++){
    const p=provinces[pid];
    let minX=p?.minX,maxX=p?.maxX,minY=p?.minY,maxY=p?.maxY;
    if(minX==null||maxX==null||minY==null||maxY==null){
      minX=WORLD_W;maxX=0;minY=WORLD_H;maxY=0;
      if(p&&p.tiles&&p.tiles.length){for(const ti of p.tiles){const x=ti%WORLD_W,y=(ti/WORLD_W)|0;if(x<minX)minX=x;if(x>maxX)maxX=x;if(y<minY)minY=y;if(y>maxY)maxY=y;}}
      else{const x=p?.centerTx||0,y=p?.centerTy||0;minX=maxX=x;minY=maxY=y;}
      if(p){p.minX=minX;p.maxX=maxX;p.minY=minY;p.maxY=maxY;}
    }
    _provRenderMinX[pid]=minX;_provRenderMaxX[pid]=maxX;
    _provRenderMinY[pid]=minY;_provRenderMaxY[pid]=maxY;
    const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,(minX/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
    const bx1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,(maxX/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
    const by0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,(minY/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
    const by1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,(maxY/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)|0));
    for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++)_provRenderBuckets[by*_provRenderBucketCols+bx].push(pid);
  }
  _occupiedRenderBucketsDirty=true;
}
function _provIntersectsRenderView(pid,l,r,t,b){
  if(!_provRenderMinX||_provRenderMinX.length!==provinces.length) _buildProvinceRenderBounds();
  return !(_provRenderMaxX[pid]<l||_provRenderMinX[pid]>r||_provRenderMaxY[pid]<t||_provRenderMinY[pid]>b);
}
function _collectRenderProvinceCandidates(l,r,t,b){
  if(!_renderProvStamp||_renderProvStamp.length!==provinces.length)_buildProvinceRenderBounds();
  _ensureOccupiedRenderBuckets();
  _renderProvCandidates.length=0;
  let stamp=(++_renderProvStampId)>>>0;
  if(stamp===0){_renderProvStamp.fill(0);_renderProvStampId=stamp=1;}
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,Math.floor(l/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const bx1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,Math.floor(r/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const by0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,Math.floor(t/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const by1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,Math.floor(b/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){
    const bucket=_occupiedRenderBuckets[by*_provRenderBucketCols+bx];
    for(const pid of bucket){
      if(_renderProvStamp[pid]===stamp)continue;
      _renderProvStamp[pid]=stamp;
      if(_provIntersectsRenderView(pid,l,r,t,b))_renderProvCandidates.push(pid);
    }
  }
  return _renderProvCandidates;
}
const _geometryProvCandidates=[];
function _collectGeometryProvinceCandidates(l,r,t,b){
  if(!_renderProvStamp||_renderProvStamp.length!==provinces.length)_buildProvinceRenderBounds();
  _geometryProvCandidates.length=0;
  let stamp=(++_renderProvStampId)>>>0;
  if(stamp===0){_renderProvStamp.fill(0);_renderProvStampId=stamp=1;}
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,Math.floor(l/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const bx1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketCols-1,Math.floor(r/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const by0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,Math.floor(t/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  const by1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_provRenderBucketRows-1,Math.floor(b/_PROV_RENDER_BUCKET)));
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){
    const bucket=_provRenderBuckets[by*_provRenderBucketCols+bx];
    for(let i=0;i<bucket.length;i++){
      const pid=bucket[i];
      if(_renderProvStamp[pid]===stamp)continue;
      _renderProvStamp[pid]=stamp;
      if(_provIntersectsRenderView(pid,l,r,t,b))_geometryProvCandidates.push(pid);
    }
  }
  return _geometryProvCandidates;
}
const _renderDivs=[];                 // only divisions close enough to be drawable
const _renderMovingDivs=[];           // drawable representatives with a live route only
const _renderMovingByCiv=[];          // civId -> drawable moving representatives only
let _renderBotHash=2166136261>>>0;    // folded while collecting; avoids a second all-visible-units scan
let _renderPlayerHash=2166136261>>>0,_renderPlayerMoving=false;
const _renderCivIds=[];               // visible civ ids, sorted to preserve old draw order
const _renderByCiv=[];                // civId -> reusable division[]
const _renderCivActive=[];            // sparse booleans, reset only for civs used last frame
const _renderBotMovingActive=[];      // civId -> has a visible moving bot this frame
const _slotScratch=[];                // reusable per-province division list
const _slotVisibleScratch=[];         // reusable visible leaders/bot list
const _groupLeaderScratch=new Map();
const _groupCountScratch=new Map();
function _clearUnitRenderLists(){
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    if(_renderByCiv[cid]) _renderByCiv[cid].length=0;
    if(_renderMovingByCiv[cid]) _renderMovingByCiv[cid].length=0;
    _renderCivActive[cid]=false;
  }
  for(let i=0;i<_arrowLnBotActive.length;i++)_renderBotMovingActive[_arrowLnBotActive[i]]=false;
  _arrowLnBotActive.length=0;
  _renderCivIds.length=0;
  _renderDivs.length=0;_renderMovingDivs.length=0;
  _renderBotHash=2166136261>>>0;_renderPlayerHash=2166136261>>>0;_renderPlayerMoving=false;
}
function _pushRenderableDiv(d,tpx,sw,sh,marginPx=105,knownPlayerMoving=false){
  const sx=(d.tileX-camX)*tpx,sy=(d.tileY-camY)*tpx;
  if(sx<-marginPx||sx>sw+marginPx||sy<-marginPx||sy>sh+marginPx) return;
  d._renderSx=sx;d._renderSy=sy;d._renderSelected=selectedDivs.has(d.id);
  const cid=d.civId,hasRoute=d.path.length>0;
  _renderDivs.push(d);
  if(hasRoute){_renderMovingDivs.push(d);let ma=_renderMovingByCiv[cid];if(!ma)ma=_renderMovingByCiv[cid]=[];ma.push(d);}

  if(cid!==playerCivId){if(d.path.length>0&&!_renderBotMovingActive[cid]){_renderBotMovingActive[cid]=true;_arrowLnBotActive.push(cid);}let h=_renderBotHash;h=Math.imul(h^(d.id+1),16777619)>>>0;h=Math.imul(h^((d.provId+1)*31+(d.dir<0?7:3)+(d._slotIdx+2)*13),16777619)>>>0;h=Math.imul(h^(((d.tileX*8)|0)*131+((d.tileY*8)|0)),16777619)>>>0;_renderBotHash=h;}
  else{const moving=knownPlayerMoving||(d.path.length>0&&d.moveProgress>0);if(moving)_renderPlayerMoving=true;if(!isMobile&&!_renderPlayerMoving){let h=_renderPlayerHash;h=Math.imul(h^(d.id+1),16777619)>>>0;h=Math.imul(h^((d.provId+1)*31+(d.dir<0?7:3)+(d._slotIdx+2)*13+(((((_simTick+(d._animSeed||0))>>2)>>>0)%FOX_ANIM_N)+1)*257),16777619)>>>0;_renderPlayerHash=h;}}
  let arr=_renderByCiv[cid]; if(!arr) arr=_renderByCiv[cid]=[];
  if(!_renderCivActive[cid]){_renderCivActive[cid]=true;_renderCivIds.push(cid);}
  arr.push(d);
}

function _assignProvSlots(){
  if(_playerSlotCacheCivId!==playerCivId){_playerSlotCacheCivId=playerCivId;_provPlayerSlotCache.clear();}
  if(_provDivIndexDirty) _rebuildProvDivIndex();
  _clearUnitRenderLists();

  const tpx=TS*zoom,sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height;
  // Tile-space view plus enough room for the largest fox + labels/arrows around it.
  const tileMargin=Math.max(6,120/Math.max(0.001,tpx)+2);
  const vLeft=camX-tileMargin,vRight=camX+sw/tpx+tileMargin;
  const vTop=camY-tileMargin,vBottom=camY+sh/tpx+tileMargin;

  // The sparse movement bookkeeping includes ordinary, scheduled and parked hops.
  // Only skip the global interpolation scan when none of those representations exists.
  const pc=playerCivId>=0?civs[playerCivId]:null;
  if(pc)_ensureActiveDivSet(pc);
  const playerStatic=!!pc&&!(pc._activeDivCount||pc._safeMoveNextDue||_blockedPlayerCount);
  if(pc&&!playerStatic){
    const movers=_orderedRenderMovers(pc);
    for(let fi=0,fn=movers.length;fi<fn;fi++){
      const d=movers[fi];if(!d||d.path.length===0)continue;const prog=d.moveProgress;if(prog<=0)continue;
      const cp=provinces[d.provId];if(!cp)continue;
      if(!d._nextIsBlocked()){
        const np=provinces[d.path[0]];
        if(np){
          const cx=cp.centerTx+0.5,cy=cp.centerTy+0.5;
          d.tileX=cx+((np.centerTx+0.5)-cx)*prog;
          d.tileY=cy+((np.centerTy+0.5)-cy)*prog;
        }else{d.tileX=cp.centerTx+0.5;d.tileY=cp.centerTy+0.5;}
      }else{d.tileX=cp.centerTx+0.5;d.tileY=cp.centerTy+0.5;}
      d._slotIdx=-1;d._provStackN=0;d._hiddenInGroup=false;
      _pushRenderableDiv(d,tpx,sw,sh,105,true);
    }
  }

  // Query only coarse spatial buckets touched by the viewport. This keeps the fast path
  // fast even in normal/small battles instead of scanning every occupied province globally.
  const candidatePids=_collectRenderProvinceCandidates(vLeft,vRight,vTop,vBottom);
  for(const pid of candidatePids){
    const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);if(!byCiv)continue;
    const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) continue;
    const cx=p.centerTx+0.5,cy=p.centerTy+0.5;

    // Vast majority of occupied provinces contain bots only. Their stacking layout is pure
    // world-space state and does not change when the camera moves, so cache it until the
    // province's division membership changes. Player provinces retain the exact grouping path.
    const playerHere=playerCivId>=0&&byCiv.has(playerCivId);
    if(!playerHere){
      let cached=_provBotSlotCache.get(pid);
      if(!cached){
        // Pure render grouping: one representative sprite per civ/province. Every
        // underlying bot division remains in byCiv and continues simulating independently.
        const arr=[];
        for(const a of byCiv.values()){
          if(!a?.length)continue;
          const leader=a[0];leader._provStackN=a.length;leader._hiddenInGroup=false;arr.push(leader);
          for(let i=1;i<a.length;i++){a[i]._hiddenInGroup=true;a[i]._provStackN=a.length;}
        }
        const n=arr.length,cols=Math.min(n,_STACK_COLS),rows=Math.ceil(n/_STACK_COLS),offX=(cols-1)*_STACK_SX*.5,offY=(rows-1)*_STACK_SY*.5;
        for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const d=arr[i],col=i%_STACK_COLS,row=(i/_STACK_COLS)|0;d.tileX=cx+col*_STACK_SX-offX;d.tileY=cy-row*_STACK_SY+offY;d._slotIdx=i;}
        cached=arr;_provBotSlotCache.set(pid,cached);
      }
      for(const d of cached)_pushRenderableDiv(d,tpx,sw,sh);
      continue;
    }

    if(playerStatic){
      const cached=_provPlayerSlotCache.get(pid);
      if(cached&&cached.player===playerCivId){for(const d of cached.visible)_pushRenderableDiv(d,tpx,sw,sh);continue;}
    }
    _slotScratch.length=0;
    for(const arr of byCiv.values()){
      for(const d of arr){
        // Moving player units were already interpolated/pushed above.
        if(d.civId===playerCivId&&d.path.length>0&&d.moveProgress>0) continue;
        _slotScratch.push(d);
      }
    }
    if(!_slotScratch.length) continue;

    _groupLeaderScratch.clear();_groupCountScratch.clear();
    let ungroupedFirst=null,ungroupedCount=0;

    // One linear pass finds group leaders/counts and the ungrouped player representative.
    for(const d of _slotScratch){
      if(d.civId!==playerCivId) continue;
      if(d._vGroupId!=null){
        const gid=d._vGroupId;
        _groupCountScratch.set(gid,(_groupCountScratch.get(gid)||0)+1);
        if(!_groupLeaderScratch.has(gid)) _groupLeaderScratch.set(gid,d);
      }else{
        ungroupedCount++;
        if(!ungroupedFirst) ungroupedFirst=d;
      }
    }

    // Preserve the original hide/group behaviour without the old nested counting scans.
    for(const d of _slotScratch){
      if(d.civId!==playerCivId) continue;
      if(d._vGroupId!=null){
        const leader=_groupLeaderScratch.get(d._vGroupId);
        d._vGroupCount=_groupCountScratch.get(d._vGroupId)||1;
        d._hiddenInGroup=d!==leader;
        if(d._hiddenInGroup){d.tileX=cx;d.tileY=cy;d._slotIdx=-1;d._provStackN=0;}
      }else{
        const vis=d===ungroupedFirst;
        d._hiddenInGroup=!vis;
        d._provStackN=ungroupedCount;
        if(!vis){d.tileX=cx;d.tileY=cy;d._slotIdx=-1;}
      }
    }

    _slotVisibleScratch.length=0;
    for(const leader of _groupLeaderScratch.values()) _slotVisibleScratch.push(leader);
    if(ungroupedFirst) _slotVisibleScratch.push(ungroupedFirst);
    for(const d of _slotScratch){
      if(d.civId===playerCivId) continue;
      d._hiddenInGroup=false;
      _slotVisibleScratch.push(d);
    }

    const n=_slotVisibleScratch.length;
    const cols=Math.min(n,_STACK_COLS),rows=Math.ceil(n/_STACK_COLS);
    const offX=(cols-1)*_STACK_SX*0.5,offY=(rows-1)*_STACK_SY*0.5;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const d=_slotVisibleScratch[i],col=i%_STACK_COLS,row=(i/_STACK_COLS)|0;
      d.tileX=cx+col*_STACK_SX-offX;
      d.tileY=cy-row*_STACK_SY+offY;
      d._slotIdx=i;d._hiddenInGroup=false;
      if(d.civId===playerCivId&&d._vGroupId==null) d._provStackN=ungroupedCount;
      else d._provStackN=0;
      _pushRenderableDiv(d,tpx,sw,sh);
    }
    // A strict entry bound prevents view-only caches from growing without limit.
    // At the bound, keep the ordinary path rather than churning an LRU every frame.
    if(playerStatic&&_provPlayerSlotCache.size<_PLAYER_SLOT_CACHE_MAX)_provPlayerSlotCache.set(pid,{player:playerCivId,visible:_slotVisibleScratch.slice()});
  }

  _renderCivIds.sort((a,b)=>a-b); // same civ-major layering as the old renderer
  return _renderDivs;
}


// ── PER-FRAME FOX SCALE CACHE ──────────────────────────────────────
// sz and S depend only on zoom — compute once per frame.
let _foxSzCache=-1, _foxSCache=0;
function _foxS(){
  if(_foxSzCache!==zoom){
    _foxSzCache=zoom;
    const sz=Math.max(9,Math.min(55,zoom*42+11));
    _foxSCache=sz/68;
  }
  return _foxSCache;
}

// FOX VISUAL CONSISTENCY
// No geometric LOD substitution: every zoom level uses the same cached fox artwork.
// The sprite is simply scaled smaller when zoomed far out; instanced WebGL and visual-only
// province grouping keep this fast without changing the unit silhouette.

// Reused full-detail buffers — no large arrays/objects allocated every render frame.
const _foxBadgeQueue=[]; // flat x,y,n,borderColor,textColor
const _foxSelRings=[];
const _foxHpBars=[];
const _foxMarchDots=[];
const _foxAnchors=[];
const _foxTierRenderCache=[];
function _queueFoxOverlaysOnly(arr,S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors){
  if(!arr)return;
  for(const d of arr){
    if(d._hiddenInGroup)continue;
    const isPlayer=d.civId===playerCivId;
    const hasBadge=isPlayer?((d._vGroupId!=null&&d._vGroupCount>1)||(d._vGroupId==null&&d._provStackN>1)):((d._provStackN||1)>1||(d.divCount||1)>1||d.size>1);
    if(!d._renderSelected&&!hasBadge&&d.path.length===0&&d.hp>=d.maxHp&&!provinces[d.provId]?.isSea)continue;
    d.draw(S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors,_foxTierRenderCache,false);
  }
}


// Large-army rendering intentionally stays on the exact cached fox-sprite path below.
// Logical divisions remain independent; only render representatives are grouped visually.

function drawDivisions(){
  if(!showFoxes)return;
  const tpx=TS*zoom,S=_foxS();
  const visible=_assignProvSlots();

  // One art pipeline in every situation: low/high zoom, small/huge armies, WebGL or Canvas.
  // _renderFoxBodiesGL() uses the exact same cached sprite canvases as Division.draw().
  ctx.save();
  // Compute armour/boot tiers only for civs that actually have a visible fox.
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds)_foxTierRenderCache[cid]=_foxTierInfo(cid);

  const badgeQueue=(_foxBadgeQueue.length=0,_foxBadgeQueue);
  const selRings=(_foxSelRings.length=0,_foxSelRings);
  const hpBars=(_foxHpBars.length=0,_foxHpBars);
  const marchDots=(_foxMarchDots.length=0,_foxMarchDots);
  const anchors=(_foxAnchors.length=0,_foxAnchors);

  _drawMovementArrowsBatched(tpx,_renderMovingDivs);

  // Large battles: instanced WebGL draws the exact cached sprites in one call.
  // If WebGL2 is unavailable/atlas capacity is exceeded, retain the proven Canvas2D layer path.
  _clearFoxPassSprites();_foxSpritePassActive=true;
  try{
  const useGL=_renderFoxBodiesGL(S);
  if(useGL){
    for(const cid of _renderCivIds)_queueFoxOverlaysOnly(_renderByCiv[cid],S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors);
  }else{
    const useBotLayer=_prepareBotSpriteLayers(S);
    if(!useBotLayer){
      // Small/medium visible battles: direct blits avoid full-canvas composition overhead.
      for(const cid of _renderCivIds){const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(arr)for(const d of arr)d.draw(S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors,_foxTierRenderCache,true);}
    }else if(playerCivId>=0){
      if(_botLayerLow.width)_drawCachedBotLayer(_botLayerLow);
      for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
        if(cid>playerCivId)break;
        const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;
        if(cid===playerCivId)for(const d of arr)d.draw(S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors,_foxTierRenderCache,true);
        else _queueFoxOverlaysOnly(arr,S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors);
      }
      if(_botLayerHigh.width)_drawCachedBotLayer(_botLayerHigh);
      for(const cid of _renderCivIds){if(cid<=playerCivId)continue;_queueFoxOverlaysOnly(_renderByCiv[cid],S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors);}
    }else{
      if(_botLayerLow.width)_drawCachedBotLayer(_botLayerLow);
      for(const cid of _renderCivIds)_queueFoxOverlaysOnly(_renderByCiv[cid],S,badgeQueue,selRings,hpBars,marchDots,anchors);
    }
  }

  }finally{_clearFoxPassSprites();}

  if(selRings.length){
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';ctx.lineWidth=3.8;ctx.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<selRings.length;i+=4)ctx.ellipse(selRings[i],selRings[i+1],selRings[i+2],selRings[i+3],0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#fff8a0';ctx.lineWidth=2.2;ctx.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<selRings.length;i+=4)ctx.ellipse(selRings[i],selRings[i+1],selRings[i+2],selRings[i+3],0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
  }

  if(hpBars.length){
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
    for(let i=0;i<hpBars.length;i+=5){const bw=hpBars[i+2],bh=hpBars[i+3];ctx.fillRect(hpBars[i]-1,hpBars[i+1]-1,bw+2,bh+2);}
    ctx.fillStyle='#444';for(let i=0;i<hpBars.length;i+=5)ctx.fillRect(hpBars[i],hpBars[i+1],hpBars[i+2],hpBars[i+3]);
    // Three no-allocation color passes are cheaper than building bucket arrays every frame.
    ctx.fillStyle='#4cde50';for(let i=0;i<hpBars.length;i+=5)if(hpBars[i+4]>0.6)ctx.fillRect(hpBars[i],hpBars[i+1],hpBars[i+2]*hpBars[i+4],hpBars[i+3]);
    ctx.fillStyle='#e0c040';for(let i=0;i<hpBars.length;i+=5)if(hpBars[i+4]<=0.6&&hpBars[i+4]>0.3)ctx.fillRect(hpBars[i],hpBars[i+1],hpBars[i+2]*hpBars[i+4],hpBars[i+3]);
    ctx.fillStyle='#de3030';for(let i=0;i<hpBars.length;i+=5)if(hpBars[i+4]<=0.3)ctx.fillRect(hpBars[i],hpBars[i+1],hpBars[i+2]*hpBars[i+4],hpBars[i+3]);
  }

  if(marchDots.length){
    ctx.fillStyle='#80ff80';ctx.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<marchDots.length;i+=4){ctx.moveTo(marchDots[i]+marchDots[i+2],marchDots[i+1]);ctx.arc(marchDots[i],marchDots[i+1],marchDots[i+2],0,Math.PI*2);}ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='#004400';ctx.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<marchDots.length;i+=4){ctx.moveTo(marchDots[i]+marchDots[i+3],marchDots[i+1]);ctx.arc(marchDots[i],marchDots[i+1],marchDots[i+3],0,Math.PI*2);}ctx.fill();
  }

  if(anchors.length){const afs=Math.max(8,10*S);ctx.font=`${afs}px serif`;ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.globalAlpha=0.85;for(let i=0;i<anchors.length;i+=2)ctx.fillText('⚓',anchors[i],anchors[i+1]);ctx.globalAlpha=1;}

  if(badgeQueue.length){
    const fs=8*S;ctx.font=`bold ${fs}px Cinzel,serif`;ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    for(let i=0;i<badgeQueue.length;i+=5){
      const x=badgeQueue[i],y=badgeQueue[i+1],n=badgeQueue[i+2];
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,8*S,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(5,2,0,.90)';ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle=badgeQueue[i+3];ctx.lineWidth=0.9*S;ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle=badgeQueue[i+4];ctx.fillText(n>9?'9+':String(n),x,y+0.5*S);
    }
  }
  ctx.restore();

  if(_boxSelecting){
    const x=Math.min(_boxStart.x,_boxEnd.x),y=Math.min(_boxStart.y,_boxEnd.y),w=Math.abs(_boxEnd.x-_boxStart.x),h=Math.abs(_boxEnd.y-_boxStart.y);
    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,100,0.9)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.setLineDash([5,3]);ctx.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,240,100,0.07)';ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.restore();
  }
}

// drawCommittedRoutes is now folded into drawDivisions above
function drawCommittedRoutes(){ /* no-op — arrows drawn in drawDivisions */ }

// Draw drag-preview route while player is actively dragging a division
function drawRoutePreview(){
  if(!_divDragging||_divRoutePreview.length<2) return;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.setLineDash([5,5]);
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,100,0.85)';
  ctx.lineWidth=2.2;
  ctx.shadowColor='#ffe860';ctx.shadowBlur=5;
  ctx.beginPath();
  let first=true;
  for(const pid of _divRoutePreview){
    const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) continue;
    const sx=(p.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, sy=(p.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
    first?ctx.moveTo(sx,sy):ctx.lineTo(sx,sy);
    first=false;
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  const len=_divRoutePreview.length;
  const ep=provinces[_divRoutePreview[len-1]];
  const pp2=provinces[_divRoutePreview[Math.max(0,len-2)]];
  if(ep&&pp2){
    const ex=(ep.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, ey=(ep.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
    const px2=(pp2.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, py2=(pp2.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
    const ang=Math.atan2(ey-py2,ex-px2), ar=10;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(ex,ey);
    ctx.lineTo(ex-ar*Math.cos(ang-.42),ey-ar*Math.sin(ang-.42));
    ctx.lineTo(ex-ar*Math.cos(ang+.42),ey-ar*Math.sin(ang+.42));
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,240,100,0.95)';ctx.shadowColor='#ffe860';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// Draw defense line while player is right-dragging
function drawDefenseLine(){
  if(!_defLineDragging || _defLineProvs.length===0) return;
  ctx.save();
  // Filled circles on each defense province
  for(const pid of _defLineProvs){
    const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) continue;
    const sx=(p.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, sy=(p.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sx,sy,8,0,Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(80,160,255,0.45)';
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(120,200,255,0.9)';
    ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
    ctx.shadowColor='#60c0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=8;
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    // Shield icon
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    ctx.font='11px serif';
    ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.fillStyle='#a0d8ff';
    ctx.fillText('\uD83D\uDEE1',sx,sy);
  }
  // Connect them with a dashed blue line
  if(_defLineProvs.length>1){
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.setLineDash([4,4]);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(100,180,255,0.65)';
    ctx.lineWidth=1.8;
    ctx.shadowColor='#60c0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=5;
    let first=true;
    for(const pid of _defLineProvs){
      const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) continue;
      const sx=(p.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, sy=(p.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
      first?ctx.moveTo(sx,sy):ctx.lineTo(sx,sy);
      first=false;
    }
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  }
  // Label
  ctx.font='bold 11px sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='top';
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(160,220,255,0.9)';
  ctx.shadowColor='#000';ctx.shadowBlur=3;
  const selCount=playerDivs().filter(d=>d.selected).length;
  if(selCount>0){
    const fp=provinces[_defLineProvs[0]];
    if(fp){
      const sx=(fp.centerTx+.5-camX)*TS*zoom, sy=(fp.centerTy+.5-camY)*TS*zoom;
      ctx.fillText(`${selCount} div${selCount>1?'s':''} → defense`,sx,sy-22);
    }
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// drawDivHint removed — hint bubbles caused per-frame measureText lag


// ── PARTICLES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let particles=[];
function burst(wx,wy,color){for(let i=0;i<6;i++)particles.push({x:wx,y:wy,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*TS*.35,vy:-Math.random()*TS*.45-TS*.1,life:40,max:40,color});}
function tickParticles(){
  let w=0;
  for(let i=0;i<particles.length;i++){
    const p=particles[i];
    p.x+=p.vx; p.y+=p.vy; p.vy+=.15;
    if(--p.life>0) particles[w++]=p;
  }
  particles.length=w;
}
const _particleDrawBuckets=new Map();
function drawParticles(){
  if(!particles.length){ctx.globalAlpha=1;return;}
  // Persistent color×alpha buckets: same visual quantisation/order as before, without
  // allocating a Map/arrays and sorting particle objects every render frame.
  for(const bins of _particleDrawBuckets.values())for(let i=0;i<9;i++)bins[i].length=0;
  for(let i=0;i<particles.length;i++){
    const p=particles[i];let bins=_particleDrawBuckets.get(p.color);
    if(!bins){bins=Array.from({length:9},()=>[]);_particleDrawBuckets.set(p.color,bins);}
    bins[Math.max(0,Math.min(8,Math.round((p.life/p.max)*8)))].push(p);
  }
  for(const [color,bins] of _particleDrawBuckets){
    let used=false;for(let q=8;q>=0;q--)if(bins[q].length){used=true;break;}if(!used)continue;
    ctx.fillStyle=color;
    for(let q=8;q>=0;q--){const ps=bins[q];if(!ps.length)continue;ctx.globalAlpha=q/8;ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<ps.length;i++){const p=ps[i];ctx.moveTo(p.x+2.5,p.y);ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,2.5,0,Math.PI*2);}ctx.fill();}
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
}

// ── RENDERING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// OPTIMISATION: Offscreen tile-resolution texture (1px per tile).
// Only rebuilt when province ownership changes (dirty flag).
// Every frame: single GPU-accelerated ctx.drawImage instead of
// a per-pixel CPU fill loop over the full screen buffer.
let _worldTex=null,_worldTexCtx=null,_worldTexData=null,_worldTexU32=null;
const _worldPatchCache=new Map();let _worldPatchCacheBytes=0;const _WORLD_PATCH_CACHE_BUDGET=isMobile?(2*1024*1024):(8*1024*1024);
let _worldDirty=true;
let _worldForceFull=true;
const _worldDirtyRects=[];
// IMPORTANT: do not promote the full 1.7M-pixel map to ImageBitmap after every tiny
// ownership patch. Chromium can allocate/copy the entire graphics resource even when
// only a province changed. The canvas/OffscreenCanvas is already a native drawImage source.
let _worldTexBitmap=null,_worldBitmapGen=0,_worldBitmapIdlePending=false;

function _dropWorldBitmap(){
  _worldBitmapGen++;
  if(_worldTexBitmap?.close)_worldTexBitmap.close();
  _worldTexBitmap=null;
  _worldBitmapIdlePending=false;
}
function _markWorldFullDirty(){
  _worldDirty=true;_worldForceFull=true;_worldDirtyRects.length=0;_dropWorldBitmap();
}
function _markWorldRectDirty(x0,y0,x1,y1){
  _worldDirty=true;_dropWorldBitmap();
  if(_worldForceFull)return;
  x0=Math.max(0,x0|0);y0=Math.max(0,y0|0);x1=Math.min(WORLD_W-1,x1|0);y1=Math.min(WORLD_H-1,y1|0);
  if(x1<x0||y1<y0)return;
  // Ownership changes are usually adjacent. Merge touching/nearby dirty rectangles so
  // a multi-province peace/conquest batch becomes a few putImageData calls, not hundreds.
  for(let i=0;i<_worldDirtyRects.length;i++){
    const r=_worldDirtyRects[i];
    if(x1<r[0]-2||x0>r[2]+2||y1<r[1]-2||y0>r[3]+2)continue;
    r[0]=Math.min(r[0],x0);r[1]=Math.min(r[1],y0);r[2]=Math.max(r[2],x1);r[3]=Math.max(r[3],y1);
    // Re-merge any rectangles newly touched by the expansion.
    for(let j=_worldDirtyRects.length-1;j>i;j--){const q=_worldDirtyRects[j];if(r[2]<q[0]-2||r[0]>q[2]+2||r[3]<q[1]-2||r[1]>q[3]+2)continue;r[0]=Math.min(r[0],q[0]);r[1]=Math.min(r[1],q[1]);r[2]=Math.max(r[2],q[2]);r[3]=Math.max(r[3],q[3]);_worldDirtyRects.splice(j,1);}
    return;
  }
  _worldDirtyRects.push([x0,y0,x1,y1]);
  if(_worldDirtyRects.length>24){_worldForceFull=true;_worldDirtyRects.length=0;}
}
function _markWorldProvinceDirty(p){
  if(!p){_markWorldFullDirty();return;}
  _markWorldRectDirty(p.minX??p.centerTx,p.minY??p.centerTy,p.maxX??p.centerTx,p.maxY??p.centerTy);
}
function _ensureWorldTex(){
  if(_worldTex)return;
  _worldTex=(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')?new OffscreenCanvas(WORLD_W,WORLD_H):document.createElement('canvas');
  _worldTex.width=WORLD_W;_worldTex.height=WORLD_H;
  _worldTexCtx=_worldTex.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,willReadFrequently:false});
  // Reuse the existing ownership-colour buffer as ImageData backing storage where
  // supported. This removes a duplicate ~6.9 MB world buffer and the full u.set()
  // copy that used to precede every complete cache upload.
  try{
    const bytes=new Uint8ClampedArray(ownedColorBuf.buffer,ownedColorBuf.byteOffset,ownedColorBuf.byteLength);
    _worldTexData=new ImageData(bytes,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);_worldTexU32=ownedColorBuf;
  }catch(_e){
    _worldTexData=_worldTexCtx.createImageData(WORLD_W,WORLD_H);_worldTexU32=new Uint32Array(_worldTexData.data.buffer);
    _worldTexU32.set(ownedColorBuf);
  }
  _worldDirty=true;_worldForceFull=true;_worldDirtyRects.length=0;_worldTexBitmap=null;
}
// Compatibility stub. Direct canvas/OffscreenCanvas blits avoid full-world bitmap churn.
function _scheduleWorldBitmapPromotion(){ _worldBitmapIdlePending=false; }
function _rebuildWorldTex(){
  _ensureWorldTex();
  const u=_worldTexU32,oc=ownedColorBuf;
  if(_worldForceFull||!_worldDirtyRects.length){
    // Normally ImageData directly shares ownedColorBuf, so no 6.9 MB duplicate copy.
    // Keep the fallback correct for engines that rejected external ImageData storage.
    if(u!==oc)u.set(oc);
    _worldTexCtx.putImageData(_worldTexData,0,0);
  }else{    // Passing the 6.9 MB full-world ImageData to putImageData with a tiny dirty rect is
    // surprisingly expensive in Chromium. Copy only each dirty rectangle into a reusable
    // small ImageData and upload that exact patch. Typical conquest patches are ~35 pixels.
    for(const r of _worldDirtyRects){
      const x0=r[0],y0=r[1],w=r[2]-x0+1,h=r[3]-y0+1,key=w+'x'+h;
      let rec=_worldPatchCache.get(key);
      if(rec){_worldPatchCache.delete(key);_worldPatchCache.set(key,rec);}
      else{
        const data=_worldTexCtx.createImageData(w,h),bytes=w*h*4;rec={data,u:new Uint32Array(data.data.buffer),bytes};
        // Large one-off conquest/peace patches must not become permanent retained
        // ImageData. Cache only reusable shapes inside a strict byte budget.
        if(bytes<=(_WORLD_PATCH_CACHE_BUDGET>>1)){
          while(_worldPatchCache.size&&_worldPatchCacheBytes+bytes>_WORLD_PATCH_CACHE_BUDGET){const k0=_worldPatchCache.keys().next().value,r0=_worldPatchCache.get(k0);_worldPatchCache.delete(k0);_worldPatchCacheBytes-=r0?.bytes||0;}
          _worldPatchCache.set(key,rec);_worldPatchCacheBytes+=bytes;
        }
      }
      const pu=rec.u;for(let y=0;y<h;y++)pu.set(oc.subarray((y0+y)*WORLD_W+x0,(y0+y)*WORLD_W+x0+w),y*w);
      _worldTexCtx.putImageData(rec.data,x0,y0);
    }
  }
  _worldDirty=false;_worldForceFull=false;_worldDirtyRects.length=0;
  _scheduleWorldBitmapPromotion();
}
function drawTilesFast(){
  _ensureWorldTex();if(_worldDirty)_rebuildWorldTex();
  const tpx=TS*zoom,srcW=canvas.width/tpx,srcH=canvas.height/tpx;
  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  ctx.drawImage(_worldTex,camX,camY,srcW,srcH,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
}

// ── BORDER CACHE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// _provBorderSegs    — intra-country province edges (faint, zoom-in only)
// _countryBorderSegs — inter-country edges (bright, always visible)
let _provBorderSegs    = null;
let _countryBorderSegs = null;
let _provBorderPathChunks=null,_countryBorderPathChunks=null;

function buildBorderCache(){
  if(_provBorderSegs&&_countryBorderSegs)return; // normally fused into computeAdjacency()
  const pV=[],pH=[],cV=[],cH=[];
  for(let y=0;y<WORLD_H;y++)for(let x=0;x<WORLD_W;x++){
    const i=y*WORLD_W+x;if(tileKindBuf[i]!==1)continue;
    if(x<WORLD_W-1&&tileKindBuf[i+1]===1){if((provinces[tileProvMap[i+1]]?.countryId??-2)===(provinces[tileProvMap[i]]?.countryId??-3)){if(tileProvMap[i+1]!==tileProvMap[i])pV.push(x+1,y,x+1,y+1);}else cV.push(x+1,y,x+1,y+1);}
    if(y<WORLD_H-1&&tileKindBuf[i+WORLD_W]===1){if((provinces[tileProvMap[i+WORLD_W]]?.countryId??-2)===(provinces[tileProvMap[i]]?.countryId??-3)){if(tileProvMap[i+WORLD_W]!==tileProvMap[i])pH.push(x,y+1,x+1,y+1);}else cH.push(x,y+1,x+1,y+1);}
  }
  _provBorderSegs=new Float32Array(pV.concat(pH));_countryBorderSegs=new Float32Array(cV.concat(cH));_provBorderPathChunks=null;_countryBorderPathChunks=null;
}

// ── STATIC BORDER TEXTURE ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Province and sea-zone borders never change shape. Render them once per zoom
// into a world-space alpha canvas; camera panning then costs one cropped drawImage.
let _staticBorderTex=null,_staticBorderTexCtx=null;
let _staticBorderTexZoom=NaN,_staticBorderTexShowBorders=null,_staticBorderTexSeaVersion=-1;
let _staticBorderVersion=0;
function _invalidateStaticBorderTexture(){_staticBorderVersion++;_staticBorderTexZoom=NaN;}
function _ensureStaticBorderTexture(){
  if(_staticBorderTex)return;
  _staticBorderTex=document.createElement('canvas');_staticBorderTex.width=WORLD_W;_staticBorderTex.height=WORLD_H;
  _staticBorderTexCtx=_staticBorderTex.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
}
function _strokeTileSegments(target,segs){
  if(!segs||!segs.length)return;
  target.beginPath();
  for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=4){target.moveTo(segs[i],segs[i+1]);target.lineTo(segs[i+2],segs[i+3]);}
  target.stroke();
}

// Spatially chunk retained vector edges. Camera movement never rebuilds Path2D geometry;
// only chunks intersecting the viewport are submitted. Segment coordinates stay in exact
// tile-space, so zooming remains vector-crisp and line widths are converted back to pixels.
const _BORDER_CHUNK=160;
const _BORDER_CHUNK_COLS=Math.ceil(WORLD_W/_BORDER_CHUNK),_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS=Math.ceil(WORLD_H/_BORDER_CHUNK);
// Compile unseen Path2D border chunks only while the browser is idle. Without this,
// the first pan into each region pays the Path2D construction cost on the input frame.
const _borderWarmQueue=[];
const _borderWarmStaticQueued=new WeakMap();
const _borderWarmTerrQueued=new Uint8Array(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS*_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS);
let _borderWarmScheduled=false;
function _scheduleBorderWarmRunner(delay=0){
  if(_borderWarmScheduled||!_borderWarmQueue.length)return;_borderWarmScheduled=true;
  // A tiny deterministic timer slice warms the whole 12x6 border grid much sooner than
  // requestIdleCallback on browsers whose endless RAF loop leaves little formal idle time.
  // _runBorderWarmQueue immediately backs off whenever the user moves the camera.
  const start=()=>{_borderWarmScheduled=false;if(!_borderWarmQueue.length)return;setTimeout(()=>_runBorderWarmQueue({didTimeout:true,timeRemaining:()=>3}),0);};
  if(delay>0)setTimeout(start,delay);else start();
}
function _borderWarmKeyOrder(store){
  const out=[],tpx=Math.max(.001,TS*zoom),cx=Math.max(0,Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS-1,Math.floor((camX+canvas.width/(2*tpx))/_BORDER_CHUNK))),cy=Math.max(0,Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS-1,Math.floor((camY+canvas.height/(2*tpx))/_BORDER_CHUNK)));
  for(let key=0;key<store.buckets.length;key++)if(store.buckets[key]?.length)out.push(key);
  out.sort((a,b)=>{const ax=a%_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS,ay=(a/_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS)|0,bx=b%_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS,by=(b/_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS)|0;return Math.abs(ax-cx)+Math.abs(ay-cy)-Math.abs(bx-cx)-Math.abs(by-cy);});return out;
}
function _queueStaticBorderWarm(store){
  if(!store?.buckets)return;let flags=_borderWarmStaticQueued.get(store);if(!flags){flags=new Uint8Array(store.buckets.length);_borderWarmStaticQueued.set(store,flags);}
  for(const key of _borderWarmKeyOrder(store))if(!store.paths[key]&&!flags[key]){flags[key]=1;_borderWarmQueue.push({kind:0,store,key,flags});}
  _scheduleBorderWarmRunner(12);
}
function _queueTerritoryBorderWarm(key=-1){
  if(!_territoryEdgeChunkStore)return;
  if(key>=0){if(!_borderWarmTerrQueued[key]){_borderWarmTerrQueued[key]=1;_borderWarmQueue.push({kind:1,key});}}
  else for(const k of _borderWarmKeyOrder(_territoryEdgeChunkStore))if(!_tbChunkAllPaths.has(k)&&!_borderWarmTerrQueued[k]){_borderWarmTerrQueued[k]=1;_borderWarmQueue.push({kind:1,key:k});}
  _scheduleBorderWarmRunner(16);
}
function _runBorderWarmQueue(deadline){
  // Never compete with camera interaction. A few milliseconds of idle work is much
  // cheaper than a 10+ ms surprise on a drag/wheel frame.
  if(typeof _cameraMotionActive==='function'&&_cameraMotionActive()){_scheduleBorderWarmRunner(110);return;}
  const start=performance.now();let done=0;
  while(_borderWarmQueue.length){
    if(done>0&&((deadline?.timeRemaining?.()??0)<1.2||performance.now()-start>2.2))break;
    const job=_borderWarmQueue.shift();
    if(job.kind===0){job.flags[job.key]=0;if(job.store.paths[job.key])continue;_chunkPath(job.store,job.key);}
    else{_borderWarmTerrQueued[job.key]=0;if(!_tbChunkAllPaths.has(job.key)||!_tbChunkPlayerPaths.has(job.key))_territoryChunkPaths(job.key);}
    done++;
  }
  if(_borderWarmQueue.length)_scheduleBorderWarmRunner(6);
}

function _borderChunkKey(x1,y1,x2,y2){
  // Preserve the original midpoint chunk assignment exactly so anti-aliasing at chunk
  // boundaries remains pixel-identical to the pre-optimization vector renderer.
  const bx=Math.max(0,Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS-1,Math.floor(((x1+x2)*.5)/_BORDER_CHUNK)));
  const by=Math.max(0,Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS-1,Math.floor(((y1+y2)*.5)/_BORDER_CHUNK)));
  return by*_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS+bx;
}
function _pushBorderChunkOffset(raw,off,x1,y1,x2,y2){
  const key=_borderChunkKey(x1,y1,x2,y2);let a=raw[key];if(!a)raw[key]=a=[];a.push(off);
}
function _finalizeBorderChunkOffsets(segs,raw,stride=4){
  const buckets=new Array(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS*_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS);let size=0;
  for(let key=0;key<raw.length;key++){const a=raw[key];if(a?.length){buckets[key]=new Int32Array(a);size++;}}
  return {segs,stride,buckets,paths:new Array(buckets.length),size};
}
function _buildSegmentPathChunks(segs,stride=4){
  if(!segs||!segs.length)return null;
  // Fixed 12x6 spatial grid. Two typed-array passes are substantially cheaper and
  // lower-GC than creating/pushing hundreds of thousands of nested JS arrays.
  const nChunks=_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS*_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS,counts=new Uint32Array(nChunks);
  for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=stride){const o=(stride===6)?i+2:i;counts[_borderChunkKey(segs[o],segs[o+1],segs[o+2],segs[o+3])]++;}
  const buckets=new Array(nChunks);let size=0;
  for(let k=0;k<nChunks;k++)if(counts[k]){buckets[k]=new Int32Array(counts[k]);size++;}
  const pos=new Uint32Array(nChunks);
  for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=stride){const o=(stride===6)?i+2:i,key=_borderChunkKey(segs[o],segs[o+1],segs[o+2],segs[o+3]);buckets[key][pos[key]++]=i;}
  const store={segs,stride,buckets,paths:new Array(nChunks),size};
  // Political edges are compiled after ownership filtering, in _territoryChunkPaths.
  // Warming an additional unfiltered copy wasted path memory and startup CPU.
  if(stride!==6)_queueStaticBorderWarm(store);return store;
}
function _borderUnitCode(x1,y1,x2,y2){
  if(!Number.isInteger(x1)||!Number.isInteger(y1)||!Number.isInteger(x2)||!Number.isInteger(y2)||Math.min(x1,y1,x2,y2)<0||Math.max(x1,y1,x2,y2)>=2048)return -1;
  if(y1===y2&&Math.abs(x2-x1)===1)return y1*2048+Math.min(x1,x2);
  if(x1===x2&&Math.abs(y2-y1)===1)return 4194304+x1*2048+Math.min(y1,y2);
  return -1;
}
function _appendBorderRuns(path,codes){
  if(!codes.length)return;
  codes.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  let i=0;
  while(i<codes.length){
    const first=codes[i++],line=Math.floor(first/2048),start=first%2048;let end=start+1;
    while(i<codes.length&&Math.floor(codes[i]/2048)===line&&codes[i]%2048===end){end++;i++;}
    if(first<4194304){path.moveTo(start,line);path.lineTo(end,line);}
    else{const x=line-2048;path.moveTo(x,start);path.lineTo(x,end);}
  }
}
function _chunkPath(store,key){
  if(!store)return null;let path=store.paths[key];if(path)return path;
  const offs=store.buckets[key];if(!offs?.length)return null;
  const segs=store.segs,stride=store.stride||4,p=new Path2D();
  const codes=[];
  for(let j=0;j<offs.length;j++){
    const i=offs[j]+(stride===6?2:0),code=_borderUnitCode(segs[i],segs[i+1],segs[i+2],segs[i+3]);
    if(code>=0)codes.push(code);else{p.moveTo(segs[i],segs[i+1]);p.lineTo(segs[i+2],segs[i+3]);}
  }
  _appendBorderRuns(p,codes);store.paths[key]=p;return p;
}
function _strokeVisiblePathChunks(chunks,style,widthPx,alpha=1,marginChunks=1){
  if(!chunks||!chunks.size)return;
  const tpx=Math.max(0.001,TS*zoom),vw=canvas.width/tpx,vh=canvas.height/tpx;
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(camX/_BORDER_CHUNK)-marginChunks),by0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(camY/_BORDER_CHUNK)-marginChunks);
  const bx1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS-1,Math.floor((camX+vw)/_BORDER_CHUNK)+marginChunks),by1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS-1,Math.floor((camY+vh)/_BORDER_CHUNK)+marginChunks);
  ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(tpx,0,0,tpx,-camX*tpx,-camY*tpx);ctx.strokeStyle=style;ctx.lineWidth=widthPx/tpx;ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.lineCap='butt';ctx.lineJoin='miter';
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){const path=_chunkPath(chunks,by*_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS+bx);if(path)ctx.stroke(path);}
  ctx.restore();
}

function _rebuildStaticBorderTexture(){
  _ensureStaticBorderTexture();
  const c=_staticBorderTexCtx,tpx=Math.max(0.001,TS*zoom);
  c.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);c.clearRect(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);
  c.lineCap='butt';c.lineJoin='miter';
  if(showBorders&&zoom>0.30&&_provBorderSegs?.length){
    c.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.10)';c.lineWidth=0.5/tpx;_strokeTileSegments(c,_provBorderSegs);
  }
  if(zoom>=0.12&&_seaBorderSegs?.length){
    c.strokeStyle='rgba(80,160,220,0.35)';c.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,zoom*3.5)/tpx;_strokeTileSegments(c,_seaBorderSegs);
  }
  _staticBorderTexZoom=zoom;_staticBorderTexShowBorders=showBorders;_staticBorderTexSeaVersion=_staticBorderVersion;
}
function _blitWorldLayer(src){
  if(!src)return;
  const tpx=TS*zoom,sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height;
  ctx.drawImage(src,camX,camY,sw/tpx,sh/tpx,0,0,sw,sh);
}

// Kept as a public/legacy helper. Country borders are now represented by the live
// political territory border layer, exactly as draw() has always done.
function drawCountryBorders(){
  if(!_countryBorderSegs?.length)return;
  if(!_countryBorderPathChunks)_countryBorderPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_countryBorderSegs);
  _strokeVisiblePathChunks(_countryBorderPathChunks,'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)',Math.max(2.5,zoom*6));
  _strokeVisiblePathChunks(_countryBorderPathChunks,'rgba(0,0,0,0.92)',Math.max(1,zoom*2));
}

let _pbPath2D=null,_pbCamX=NaN,_pbCamY=NaN,_pbZoom=NaN,_pbPathW=0,_pbPathH=0;
function drawBorderLines(){
  if(!showBorders || zoom<=0.30 || !_provBorderSegs?.length) return;
  if(!_provBorderPathChunks)_provBorderPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_provBorderSegs);
  _strokeVisiblePathChunks(_provBorderPathChunks,'rgba(0,0,0,0.10)',0.5);
}

// ── SEA ZONE VISUAL OVERLAYS ──────────────────────────────────────
// Draws: (1) subtle dashed borders between sea zones and land/other-sea-zones
//         (2) zone name labels when zoomed in enough
//         (3) ⚓ anchor icon on zones with naval combat in progress
let _seaBorderSegs=null,_seaBorderPathChunks=null;
function _buildSeaBorderSegs(){
  if(_seaBorderSegs)return;
  // Compatibility fallback for a state created before computeAdjacency() built the
  // fused sea-edge cache. Normal full-world startup never enters this slow path.
  const v=[];
  for(let ty=0;ty<WORLD_H;ty++)for(let tx=0;tx<WORLD_W;tx++){
    const i=ty*WORLD_W+tx,pA=tileProvMap[i];
    if(pA<0||!provinces[pA]?.isSea)continue;
    if(tx<WORLD_W-1&&tileProvMap[i+1]!==pA)v.push(tx+1,ty,tx+1,ty+1);
    if(ty<WORLD_H-1&&tileProvMap[i+WORLD_W]!==pA)v.push(tx,ty+1,tx+1,ty+1);
  }
  _seaBorderSegs=new Float32Array(v);_seaBorderPathChunks=null;
  _invalidateStaticBorderTexture();
}

let _seaBorderPath2D=null,_seaBorderPathCamX=NaN,_seaBorderPathCamY=NaN,_seaBorderPathZoom=NaN,_seaBorderPathW=0,_seaBorderPathH=0;
const _seaShownNames=new Set();
function _seaProvinceHasBattle(pid){
  if(!gameReady) return false;
  if(_provDivIndexDirty) _rebuildProvDivIndex();
  const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);
  if(!byCiv || byCiv.size<2) return false;
  for(const [cid,arr] of byCiv){
    let active=false;
    for(const d of arr){ if(d.combatCooldown>0){active=true;break;} }
    if(!active) continue;
    for(const otherCid of byCiv.keys()){
      if(otherCid!==cid && areAtWar(cid,otherCid)) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

function drawSeaZones(){
  if(zoom<0.09)return;
  if(!_seaBorderSegs)_buildSeaBorderSegs();
  if(_seaBorderSegs.length>0&&zoom>=0.12){if(!_seaBorderPathChunks)_seaBorderPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_seaBorderSegs);_strokeVisiblePathChunks(_seaBorderPathChunks,'rgba(80,160,220,0.35)',Math.max(1.5,zoom*3.5));}
}
function drawSeaZoneLabels(){
  if(zoom<0.18||!showLabels)return;
  const tpx=TS*zoom,sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height,vx0=camX,vy0=camY,vx1=camX+sw/tpx,vy1=camY+sh/tpx;
  ctx.save();ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';_seaShownNames.clear();
  const seaCandidates=_collectGeometryProvinceCandidates(vx0,vx1,vy0,vy1);
  for(let ci=0;ci<seaCandidates.length;ci++){
    const pi=seaCandidates[ci],p=provinces[pi];if(!p||!p.isSea)continue;const cx=p.centerTx,cy=p.centerTy;if(cx<vx0||cx>vx1||cy<vy0||cy>vy1)continue;
    const nameKey=p.name+'_'+Math.round(cx/80)+'_'+Math.round(cy/80);if(_seaShownNames.has(nameKey))continue;_seaShownNames.add(nameKey);
    const scx=(cx-camX)*tpx,scy=(cy-camY)*tpx,fs=Math.max(7,Math.min(13,zoom*28));ctx.font=`italic ${fs}px 'Crimson Pro',serif`;const hasBattle=_seaProvinceHasBattle(pi);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';ctx.fillText(p.name,scx+1,scy+1);ctx.fillStyle=hasBattle?'#ff8844':'rgba(180,220,255,0.55)';ctx.fillText(p.name,scx,scy);
    if(hasBattle&&zoom>=0.20){const pulse=0.6+0.4*Math.sin(time*0.1);ctx.globalAlpha=pulse;ctx.font=`${fs*1.5}px serif`;ctx.fillText('⚓',scx,scy-fs*1.2);ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.font=`italic ${fs}px 'Crimson Pro',serif`;}
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// ── TERRITORY BORDER CACHE ────────────────────────────────────────
// Instead of per-civ colored segments, we build:
//   _terrBorderAll  — every edge between two DIFFERENT civs (or civ→unowned)
//   _terrBorderPlayer — edges belonging to the player's civ (for glow)
// Both stored as Float32Array [x1tile, y1tile, x2tile, y2tile, ...]
let _terrBorderAll    = null;
let _terrBorderPlayer = null;
let _terrBorderDirty  = true;
let _terrBorderLastBuild = -999;
// Dynamic political borders use the static edge graph grouped by spatial chunk.
// Ownership changes invalidate only affected chunk Path2Ds; no world-edge rebuild occurs
// during normal rendering or conquest.
let _territoryEdgeChunkStore=null;
const _tbChunkAllPaths=new Map(),_tbChunkPlayerPaths=new Map();
let _tbTargetedDirty=false,_tbPlayerPathCiv=-999;
function _markTerritoryProvinceBorderDirty(pid){
  _terrBorderDirty=true;_tbTargetedDirty=true;_tbSegsVersion++;
  const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||!_territoryEdgeChunkStore){_tbChunkAllPaths.clear();_tbChunkPlayerPaths.clear();return;}
  const x0=Math.max(0,(p.minX??p.centerTx)-1),x1=Math.min(WORLD_W-1,(p.maxX??p.centerTx)+1);
  const y0=Math.max(0,(p.minY??p.centerTy)-1),y1=Math.min(WORLD_H-1,(p.maxY??p.centerTy)+1);
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(x0/_BORDER_CHUNK)),bx1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS-1,Math.floor(x1/_BORDER_CHUNK));
  const by0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(y0/_BORDER_CHUNK)),by1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS-1,Math.floor(y1/_BORDER_CHUNK));
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){const k=by*_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS+bx;_tbChunkAllPaths.delete(k);_tbChunkPlayerPaths.delete(k);_queueTerritoryBorderWarm(k);}
}
function _consumeTerritoryBorderDirty(){
  if(!_terrBorderDirty)return;
  if(!_tbTargetedDirty){_tbChunkAllPaths.clear();_tbChunkPlayerPaths.clear();_tbSegsVersion++;}
  _tbTargetedDirty=false;_terrBorderDirty=false;_terrBorderLastBuild=time;
}
function _territoryChunkPaths(key){
  let all=_tbChunkAllPaths.get(key),pl=_tbChunkPlayerPaths.get(key);
  if(_tbChunkAllPaths.has(key)&&_tbChunkPlayerPaths.has(key))return {all,pl};
  const store=_territoryEdgeChunkStore;if(!store)return {all:null,pl:null};
  const offs=store.buckets[key];if(!offs?.length){_tbChunkAllPaths.set(key,null);_tbChunkPlayerPaths.set(key,null);return {all:null,pl:null};}
  const e=store.segs,pa=new Path2D(),pp=new Path2D(),ac=[],pc=[];let na=0,np=0;
  for(let j=0;j<offs.length;j++){
    const i=offs[j],pA=e[i],pB=e[i+1],cA=pA>=0?provOwner[pA]:-1,cB=pB>=0?provOwner[pB]:-1;
    if(cA===cB||(cA<0&&cB<0))continue;
    const x1=e[i+2],y1=e[i+3],x2=e[i+4],y2=e[i+5],code=_borderUnitCode(x1,y1,x2,y2);
    if(code>=0)ac.push(code);else{pa.moveTo(x1,y1);pa.lineTo(x2,y2);}na++;
    if(cA===playerCivId||cB===playerCivId){if(code>=0)pc.push(code);else{pp.moveTo(x1,y1);pp.lineTo(x2,y2);}np++;}
  }
  _appendBorderRuns(pa,ac);_appendBorderRuns(pp,pc);
  all=na?pa:null;pl=np?pp:null;_tbChunkAllPaths.set(key,all);_tbChunkPlayerPaths.set(key,pl);return {all,pl};
}
const _tbVisibleAllPaths=[],_tbVisiblePlayerPaths=[];
function _strokeVisibleTerritoryChunks(){
  if(!_territoryEdgeChunkStore&&_territoryEdgeData?.length){_territoryEdgeChunkStore=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_territoryEdgeData,6);_queueTerritoryBorderWarm();}
  if(!_territoryEdgeChunkStore)return false;
  _consumeTerritoryBorderDirty();
  if(_tbPlayerPathCiv!==playerCivId){_tbPlayerPathCiv=playerCivId;_tbChunkPlayerPaths.clear();}
  const tpx=Math.max(.001,TS*zoom),vw=canvas.width/tpx,vh=canvas.height/tpx;
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(camX/_BORDER_CHUNK)-1),by0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(camY/_BORDER_CHUNK)-1);
  const bx1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS-1,Math.floor((camX+vw)/_BORDER_CHUNK)+1),by1=Math.min(_BORDER_CHUNK_ROWS-1,Math.floor((camY+vh)/_BORDER_CHUNK)+1);
  const all=_tbVisibleAllPaths,pl=_tbVisiblePlayerPaths;all.length=0;pl.length=0;
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){
    const key=by*_BORDER_CHUNK_COLS+bx;if(!_territoryEdgeChunkStore.buckets[key]?.length)continue;
    if(!_tbChunkAllPaths.has(key)||!_tbChunkPlayerPaths.has(key))_territoryChunkPaths(key);
    const a=_tbChunkAllPaths.get(key),p=_tbChunkPlayerPaths.get(key);if(a)all.push(a);if(p)pl.push(p);
  }
  ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(tpx,0,0,tpx,-camX*tpx,-camY*tpx);ctx.lineCap='butt';ctx.lineJoin='miter';
  if(playerCivId>=0){const pc=civs[playerCivId]?.color;if(pc){ctx.strokeStyle=pc;ctx.globalAlpha=.30;ctx.lineWidth=5/tpx;for(const p of pl)ctx.stroke(p);}}
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.62)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5/tpx;for(const p of all)ctx.stroke(p);
  ctx.restore();return true;
}

function _rebuildTerrBorders(force=false){
  if(!_terrBorderDirty) return;
  if(!force && time - _terrBorderLastBuild < 20) return;
  _terrBorderDirty=false;
  _terrBorderLastBuild=time;

  const all=[],pl=[];
  const e=_territoryEdgeData;
  if(e&&e.length){
    // Edge graph is static; only owner IDs change. This is usually tens of times
    // smaller than WORLD_W*WORLD_H and has no per-tile land/sea branches.
    for(let i=0;i<e.length;i+=6){
      const pA=e[i],pB=e[i+1];
      const cA=pA>=0?provOwner[pA]:-1,cB=pB>=0?provOwner[pB]:-1;
      if(cA===cB||(cA<0&&cB<0))continue;
      const x1=e[i+2],y1=e[i+3],x2=e[i+4],y2=e[i+5];
      all.push(x1,y1,x2,y2);
      if(cA===playerCivId||cB===playerCivId)pl.push(x1,y1,x2,y2);
    }
  }else{
    // Old-state compatibility fallback. New maps always have _territoryEdgeData.
    for(let ty=0;ty<WORLD_H;ty++)for(let tx=0;tx<WORLD_W;tx++){
      const i=ty*WORLD_W+tx,cA=tileCivBuf[i];
      if(tileKindBuf[i]!==1)continue;
      if(tx<WORLD_W-1){const cR=tileCivBuf[i+1];if(cR!==cA&&(cA>=0||cR>=0)){all.push(tx+1,ty,tx+1,ty+1);if(cA===playerCivId||cR===playerCivId)pl.push(tx+1,ty,tx+1,ty+1);}}
