      if(ty<WORLD_H-1){const cB=tileCivBuf[i+WORLD_W];if(cB!==cA&&(cA>=0||cB>=0)){all.push(tx,ty+1,tx+1,ty+1);if(cA===playerCivId||cB===playerCivId)pl.push(tx,ty+1,tx+1,ty+1);}}
    }
  }
  _terrBorderAll=new Float32Array(all);
  _terrBorderPlayer=new Float32Array(pl);
  _tbSegsVersion++;
  _tbAllPath2D=null;_tbPlPath2D=null;_tbAllPathChunks=null;_tbPlPathChunks=null;
  _terrBorderTexVersion=-1;
}

// Territory border Path2D caches (rebuilt when segments or camera changes)
let _tbAllPath2D=null,   _tbAllCamX=-999, _tbAllCamY=-999, _tbAllZoom=-1;
let _tbPlPath2D=null,    _tbPlCamX=-999,  _tbPlCamY=-999,  _tbPlZoom=-1;
let _tbAllPathChunks=null,_tbPlPathChunks=null;
let _tbSegsVersion=0;    // incremented whenever _terrBorderAll/Player rebuilt

function _segsToPath2D(segs, tpx, sw, sh){
  const p=new Path2D();
  const m=3;
  for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=4){
    const sx1=(segs[i  ]-camX)*tpx, sy1=(segs[i+1]-camY)*tpx;
    const sx2=(segs[i+2]-camX)*tpx, sy2=(segs[i+3]-camY)*tpx;
    if(sx1<-m&&sx2<-m||sx1>sw+m&&sx2>sw+m) continue;
    if(sy1<-m&&sy2<-m||sy1>sh+m&&sy2>sh+m) continue;
    p.moveTo(sx1,sy1); p.lineTo(sx2,sy2);
  }
  return p;
}

let _terrBorderTex=null,_terrBorderTexCtx=null,_terrBorderTexZoom=NaN,_terrBorderTexPlayer=-999,_terrBorderTexVersion=-1;
function _ensureTerrBorderTexture(){
  if(_terrBorderTex)return;
  _terrBorderTex=document.createElement('canvas');_terrBorderTex.width=WORLD_W;_terrBorderTex.height=WORLD_H;
  _terrBorderTexCtx=_terrBorderTex.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
}
function _rebuildTerrBorderTexture(){
  _ensureTerrBorderTexture();
  const c=_terrBorderTexCtx,tpx=Math.max(0.001,TS*zoom);
  c.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);c.clearRect(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);c.lineCap='butt';c.lineJoin='miter';
  if(playerCivId>=0&&_terrBorderPlayer?.length){const pc=civs[playerCivId]?.color;if(pc){c.strokeStyle=pc;c.globalAlpha=0.30;c.lineWidth=5/tpx;_strokeTileSegments(c,_terrBorderPlayer);}}
  if(_terrBorderAll?.length){c.globalAlpha=1;c.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.62)';c.lineWidth=1.5/tpx;_strokeTileSegments(c,_terrBorderAll);}
  c.globalAlpha=1;
  _terrBorderTexZoom=zoom;_terrBorderTexPlayer=playerCivId;_terrBorderTexVersion=_tbSegsVersion;
}
function drawTerritoryBorders(){
  if(_territoryEdgeData?.length){_strokeVisibleTerritoryChunks();return;}
  // Compatibility fallback for legacy/external maps that predate the edge chunk store.
  _rebuildTerrBorders();
  if(!_terrBorderAll)return;
  if(!_tbAllPathChunks)_tbAllPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_terrBorderAll);
  if(playerCivId>=0&&_terrBorderPlayer?.length){
    if(!_tbPlPathChunks)_tbPlPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_terrBorderPlayer);
    const pc=civs[playerCivId]?.color;if(pc)_strokeVisiblePathChunks(_tbPlPathChunks,pc,5,.30);
  }
  _strokeVisiblePathChunks(_tbAllPathChunks,'rgba(0,0,0,0.62)',1.5);
}

// Per-province outline cache: pid → Float32Array [x1,y1,x2,y2,...] in tile-space
// Built lazily on first selection of that province. Provinces never change shape.
const _provOutlineCache = new Map();
const _PROV_OUTLINE_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?256:1024;

function _buildProvOutline(pid){
  const p = provinces[pid];
  const segs = [];
  for(const ti of p.tiles){
    const tx2 = ti % WORLD_W, ty2 = (ti / WORLD_W)|0;
    if(tileProvAt(tx2-1, ty2) !== pid){ segs.push(tx2,   ty2,   tx2,   ty2+1); }
    if(tileProvAt(tx2+1, ty2) !== pid){ segs.push(tx2+1, ty2,   tx2+1, ty2+1); }
    if(tileProvAt(tx2, ty2-1) !== pid){ segs.push(tx2,   ty2,   tx2+1, ty2); }
    if(tileProvAt(tx2, ty2+1) !== pid){ segs.push(tx2,   ty2+1, tx2+1, ty2+1); }
  }
  const fa = new Float32Array(segs);
  _provOutlineCache.set(pid, fa);
  while(_provOutlineCache.size>_PROV_OUTLINE_CACHE_MAX){const k=_provOutlineCache.keys().next().value;_provOutlineCache.delete(k);_provOutlinePathCache.delete(k);}
  return fa;
}

// ── COUNTRY SELECTION HIGHLIGHT ──────────────────────────────────
// Builds the OUTER border of an entire civ's territory (edges where the
// adjacent tile is not owned by the same civ). Cached per civId.
// Cache is invalidated by setting _civOutlineDirty.
const _civOutlineCache = new Map();
const _civOutlinePathCache = new Map();
const _provOutlinePathCache = new Map();
const _CIV_OUTLINE_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?16:96;
const _CIV_PATH_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?8:64;
const _PROV_PATH_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?64:512;
let _civOutlineDirty = false; // set true on any province ownership change

function _invalidateCivOutline(civId){
  _civOutlineCache.delete(civId);_civOutlinePathCache.delete(civId);
}
function _outlineWorldPath(cache,key,segs){let p=cache.get(key);if(p){cache.delete(key);cache.set(key,p);return p;}p=new Path2D();for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=4){p.moveTo(segs[i],segs[i+1]);p.lineTo(segs[i+2],segs[i+3]);}cache.set(key,p);const max=cache===_civOutlinePathCache?_CIV_PATH_CACHE_MAX:_PROV_PATH_CACHE_MAX;while(cache.size>max)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);return p;}

function _buildCivOutline(civId){
  const civ = civs[civId]; if(!civ) return new Float32Array(0);
  const segs = [];
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){
    // ownedProvs is normally authoritative, but verify against provOwner so even a
    // transient/stale membership can never make selection show historical land.
    if(provOwner?.[pid]!==civId) continue;
    const p = provinces[pid]; if(!p || p.isSea) continue;
    for(const ti of p.tiles){
      const tx2 = ti % WORLD_W, ty2 = (ti / WORLD_W)|0;
      // Emit edge if the neighbour tile belongs to a different civ (or is off-map)
      const leftCiv  = tx2>0        ? tileCivBuf[ti-1]       : -1;
      const rightCiv = tx2<WORLD_W-1? tileCivBuf[ti+1]       : -1;
      const topCiv   = ty2>0        ? tileCivBuf[ti-WORLD_W] : -1;
      const botCiv   = ty2<WORLD_H-1? tileCivBuf[ti+WORLD_W] : -1;
      if(leftCiv  !== civId) segs.push(tx2,   ty2,   tx2,   ty2+1);
      if(rightCiv !== civId) segs.push(tx2+1, ty2,   tx2+1, ty2+1);
      if(topCiv   !== civId) segs.push(tx2,   ty2,   tx2+1, ty2);
      if(botCiv   !== civId) segs.push(tx2,   ty2+1, tx2+1, ty2+1);
    }
  }
  const fa = new Float32Array(segs);
  _civOutlineCache.set(civId, fa);
  while(_civOutlineCache.size>_CIV_OUTLINE_CACHE_MAX){const k=_civOutlineCache.keys().next().value;_civOutlineCache.delete(k);_civOutlinePathCache.delete(k);}
  return fa;
}

// Country selection itself is border-only. The offscreen highlight cache below
// remains only for neutral/single-province selection, where a fill is still useful.
// ── SELECTED HIGHLIGHT CACHE ──────────────────────────────────────
let _hlCanvas=null, _hlCtx=null, _hlCivId=-999, _hlDirty=true;
let _hlBitmap=null; // GPU-resident ImageBitmap of _hlCanvas
let _hlPath2D=null, _hlPathCivId=-999, _hlPathCamX=-999, _hlPathCamY=-999, _hlPathZoom=-1;

function _ensureHlCanvas(){
  if(!_hlCanvas){
    _hlCanvas=(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')?new OffscreenCanvas(WORLD_W,WORLD_H):document.createElement('canvas');
    _hlCanvas.width=WORLD_W; _hlCanvas.height=WORLD_H;
    _hlCtx=_hlCanvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
  }
}

function _rebuildHlCanvas(civId){
  _ensureHlCanvas();
  _hlCtx.clearRect(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);
  if(civId>=0 && civs[civId]){
    _hlCtx.fillStyle='rgba(255,238,0,0.22)';
    for(const pid of civs[civId].ownedProvs){
      const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) continue;
      for(const ti of p.tiles){
        const tx2=ti%WORLD_W, ty2=(ti/WORLD_W)|0;
        _hlCtx.fillRect(tx2,ty2,1,1);
      }
    }
  } else if(civId===-2){
    // Single province highlight (selectedProv)
    const p=provinces[selectedProv]; if(!p) return;
    _hlCtx.fillStyle='rgba(255,238,0,0.20)';
    for(const ti of p.tiles){
      _hlCtx.fillRect(ti%WORLD_W,(ti/WORLD_W)|0,1,1);
    }
  }
  _hlCivId=civId; _hlDirty=false;
  _hlPath2D=null; // invalidate outline path too
  // A world-sized ImageBitmap allocation here caused large selection spikes and could
  // retain old GPU resources. OffscreenCanvas is already a native drawImage source.
  if(_hlBitmap?.close)_hlBitmap.close();_hlBitmap=null;
}

function drawSelectedHighlight(){
  const tpx = TS*zoom;
  const sw = canvas.width, sh = canvas.height;
  ctx.save();

  if(panelCivId>=0 && civs[panelCivId]){
    // Country selection is BORDER-ONLY. Do not tint/fill individual provinces:
    // that looked like historical/original provinces were also selected and made
    // ownership changes visually messy. The outline below is derived exclusively
    // from the country's CURRENT owned provinces.
    let segs = _civOutlineCache.get(panelCivId);
    if(!segs) segs = _buildCivOutline(panelCivId);

    // Cache the border in WORLD coordinates once. Camera movement is now a matrix change,
    // not an O(country-border-segments) Path2D rebuild on every pan/zoom frame.
    const worldPath=_outlineWorldPath(_civOutlinePathCache,panelCivId,segs);
    ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(tpx,0,0,tpx,-camX*tpx,-camY*tpx);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,80,0.45)';ctx.lineWidth=4/tpx;ctx.stroke(worldPath);
    ctx.strokeStyle='#fff8a0';ctx.lineWidth=1.5/tpx;ctx.stroke(worldPath);ctx.restore();

  } else if(panelCivId===-2 && selectedProv>=0){
    // Single province tint + outline
    if(_hlDirty || _hlCivId!==-2) _rebuildHlCanvas(-2);
    const srcX=camX, srcY=camY, srcW=sw/tpx, srcH=sh/tpx;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    ctx.drawImage(_hlCanvas, srcX,srcY,srcW,srcH, 0,0,sw,sh);

    let segs = _provOutlineCache.get(selectedProv);
    if(!segs) segs = _buildProvOutline(selectedProv);
    const pp2=_outlineWorldPath(_provOutlinePathCache,selectedProv,segs);
    ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(tpx,0,0,tpx,-camX*tpx,-camY*tpx);ctx.strokeStyle='#fff8a0';ctx.lineWidth=2/tpx;ctx.stroke(pp2);ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawBldIcons(){ /* Building icons removed from map for performance */ }

// ── HOI4-STYLE DYNAMIC COUNTRY LABELS ─────────────────────────────
let _civLabelCache = null;
let _civLabelDirty = true;
let _civLabelLastBuild = -999; // frame counter of last rebuild

// Label image cache: maps `${civId}_${fsBucket}` → {oc, halfW, halfH}
// Cleared whenever _civLabelCache is rebuilt (country shape changed).
const _labelImgCache = new Map();
const _LABEL_IMG_CACHE_MAX=640;
let _labelImgZoom = -1; // retained for save/reset compatibility; cache is now multi-scale
function _clearLabelImgCache(){
  if(_BMP_SUPPORTED)for(const e of _labelImgCache.values())if(e?.oc instanceof ImageBitmap)e.oc.close?.();
  _labelImgCache.clear();
}
function _trimLabelImgCache(){
  while(_labelImgCache.size>_LABEL_IMG_CACHE_MAX){const k=_labelImgCache.keys().next().value,e=_labelImgCache.get(k);if(_BMP_SUPPORTED&&e?.oc instanceof ImageBitmap)e.oc.close?.();_labelImgCache.delete(k);}
}
// Shared measurement context — reused across all label builds (no DOM alloc per label)
const _lblMeasureCtx = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
let _civLabelCacheVersion=0;
function _provinceLabelStats(p){
  if(!p)return [0,0,0,0,0,0];
  if(p._labelStats)return p._labelStats;
  let sx=0,sy=0,n=0,sxx=0,syy=0,sxy=0;
  if(!p.isSea)for(const ti of p.tiles){
    const tx2=ti%WORLD_W,ty2=(ti/WORLD_W)|0;
    if(tileKindBuf[ty2*WORLD_W+tx2]!==1)continue;
    sx+=tx2;sy+=ty2;n++;sxx+=tx2*tx2;syy+=ty2*ty2;sxy+=tx2*ty2;
  }
  return p._labelStats=[n,sx,sy,sxx,syy,sxy];
}

function _buildCivLabelCache(){
  _civLabelDirty = false;
  _clearLabelImgCache();
  _civLabelCacheVersion++;
  if(!tileCivBuf || !civs.length){ _civLabelCache=[]; return; }

  _civLabelCache = [];

  for(let cid = 0; cid < civs.length; cid++){
    const civ = civs[cid];
    if(!civ || !civ.ownedProvs.size) continue;

    // ── Find connected province components via adjacency graph ──────
    const visited = new Set();
    const components = [];
    for(const startPid of civ.ownedProvs){
      if(visited.has(startPid)) continue;
      const comp = [];
      const q = [startPid];
      visited.add(startPid);
      let qi = 0;
      while(qi < q.length){
        const pid = q[qi++];
        comp.push(pid);
        const adj = provAdj.get(pid);
        if(adj) for(const n of adj){
          if(!visited.has(n) && civ.ownedProvs.has(n)){
            visited.add(n); q.push(n);
          }
        }
      }
      components.push(comp);
    }

    // ── Compute tile stats per component ────────────────────────────
    for(const comp of components){
      let sx=0,sy=0,n=0,sxx=0,syy=0,sxy=0;
      for(const pid of comp){
        const st=_provinceLabelStats(provinces[pid]);
        n+=st[0];sx+=st[1];sy+=st[2];sxx+=st[3];syy+=st[4];sxy+=st[5];
      }
      // Minimum tile threshold — skip tiny islands/exclaves
      if(n < 100) continue;

      const cx=sx/n, cy=sy/n;
      const mxx=sxx/n-cx*cx;
      const myy=syy/n-cy*cy;
      const mxy=sxy/n-cx*cy;

      let angle=0.5*Math.atan2(2*mxy, mxx-myy);
      if(angle > Math.PI/2)  angle -= Math.PI;
      if(angle < -Math.PI/2) angle += Math.PI;
      angle=Math.max(-0.87,Math.min(0.87,angle));

      const tr=mxx+myy;
      const disc=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(mxx-myy)*(mxx-myy)*0.25+mxy*mxy));
      const lam1=tr*0.5+disc;
      const spread=Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,lam1));

      _civLabelCache.push({cid, cx, cy, angle, spread, n, name: civ.name.toUpperCase()});
    }
  }
}

// Pre-render a single label to an offscreen canvas, return {oc, halfW, halfH}.
// Key: font-size bucket so we only re-render on significant zoom change.
function _getOrBuildLabelImg(lbl, fs, spreadW){
  const fsBucket = Math.round(fs * 2); // re-render when fs changes by ≥ 0.5px
  const spreadBucket=Math.round(spreadW/12);
  const key = lbl.cid + '_' + fsBucket + '_' + spreadBucket + '_' + lbl.name;
  let entry = _labelImgCache.get(key);
  if(entry){_labelImgCache.delete(key);_labelImgCache.set(key,entry);return entry;}

  const REF_FS = 12.8;
  // Ensure refCWs is computed
  if(!lbl.refCWs){
    // Reuse module-level measurement canvas (no DOM alloc per call)
    _lblMeasureCtx.font = `700 ${REF_FS}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
    lbl.chars   = [...lbl.name];
    lbl.refCWs  = lbl.chars.map(c => _lblMeasureCtx.measureText(c).width);
    lbl.refTotal = lbl.refCWs.reduce((s,w)=>s+w, 0);
  }
  const chars = lbl.chars, nc = chars.length;
  if(!nc || lbl.refTotal <= 0) return null;

  const scale   = fs / REF_FS;
  const cws     = lbl.refCWs.map(w => w * scale);
  const totalCW = lbl.refTotal * scale;
  const gap     = nc > 1 ? (spreadW - totalCW) / (nc - 1) : 0;
  const curveAmp = Math.min(spreadW * 0.04, fs * 0.85);

  const PAD = Math.ceil(fs * 1.0); // generous padding: stroke (fs*0.32) + descenders + safety
  const canW = Math.ceil(spreadW) + PAD * 2 + Math.ceil(fs * 0.5); // extra right margin for last char stroke
  const canH = Math.ceil(fs * 2.0) + PAD * 2; // taller to avoid vertical clip

  const oc = document.createElement('canvas');
  oc.width = canW; oc.height = canH;
  const c2 = oc.getContext('2d', {alpha: true, willReadFrequently: false});

  c2.textBaseline = 'middle';
  c2.textAlign    = 'left';
  c2.font = `700 ${fs}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
  c2.lineWidth   = fs * 0.32;
  c2.lineJoin    = 'round';

  const originX = PAD + spreadW * 0.5; // center of canvas = center of label
  const originY = canH * 0.5;

  let x = -spreadW * 0.5;
  for(let i = 0; i < nc; i++){
    const cx2 = x + cws[i] * 0.5;
    const t   = spreadW > 0 ? cx2 / (spreadW * 0.5) : 0;
    const cy2 = -curveAmp * (1 - t * t);
    const drawX = originX + cx2 - cws[i] * 0.5;
    const drawY = originY + cy2;

    c2.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.40)';
    c2.strokeText(chars[i], drawX, drawY);
    c2.fillStyle   = 'rgba(255,248,215,0.28)';
    c2.fillText(chars[i], drawX, drawY);

    x += cws[i] + gap;
  }

  entry = {oc, halfW: canW * 0.5, halfH: canH * 0.5};
  _labelImgCache.set(key, entry);_trimLabelImgCache();
  // Keep the small canvas directly. Promoting every zoom variant to ImageBitmap creates
  // extra graphics allocations and async upload work with little benefit here.
  return entry;
}

function drawLabels(){
  if(!showLabels || zoom < 0.10) return;
  if((_civLabelDirty && time - _civLabelLastBuild >= 90) || !_civLabelCache){
    _buildCivLabelCache();
    _civLabelLastBuild = time;
  }
  // Keep recent quantized zoom variants in a bounded LRU. Clearing every >4% zoom
  // delta caused severe wheel/pinch allocation churn and repeated ImageBitmap promotion.
  _labelImgZoom=zoom;
  if(!_civLabelCache) return;

  const tpx = TS * zoom;
  const sw = canvas.width, sh = canvas.height;
  const REF_FS = 12.8;

  for(const lbl of _civLabelCache){
    // In alliance view: hide nation labels for civs that belong to an alliance
    if(viewMode === 'alliances' && getAllianceOfCiv(lbl.cid)) continue;

    const scx = (lbl.cx - camX) * tpx;
    const scy = (lbl.cy - camY) * tpx;
    // Tighter culling: skip labels whose center is well off screen
    if(scx < -400 || scx > sw+400 || scy < -80 || scy > sh+80) continue;

    // Compute font size (needed to look up/build the cached canvas)
    if(!lbl.refCWs){
      _lblMeasureCtx.font = `700 ${REF_FS}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
      lbl.chars    = [...lbl.name];
      lbl.refCWs   = lbl.chars.map(c => _lblMeasureCtx.measureText(c).width);
      lbl.refTotal = lbl.refCWs.reduce((s,w)=>s+w, 0);
    }
    if(!lbl.refTotal) continue;

    const targetW = lbl.spread * tpx * 2.5;
    const desiredLetterW = targetW * 0.65;
    let fs = REF_FS * desiredLetterW / lbl.refTotal;
    fs = Math.max(5.6, Math.min(38.4, fs));
    if(fs < 5.6) continue;

    const scale   = fs / REF_FS;
    const totalCW = lbl.refTotal * scale;
    const nc      = lbl.chars.length;
    // Ensure spreadW is always >= actual text width + a safety margin
    const spreadW = Math.max(totalCW * 1.15, Math.min(targetW, totalCW / 0.65));

    const img = _getOrBuildLabelImg(lbl, fs, spreadW);
    if(!img) continue;

    // Use setTransform instead of save/translate/rotate/restore (avoids stack ops)
    const ca=Math.cos(lbl.angle), sa=Math.sin(lbl.angle);
    const hw=img.halfW, hh=img.halfH;
    ctx.setTransform(ca,sa,-sa,ca, scx-hw*ca+hh*sa, scy-hw*sa-hh*ca);
    ctx.drawImage(img.oc, 0, 0);
  }
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0); // reset transform once after all labels
}

// Pre-project city positions once after map is ready. Each city also stores the
// fixed province it sits in, so ownership/name lookup is O(1) while rendering.
let _cityPx = null; // Array of {tx,ty,name,cap,pid}
let _cityDisplayOwnerCache=null,_cityDisplayOriginalCache=null,_cityDisplayBaseCache=null,_cityDisplayLangCache=null,_cityDisplayResolvedCache=null;
const _CITY_BUCKET=64;
let _cityBuckets=null,_cityBucketCols=0,_cityBucketRows=0;
const _visibleCityIds=[];
function buildCityCache(){
  _cityPx = [];_citiesByPid=new Map();
  _cityBucketCols=Math.ceil(WORLD_W/_CITY_BUCKET);_cityBucketRows=Math.ceil(WORLD_H/_CITY_BUCKET);
  _cityBuckets=Array.from({length:_cityBucketCols*_cityBucketRows},()=>[]);
  for(let srcIndex=0;srcIndex<CITIES.length;srcIndex++){
    const [lon,lat,name,cap]=CITIES[srcIndex],pt=proj([lon,lat]);
    if(!pt) continue;
    const ci=_cityPx.length,c={tx:pt[0],ty:pt[1],name,cap,baseCap:!!cap,srcIndex,pid:_findCityProvince(pt[0],pt[1])};_cityPx.push(c);
    if(c.pid>=0){let a=_citiesByPid.get(c.pid);if(!a)_citiesByPid.set(c.pid,a=[]);a.push(ci);}
    const bx=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketCols-1,(c.tx/_CITY_BUCKET)|0)),by=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketRows-1,(c.ty/_CITY_BUCKET)|0));
    _cityBuckets[by*_cityBucketCols+bx].push(ci);
  }
  _cityDisplayOwnerCache=new Int32Array(_cityPx.length);_cityDisplayOwnerCache.fill(-2147483648);
  _cityDisplayOriginalCache=new Int32Array(_cityPx.length);_cityDisplayOriginalCache.fill(-2147483648);
  _cityDisplayBaseCache=new Array(_cityPx.length);_cityDisplayLangCache=new Array(_cityPx.length);_cityDisplayResolvedCache=new Array(_cityPx.length);
  // Initial fallback baseline. Fresh game/editor starts recapture this after their
  // final scenario ownership is applied; save-load restores its serialized baseline.
  _restoreCityOriginalOwners(null);_markCapitalsDirty();
}
function _collectVisibleCityIds(l,r,t,b){
  _visibleCityIds.length=0;if(!_cityBuckets)return _visibleCityIds;
  const bx0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketCols-1,Math.floor(l/_CITY_BUCKET))),bx1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketCols-1,Math.floor(r/_CITY_BUCKET)));
  const by0=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketRows-1,Math.floor(t/_CITY_BUCKET))),by1=Math.max(0,Math.min(_cityBucketRows-1,Math.floor(b/_CITY_BUCKET)));
  for(let by=by0;by<=by1;by++)for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++){const a=_cityBuckets[by*_cityBucketCols+bx];for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)_visibleCityIds.push(a[i]);}
  return _visibleCityIds;
}

function drawCities(){
  if(!_cityPx || !showCities) return;
  const tpx = TS * zoom;
  const SHOW_DOT_ZOOM  = 0.10; // dots visible from very zoomed out
  const SHOW_NAME_ZOOM = 0.55; // names appear when zoomed in enough
  if(zoom < SHOW_DOT_ZOOM) return;

  ctx.save();
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  const pad=20/Math.max(0.001,tpx),cityIds=_collectVisibleCityIds(camX-pad,camX+canvas.width/tpx+pad,camY-pad,camY+canvas.height/tpx+pad);

  for(let vi=0;vi<cityIds.length;vi++){
    const cityIndex=cityIds[vi],c=_cityPx[cityIndex];
    const isCapital=_cityIsCapital(cityIndex);
    // Screen position
    const sx = (c.tx - camX) * tpx;
    const sy = (c.ty - camY) * tpx;
    if(sx < -20 || sx > canvas.width+20 || sy < -20 || sy > canvas.height+20) continue;

    if(isCapital){
      // ── Dynamic capital: gold star ──
      const r = Math.max(2.5, Math.min(6, zoom * 14));
      drawStar(sx, sy, r);
    } else {
      // ── City: white circle with dark border ──
      const r = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(4, zoom * 9));
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, r + 0.8, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#f5f0e0';
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Name label at higher zoom
    if(zoom >= SHOW_NAME_ZOOM){
      const fs = Math.max(9, Math.min(13, zoom * 22));
      ctx.font = `${isCapital?'700':'500'} ${fs}px 'Crimson Pro', serif`;
      const off = (isCapital ? Math.max(3,zoom*8)+2 : Math.max(2,zoom*6)+2);
      // Every occupation label is romanized before rendering, including Arabic/Russian,
      // so the canvas direction must stay LTR. Treating romanized Arabic as RTL reversed
      // its alignment on some browsers. Capital labels are deliberately uppercase.
      ctx.direction='ltr';
      ctx.textAlign='left';
      const tx2 = sx + off, ty2 = sy;
      const resolvedName=_cityDisplayName(c,cityIndex);
      const displayName=isCapital?String(resolvedName).toLocaleUpperCase('en-US'):resolvedName;
      // Shadow
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.7)';
      ctx.fillText(displayName, tx2+1, ty2+1);
      // Label — capitals gold, cities cream
      ctx.fillStyle = isCapital ? '#ffe080' : '#f0e8cc';
      ctx.fillText(displayName, tx2, ty2);
    }
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawStar(cx, cy, r){
  const spikes = 5, inner = r * 0.42;
  // Shadow ring
  ctx.beginPath();
  for(let i=0;i<spikes*2;i++){
    const ang = (i*Math.PI/spikes) - Math.PI/2;
    const rad = i%2===0 ? r+1.2 : inner+0.6;
    i===0 ? ctx.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad, cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad)
           : ctx.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad, cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad);
  }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
  ctx.fill();
  // Gold star
  ctx.beginPath();
  for(let i=0;i<spikes*2;i++){
    const ang = (i*Math.PI/spikes) - Math.PI/2;
    const rad = i%2===0 ? r : inner;
    i===0 ? ctx.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad, cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad)
           : ctx.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad, cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad);
  }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd54f';
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#b8860b';
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;
  ctx.stroke();
}

// ── ALLIANCE MAP OVERLAY ──────────────────────────────────────────

// Build per-alliance label data using same PCA spread as civ labels
function _buildAllianceLabelCache(){
  _allianceLabelDirty=false;
  _allianceLabelImgCache.clear();
  _allianceLabelCache=[];
  if(!tileCivBuf||!civs.length)return;

  for(const al of alliances){
    // Build the alliance's LAND province set, then split it into connected components.
    // This prevents one alliance name from being stretched letter-by-letter across oceans,
    // disconnected member countries, islands, or separate fronts.
    const landProvs=new Set();
    for(const civId of al.memberIds){
      if(civId<0||civId>=civs.length)continue;
      for(const pid of civs[civId].ownedProvs){const p=provinces[pid];if(p&&!p.isSea)landProvs.add(pid);}
    }
    if(!landProvs.size)continue;

    const visited=new Set();let compIdx=0;
    for(const startPid of landProvs){
      if(visited.has(startPid))continue;
      const comp=[],q=[startPid];visited.add(startPid);
      for(let qi=0;qi<q.length;qi++){
        const pid=q[qi];comp.push(pid);
        const adj=provAdj.get(pid);if(!adj)continue;
        for(const n of adj){if(!visited.has(n)&&landProvs.has(n)&&!provinces[n]?.isSea){visited.add(n);q.push(n);}}
      }

      let sx=0,sy=0,n=0,sxx=0,syy=0,sxy=0;
      for(const pid of comp){const st=_provinceLabelStats(provinces[pid]);n+=st[0];sx+=st[1];sy+=st[2];sxx+=st[3];syy+=st[4];sxy+=st[5];}
      // Same threshold as country labels: tiny islands/exclaves do not get giant text.
      if(n<100){compIdx++;continue;}
      const cx=sx/n,cy=sy/n,mxx=sxx/n-cx*cx,myy=syy/n-cy*cy,mxy=sxy/n-cx*cy;
      let angle=0.5*Math.atan2(2*mxy,mxx-myy);
      if(angle>Math.PI/2)angle-=Math.PI;if(angle< -Math.PI/2)angle+=Math.PI;
      angle=Math.max(-0.87,Math.min(0.87,angle));
      const tr=mxx+myy,disc=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,(mxx-myy)*(mxx-myy)*0.25+mxy*mxy));
      const spread=Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,tr*0.5+disc));
      _allianceLabelCache.push({alId:al.id,compIdx,cx,cy,angle,spread,n,name:al.name.toUpperCase(),color:al.color});
      compIdx++;
    }
  }
}

// ── ALLIANCE BORDER REBUILD ───────────────────────────────────────
// Walks the current ownership buffer (tileCivBuf) and emits a segment
// wherever two adjacent land tiles belong to different alliance groups.
// "group" = allianceId of the owning civ (or -1 = no alliance / water).
// Uses the same Float32Array format as _terrBorderAll.
function _rebuildAllianceBorders(){
  _allianceBorderDirty=false;
  const n=civs.length,civAlId=new Int32Array(n);civAlId.fill(-1);
  for(const al of alliances)for(const civId of al.memberIds)if(civId>=0&&civId<n)civAlId[civId]=al.id;
  const segs=[],e=_territoryEdgeData;
  if(e&&e.length){
    for(let i=0;i<e.length;i+=6){
      const pA=e[i],pB=e[i+1];
      // Alliance borders are land↔land only, matching the old full-tile scan.
      if(pA<0||pB<0||provinces[pA]?.isSea||provinces[pB]?.isSea)continue;
      const cA=provOwner[pA],cB=provOwner[pB];
      const alA=(cA>=0&&cA<n)?civAlId[cA]:-1,alB=(cB>=0&&cB<n)?civAlId[cB]:-1;
      if(alA!==alB)segs.push(e[i+2],e[i+3],e[i+4],e[i+5]);
    }
  }else{
    for(let ty=0;ty<WORLD_H;ty++)for(let tx=0;tx<WORLD_W;tx++){
      const i=ty*WORLD_W+tx;if(tileKindBuf[i]!==1)continue;
      const cA=tileCivBuf[i],alA=(cA>=0&&cA<n)?civAlId[cA]:-1;
      if(tx<WORLD_W-1&&tileKindBuf[i+1]===1){const cR=tileCivBuf[i+1],alR=(cR>=0&&cR<n)?civAlId[cR]:-1;if(alA!==alR)segs.push(tx+1,ty,tx+1,ty+1);}
      if(ty<WORLD_H-1&&tileKindBuf[i+WORLD_W]===1){const cB=tileCivBuf[i+WORLD_W],alB=(cB>=0&&cB<n)?civAlId[cB]:-1;if(alA!==alB)segs.push(tx,ty+1,tx+1,ty+1);}
    }
  }
  _allianceBorderSegs=new Float32Array(segs);_allianceBorderSegVersion++;_alBdrPath2D=null;_allianceBorderPathChunks=null;_allianceBorderTexVersion=-1;
}

// Build or retrieve offscreen label canvas for an alliance
function _getOrBuildAllianceLabelImg(lbl, fs, spreadW){
  const fsBucket = Math.round(fs*2);
  // Include name in key so renaming an alliance busts the cached canvas
  const key='al_'+lbl.alId+'_'+(lbl.compIdx||0)+'_'+fsBucket+'_'+Math.round(spreadW/8)+'_'+lbl.name;
  let entry = _allianceLabelImgCache.get(key);
  if(entry){_allianceLabelImgCache.delete(key);_allianceLabelImgCache.set(key,entry);return entry;}

  const REF_FS = 12.8;
  if(!lbl.refCWs){    const mc = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
    mc.font = `700 ${REF_FS}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
    lbl.chars   = [...lbl.name];
    lbl.refCWs  = lbl.chars.map(c => mc.measureText(c).width);
    lbl.refTotal = lbl.refCWs.reduce((s,w)=>s+w, 0);
  }
  const chars=lbl.chars, nc=chars.length;
  if(!nc || lbl.refTotal<=0) return null;

  const scale   = fs/REF_FS;
  const cws     = lbl.refCWs.map(w=>w*scale);
  const totalCW = lbl.refTotal*scale;
  const gap     = nc>1 ? (spreadW-totalCW)/(nc-1) : 0;
  const curveAmp = Math.min(spreadW*0.04, fs*0.85);
  const PAD = Math.ceil(fs*1.0);
  const canW = Math.ceil(spreadW)+PAD*2+Math.ceil(fs*0.5);
  const canH = Math.ceil(fs*2.0)+PAD*2;

  const oc = document.createElement('canvas');
  oc.width=canW; oc.height=canH;
  const c2 = oc.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});

  c2.textBaseline='middle'; c2.textAlign='left';
  c2.font=`700 ${fs}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
  c2.lineWidth=fs*0.32; c2.lineJoin='round';

  const originX=PAD+spreadW*0.5;
  const originY=canH*0.5;
  let x=-spreadW*0.5;
  for(let i=0;i<nc;i++){
    const cx2=x+cws[i]*0.5;
    const t=spreadW>0?cx2/(spreadW*0.5):0;
    const cy2=-curveAmp*(1-t*t);
    const drawX=originX+cx2-cws[i]*0.5;
    const drawY=originY+cy2;
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.40)';
    c2.strokeText(chars[i],drawX,drawY);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,248,215,0.28)';
    c2.fillText(chars[i],drawX,drawY);
    x+=cws[i]+gap;
  }
  entry={oc, halfW:canW*0.5, halfH:canH*0.5};
  _allianceLabelImgCache.set(key,entry);_trimAllianceLabelImgCache();
  return entry;
}

// Alliance overlay offscreen canvas — rebuilt on dirty, blitted in one drawImage.
// ImageData replaces hundreds of thousands of 1×1 fillRect calls when alliances change.
let _alOverlayCanvas=null,_alOverlayCtx=null,_alOverlayData=null,_alOverlayU32=null,_alOverlayDirty=true,_alOverlayVersion=0;
let _alOverlayBitmap=null,_alOverlayBitmapGen=0;
function _ensureAllianceOverlay(){
  if(_alOverlayCanvas)return;
  _alOverlayCanvas=(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')?new OffscreenCanvas(WORLD_W,WORLD_H):document.createElement('canvas');_alOverlayCanvas.width=WORLD_W;_alOverlayCanvas.height=WORLD_H;
  _alOverlayCtx=_alOverlayCanvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
  _alOverlayData=_alOverlayCtx.createImageData(WORLD_W,WORLD_H);_alOverlayU32=new Uint32Array(_alOverlayData.data.buffer);
}
function _rebuildAllianceOverlay(){
  _ensureAllianceOverlay();
  const u=_alOverlayU32;u.fill(0);
  const civCol=new Uint32Array(civs.length);
  for(const al of alliances){
    let c=al.color.replace('#','');if(c.length===3)c=c[0]+c[0]+c[1]+c[1]+c[2]+c[2];
    const n=parseInt(c,16),abgr=(0xFF000000|((n&0xff)<<16)|(n&0xff00)|((n>>16)&0xff))>>>0;
    for(const cid of al.memberIds)if(cid>=0&&cid<civCol.length)civCol[cid]=abgr;
  }
  const tc=tileCivBuf,N=WORLD_W*WORLD_H;
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const cid=tc[i];u[i]=cid>=0?civCol[cid]||0:0;}
  _alOverlayCtx.putImageData(_alOverlayData,0,0);_alOverlayDirty=false;_alOverlayVersion++;
  // Avoid allocating another world-sized GPU resource. Direct OffscreenCanvas blits are
  // supported and eliminate async promotion churn when ownership changes in alliance view.
  if(_alOverlayBitmap?.close)_alOverlayBitmap.close();_alOverlayBitmap=null;_alOverlayBitmapGen++;
}

let _allianceBorderTex=null,_allianceBorderTexCtx=null,_allianceBorderTexZoom=NaN,_allianceBorderTexVersion=-1;
function _ensureAllianceBorderTexture(){
  if(_allianceBorderTex)return;
  _allianceBorderTex=document.createElement('canvas');_allianceBorderTex.width=WORLD_W;_allianceBorderTex.height=WORLD_H;
  _allianceBorderTexCtx=_allianceBorderTex.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
}
function _rebuildAllianceBorderTexture(){
  _ensureAllianceBorderTexture();const c=_allianceBorderTexCtx,tpx=Math.max(0.001,TS*zoom);
  c.clearRect(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);c.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.62)';c.lineWidth=1.5/tpx;c.lineCap='butt';c.lineJoin='miter';
  _strokeTileSegments(c,_allianceBorderSegs);_allianceBorderTexZoom=zoom;_allianceBorderTexVersion=_allianceBorderSegVersion;
}

function drawAllianceOverlay(){
  if(viewMode !== 'alliances') return;
  const tpx = TS * zoom;
  const sw = canvas.width, sh = canvas.height;
  ctx.save();
  // ── Alliance tint — single blitted offscreen canvas ─────────────────
  if(_alOverlayDirty || _alOverlayCanvas===null) _rebuildAllianceOverlay();
  if(_alOverlayCanvas){
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    ctx.drawImage(_alOverlayCanvas, camX,camY,sw/tpx,sh/tpx, 0,0,sw,sh);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;

  // ── Alliance borders: fast static edge graph + crisp screen-space vectors ──
  if(_allianceBorderDirty)_rebuildAllianceBorders();
  if(_allianceBorderSegs?.length){
    if(!_allianceBorderPathChunks)_allianceBorderPathChunks=_buildSegmentPathChunks(_allianceBorderSegs);
    _strokeVisiblePathChunks(_allianceBorderPathChunks,'rgba(0,0,0,0.62)',1.5);
  }
  ctx.restore();

  // ── Draw HOI4-style curved alliance name labels ───────────────────
  if(_allianceLabelDirty || !_allianceLabelCache) _buildAllianceLabelCache();
  // Retain recent quantized zoom variants instead of destroying every alliance label
  // canvas while the wheel/pinch gesture is still active.
  _allianceLabelImgZoom=zoom;
  if(!_allianceLabelCache || zoom < 0.10) return;

  const REF_FS=12.8;
  for(const lbl of _allianceLabelCache){
    const scx=(lbl.cx-camX)*tpx;
    const scy=(lbl.cy-camY)*tpx;
    if(scx<-400||scx>sw+400||scy<-80||scy>sh+80) continue;
    if(!lbl.refCWs){
      _lblMeasureCtx.font=`700 ${REF_FS}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
      lbl.chars   =[...lbl.name];
      lbl.refCWs  =lbl.chars.map(c=>_lblMeasureCtx.measureText(c).width);
      lbl.refTotal=lbl.refCWs.reduce((s,w)=>s+w,0);
    }
    if(!lbl.refTotal) continue;
    // Use same multiplier and clamp as nation labels so alliance text is consistent
    const targetW = lbl.spread*tpx*2.5;
    const desiredLetterW = targetW*0.65;
    let fs = REF_FS*desiredLetterW/lbl.refTotal;
    fs = Math.max(5.6, Math.min(38.4, fs));
    const scale   = fs/REF_FS;
    const totalCW = lbl.refTotal*scale;
    // Cap spreadW to the same natural ratio nations use (totalCW/0.65).
    // Without this cap, a clamped font-size leaves targetW >> totalCW,
    // blowing the inter-letter gap to enormous proportions.
    const spreadW = Math.max(totalCW * 1.15, Math.min(targetW, totalCW / 0.65));
    const img = _getOrBuildAllianceLabelImg(lbl, fs, spreadW);
    if(!img) continue;
    const ca=Math.cos(lbl.angle), sa=Math.sin(lbl.angle);
    const hw=img.halfW, hh=img.halfH;
    ctx.setTransform(ca,sa,-sa,ca, scx-hw*ca+hh*sa, scy-hw*sa-hh*ca);
    ctx.drawImage(img.oc, 0, 0);
  }
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
}

// ── FLAG RENDERING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Draws a rough procedural flag for the player's civ onto #gb-flag-canvas.
// Uses 2-3 color bands + optional symbol, all derived from the civ's color.
const FLAG_PATTERNS = {
  // country → [band colors as hex offsets from base, pattern type]
  // We generate procedurally from the base color.
};

function _flagDeriveColors(baseHex){
  let c=baseHex.replace('#','');
  if(c.length===3) c=c[0]+c[0]+c[1]+c[1]+c[2]+c[2];
  const n=parseInt(c,16);
  let r=(n>>16)&0xff, g=(n>>8)&0xff, b=n&0xff;
  // Complementary / analogous for 2nd and 3rd stripes
  const light=`#${Math.min(255,r+70).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.min(255,g+70).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.min(255,b+70).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  const dark=`#${Math.max(0,r-50).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.max(0,g-50).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.max(0,b-50).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  // A "complementary" accent (hue shift ~180°)
  const accent=`#${Math.min(255,255-r+30).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.min(255,255-g+20).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}${Math.min(255,255-b+10).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  return {base:baseHex, light, dark, accent};
}

// Named flag overrides: [top, middle, bottom] or [left, right] + symbol
const FLAG_OVERRIDES = {
  'France':         {bands:['#002395','#FFFFFF','#ED2939'],vert:true},
  'Germany':        {bands:['#000000','#DD0000','#FFCE00'],horiz:true},
  'Italy':          {bands:['#009246','#FFFFFF','#CE2B37'],vert:true},
  'Spain':          {bands:['#c60b1e','#ffc400','#c60b1e'],horiz:true},
  'Portugal':       {bands:['#006600','#ff0000','#006600'],vert:true},
  'United Kingdom': {bands:['#012169','#C8102E','#FFFFFF'],union:true},
  'Russia':         {bands:['#FFFFFF','#0039A6','#D52B1E'],horiz:true},
  'Ukraine':        {bands:['#005BBB','#FFD500'],horiz:true,twoband:true},
  'Poland':         {bands:['#FFFFFF','#DC143C'],horiz:true,twoband:true},
  'Netherlands':    {bands:['#AE1C28','#FFFFFF','#21468B'],horiz:true},
  'Belgium':        {bands:['#000000','#FAE042','#EF3340'],vert:true},
  'Switzerland':    {bands:['#FF0000'],cross:true},
  'Sweden':         {bands:['#006AA7','#FECC02'],cross:true,nordic:true},
  'Norway':         {bands:['#EF2B2D','#FFFFFF','#002868'],nordic:true},
  'Denmark':        {bands:['#C60C30','#FFFFFF'],nordic:true},
  'Finland':        {bands:['#003580','#FFFFFF'],nordic:true,flipped:true},
  'Austria':        {bands:['#ED2939','#FFFFFF','#ED2939'],horiz:true},
  'Turkey':         {bands:['#E30A17','#FFFFFF'],crescent:true},
  'Greece':         {bands:['#0D5EAF','#FFFFFF'],cross:true,greek:true},
  'Saudi Arabia':   {bands:['#006C35','#FFFFFF'],arabic:true},
  'Israel':         {bands:['#0038b8','#FFFFFF'],star:true},
  'Iraq':           {bands:['#CE1126','#FFFFFF','#000000'],horiz:true},
  'Egypt':          {bands:['#CE1126','#FFFFFF','#000000'],horiz:true},
  'Iran':           {bands:['#239F40','#FFFFFF','#DA0000'],horiz:true},
  'Kazakhstan':     {bands:['#00AFCA','#FFD700'],solar:true},
  'China':          {bands:['#DE2910','#FFDE00'],star:true},
  'Japan':          {bands:['#FFFFFF','#BC002D'],circle:true},
};

function renderFlagCanvas(civName, civColor){
  const canvas2 = document.getElementById('gb-flag-canvas');
  if(!canvas2) return;
  const ctx2 = canvas2.getContext('2d');
  const W = canvas2.width, H = canvas2.height;
  ctx2.clearRect(0,0,W,H);

  const ov = FLAG_OVERRIDES[civName];
  const cl = _flagDeriveColors(civColor);

  if(ov){
    _drawFlagOverride(ctx2, W, H, ov, civName);
  } else {
    // Procedural: determine pattern from hash of name
    let hash = 0;
    for(let i=0;i<civName.length;i++) hash = (hash*31 + civName.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xffff;
    const pattern = hash % 4;
    if(pattern === 0){
      // 3 horizontal bands
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.dark; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H/3);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.base; ctx2.fillRect(0,H/3,W,H/3);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.light; ctx2.fillRect(0,H*2/3,W,H/3+1);
    } else if(pattern === 1){
      // 3 vertical bands
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.light; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W/3,H);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.base; ctx2.fillRect(W/3,0,W/3,H);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.dark; ctx2.fillRect(W*2/3,0,W/3+1,H);
    } else if(pattern === 2){
      // 2 horizontal bands
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.base; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H/2);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.dark; ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2,W,H/2+1);
    } else {
      // diagonal bicolor
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.base; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
      ctx2.fillStyle=cl.dark;
      ctx2.beginPath();
      ctx2.moveTo(0,H); ctx2.lineTo(W,0); ctx2.lineTo(W,H); ctx2.closePath();
      ctx2.fill();
    }
    // Small circle in center as emblem
    ctx2.beginPath();
    ctx2.arc(W/2,H/2,H*0.18,0,Math.PI*2);
    ctx2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.55)';
    ctx2.fill();
    ctx2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
    ctx2.lineWidth=0.8;
    ctx2.stroke();
  }
  // Flag border overlay
  ctx2.strokeStyle='rgba(200,140,50,0.4)';
  ctx2.lineWidth=1;
  ctx2.strokeRect(0.5,0.5,W-1,H-1);
}

function _drawFlagOverride(ctx2, W, H, ov, name){
  const bands = ov.bands;
  if(ov.union){
    // Union Jack approximation
    ctx2.fillStyle='#012169'; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx2.strokeStyle='#FFFFFF'; ctx2.lineWidth=H*0.32;
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.moveTo(0,0); ctx2.lineTo(W,H); ctx2.stroke();
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.moveTo(W,0); ctx2.lineTo(0,H); ctx2.stroke();
    ctx2.strokeStyle='#C8102E'; ctx2.lineWidth=H*0.16;
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.moveTo(0,0); ctx2.lineTo(W,H); ctx2.stroke();
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.moveTo(W,0); ctx2.lineTo(0,H); ctx2.stroke();
    ctx2.fillStyle='#FFFFFF'; ctx2.fillRect(W/2-W*0.14,0,W*0.28,H);
    ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2-H*0.14,W,H*0.28);
    ctx2.fillStyle='#C8102E'; ctx2.fillRect(W/2-W*0.09,0,W*0.18,H);
    ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2-H*0.09,W,H*0.18);
    return;
  }
  if(ov.nordic){
    const base = bands[0], cross = bands[1]||'#FFFFFF';
    ctx2.fillStyle=base; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    const cx = W*0.33, cw=W*0.14, ch=H*0.22;
    ctx2.fillStyle=cross;
    ctx2.fillRect(cx-cw/2,0,cw,H);
    ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2-ch/2,W,ch);
    if(bands[2]){
      ctx2.fillStyle=bands[2];
      ctx2.fillRect(cx-cw*0.5*0.55,0,cw*0.55,H);
      ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2-ch*0.5*0.55,W,ch*0.55);
    }
    return;
  }
  if(ov.cross){
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]||'#FF0000'; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    if(bands[1]){
      const cw=W*0.18, ch=H*0.22;
      ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1];
      ctx2.fillRect(W/2-cw/2,0,cw,H);
      ctx2.fillRect(0,H/2-ch/2,W,ch);
    }
    return;
  }
  if(ov.crescent){
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]||'#E30A17'; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    // White star and crescent
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1]||'#FFFFFF';
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.arc(W*0.42,H/2,H*0.32,0,Math.PI*2); ctx2.fill();
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]||'#E30A17';
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.arc(W*0.49,H/2,H*0.27,0,Math.PI*2); ctx2.fill();
    return;
  }
  if(ov.circle){
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]||'#FFFFFF'; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1]||'#BC002D';
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.arc(W/2,H/2,H*0.30,0,Math.PI*2); ctx2.fill();
    return;
  }
  if(ov.solar){
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]||'#00AFCA'; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1]||'#FFD700';
    ctx2.beginPath(); ctx2.arc(W*0.35,H/2,H*0.28,0,Math.PI*2); ctx2.fill();
    return;
  }
  if(ov.twoband){
    const half = ov.vert ? W/2 : H/2;
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[0]; ctx2.fillRect(0,0,ov.vert?half:W,ov.vert?H:half);
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1]; ctx2.fillRect(ov.vert?half:0,ov.vert?0:half,ov.vert?W-half:W,ov.vert?H:H-half);
    return;
  }
  // Default: n horizontal or vertical bands
  const n=bands.length;
  if(ov.vert){
    const bw=W/n;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ ctx2.fillStyle=bands[i]; ctx2.fillRect(i*bw,0,bw+1,H); }
  } else {
    const bh=H/n;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ ctx2.fillStyle=bands[i]; ctx2.fillRect(0,i*bh,W,bh+1); }
  }
  // Optional emblem overlays
  if(ov.star){
    const sr=H*0.2, sc=W*0.25, sy2=H/2;
    ctx2.fillStyle=bands[1]||'#FFFFFF';
    _drawFlagStar(ctx2, sc, sy2, sr);
  }
}
function _drawFlagStar(ctx2, cx, cy, r){
  const spikes=5, inner=r*0.42;
  ctx2.beginPath();
  for(let i=0;i<spikes*2;i++){
    const ang=(i*Math.PI/spikes)-Math.PI/2;
    const rad=i%2===0?r:inner;
    i===0?ctx2.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad,cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad):ctx2.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*rad,cy+Math.sin(ang)*rad);
  }
  ctx2.closePath(); ctx2.fill();
}

function updateFlagBanner(){
  if(playerCivId<0){
    document.getElementById('gb-flag-civname').textContent='—';
    const fc=document.getElementById('gb-flag-canvas');
    if(fc){const c2=fc.getContext('2d');c2.clearRect(0,0,fc.width,fc.height);}
    return;
  }
  const civ=civs[playerCivId];
  document.getElementById('gb-flag-civname').textContent=civ.name.toUpperCase();
  renderFlagCanvas(civ.name, civ.color);
}


// ── GEN: TERRITORY TOOLS — GIVE LAND / NEW STATE / FREE NATIONS ─
let _giveLandMode=false;            // true while a territory tool owns map clicks
let _giveLandAction='give';         // 'give' | 'new' | 'free'
let _giveLandSelected=new Set();
let _giveLandBrush=1;
let _giveLandHoverPid=-1;

function _giveLandRecipients(){
  if(playerCivId<0) return [];
  // Include every current and editor/peace-created state, even landless states,
  // because gifting land is also a valid way to revive a conquered state.
  return civs.filter(c=>c&&c.id!==playerCivId).sort((a,b)=>{
    if(!!a.customNation!==!!b.customNation)return a.customNation?1:-1;
    return (a.name||'').localeCompare(b.name||'');
  });
}

function _populateGiveLandRecipients(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('gb-give-recipient'); if(!sel) return;
  const prev=parseInt(sel.value);sel.innerHTML='';
  const current=document.createElement('optgroup');current.label='CURRENT STATES';
  const newer=document.createElement('optgroup');newer.label='NEW STATES';
  let nCurrent=0,nNew=0;
  for(const c of _giveLandRecipients()){
    const opt=document.createElement('option');opt.value=c.id;
    const dead=c.ownedProvs.size===0?' · no land':'';
    opt.textContent=`${c.name}${dead}`;
    if(c.customNation){newer.appendChild(opt);nNew++;}else{current.appendChild(opt);nCurrent++;}
  }
  if(nCurrent)sel.appendChild(current);if(nNew)sel.appendChild(newer);
  if([...sel.options].some(o=>+o.value===prev)) sel.value=String(prev);
}

function _giveLandBrushProvs(pid){
  return _provinceBrush(pid,_giveLandBrush,p=>provOwner[p]===playerCivId&&provinces[p]&&!provinces[p].isSea);
}

function _freeNationHomeOwner(pid){
  if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea)return -1;
  if(!_originalMapOwnerIds||_originalMapOwnerIds.length!==provinces.length){
    _originalMapOwnerIds=_deriveOriginalMapOwnersFromScenario()||Int16Array.from(provOwner);
  }
  const id=Number(_originalMapOwnerIds?.[pid]);
  return Number.isInteger(id)&&id>=0&&id<civs.length&&civs[id]&&id!==playerCivId?id:-1;
}

function _selectedFreeNationTerritoryValid(){
  const out=[];
  for(const pid of _giveLandSelected){
    if(provOwner[pid]!==playerCivId||!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea)continue;
    if(_freeNationHomeOwner(pid)>=0)out.push(pid);
  }
  return out;
}

function _renderGiveLandWidget(){
  const root=document.getElementById('gb-give-land-widget'),giveStart=document.getElementById('gb-give-start'),newStart=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-start'),freeStart=document.getElementById('gb-free-nations-start');
  const controls=document.getElementById('gb-give-controls'),status=document.getElementById('gb-give-status'),confirm=document.getElementById('gb-give-confirm'),newConfirm=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-confirm'),freeConfirm=document.getElementById('gb-free-confirm');
  const existing=document.getElementById('gb-give-existing-controls'),newControls=document.getElementById('gb-give-new-controls'),freeControls=document.getElementById('gb-free-controls'),hint=document.getElementById('gb-give-hint');
  if(!root||!giveStart||!newStart||!freeStart||!controls||!status||!confirm||!newConfirm||!freeConfirm)return;
  root.classList.toggle('gb-give-active',_giveLandMode);root.classList.toggle('mode-new',_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='new');root.classList.toggle('mode-free',_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='free');
  giveStart.classList.toggle('active',_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='give');newStart.classList.toggle('active',_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='new');freeStart.classList.toggle('active',_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='free');
  giveStart.textContent=_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='give'?'🗺 GIVING LAND…':'🗺 GIVE LAND';
  newStart.textContent=_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='new'?'＋ CREATING STATE…':'＋ NEW STATE';
  freeStart.textContent=_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='free'?'🕊 FREEING…':'🕊 FREE NATIONS';
  controls.style.display=_giveLandMode?'block':'none';
  if(existing)existing.style.display=_giveLandAction==='give'?'block':'none';
  if(newControls)newControls.style.display=_giveLandAction==='new'?'block':'none';
  if(freeControls)freeControls.style.display=_giveLandAction==='free'?'block':'none';
  if(hint)hint.textContent=_giveLandAction==='give'
    ?'Brush-select your own provinces, choose any current/new state, then Give Selected.'
    :_giveLandAction==='new'
      ?'Brush-select your own provinces, name the new sovereign state, choose its colour, then Create.'
      :'Brush-select occupied land you own. Free Nations restores each selected province to its original scenario-start nation automatically.';
  const owned=playerCivId>=0?(civs[playerCivId]?.ownedProvs?.size||0):0;
  const releasable=_giveLandAction==='free'?_selectedFreeNationTerritoryValid():[];
  status.textContent=_giveLandAction==='free'
    ?`${_giveLandSelected.size} selected · ${releasable.length} releasable · brush ${_giveLandBrush} · ${owned} owned`
    :`${_giveLandSelected.size} province${_giveLandSelected.size===1?'':'s'} selected · brush ${_giveLandBrush} · ${owned} owned`;
  const keepAtLeastOne=owned-_giveLandSelected.size>=1;
  const recipient=parseInt(document.getElementById('gb-give-recipient')?.value);
  confirm.disabled=!_giveLandSelected.size||!Number.isInteger(recipient)||!civs[recipient]||!keepAtLeastOne;
  confirm.title=keepAtLeastOne?'Transfer the selected provinces':'You must keep at least one province';
  const nm=(document.getElementById('gb-new-state-name')?.value||'').trim();
  const duplicate=nm&&civs.some(c=>c&&c.name.toLowerCase()===nm.toLowerCase());
  newConfirm.disabled=!_giveLandSelected.size||!keepAtLeastOne||nm.length<2||duplicate;
  newConfirm.title=!keepAtLeastOne?'You must keep at least one province':duplicate?'That state name already exists':nm.length<2?'Enter a state name':'Create a sovereign new state from the selected land';
  const freeKeepsOne=owned-releasable.length>=1;
  freeConfirm.disabled=!releasable.length||!freeKeepsOne;
  freeConfirm.title=!releasable.length?'No selected province belongs to a different original nation':!freeKeepsOne?'You must keep at least one province':'Restore selected occupied land to its original nations';
}

function _startGiveLandMode(action='give'){
  if(playerCivId<0||!gameReady||editorMode||peaceConference)return;
  if(typeof _cancelBorderPicker==='function')_cancelBorderPicker();
  const switchOnly=_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction!==action;
  _giveLandAction=action;_giveLandMode=true;_giveLandHoverPid=-1;
  if(!switchOnly)_giveLandSelected.clear();
  _populateGiveLandRecipients();closePanel();selectedDivs.clear();updateMilBar();_renderGiveLandWidget();
  const msg=action==='give'?'GIVE LAND: hover to preview brush, click your provinces, then choose a state'
    :action==='new'?'NEW STATE: hover to preview brush, select your land, then name + create'
    :'FREE NATIONS: select occupied land you own; it will return to its original scenario-start nations';
  showWarNotification(msg,action==='free'?'#7fcf94':'#f0a030');
}

function _cancelGiveLandMode(notify=false){
  _giveLandMode=false;_giveLandSelected.clear();_giveLandHoverPid=-1;_renderGiveLandWidget();
  if(notify)showWarNotification('Territory tool cancelled','#a08050');
}

function _toggleGiveLandProvince(pid){
  if(!_giveLandMode||playerCivId<0||pid<0||!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea)return;
  if(provOwner[pid]!==playerCivId){showWarNotification('You can only select land you currently own','#d08050');return;}
  const brush=_giveLandBrushProvs(pid);if(!brush.length)return;
  const allSelected=brush.every(p=>_giveLandSelected.has(p));
  for(const p of brush){if(allSelected)_giveLandSelected.delete(p);else _giveLandSelected.add(p);}
  AudioEngine.paintStroke();
  _renderGiveLandWidget();
}

function _selectedOwnTerritoryValid(){
  return [..._giveLandSelected].filter(pid=>provOwner[pid]===playerCivId&&provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea);
}

function _afterOwnTerritoryTransfer(){
  _repatriatePeaceDivisions();_giveLandSelected.clear();_giveLandHoverPid=-1;_populateGiveLandRecipients();_renderGiveLandWidget();
  _terrBorderDirty=true;_civLabelDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;_markWorldFullDirty();_hlDirty=true;
  buildGenBar();buildAllianceWidget();
}

function _confirmGiveLand(){
  if(!_giveLandMode||_giveLandAction!=='give'||playerCivId<0||!_giveLandSelected.size)return;
  const recipientId=parseInt(document.getElementById('gb-give-recipient')?.value),recipient=civs[recipientId];if(!recipient||recipientId===playerCivId)return;
  const valid=_selectedOwnTerritoryValid(),owned=civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs.size;if(!valid.length)return;
  if(owned-valid.length<1){showWarNotification('Keep at least one province in your own country','#d06050');return;}
  _suppressWarContribution=true;try{for(const pid of valid)civClaimProvince(pid,recipientId);}finally{_suppressWarContribution=false;}
  _afterOwnTerritoryTransfer();AudioEngine.gift();showWarNotification(`Gave ${valid.length} province${valid.length===1?'':'s'} to ${recipient.name}`,recipient.color||'#f0a030');
}

function _confirmCreateOwnNewState(){
  if(!_giveLandMode||_giveLandAction!=='new'||playerCivId<0||!_giveLandSelected.size)return;
  const valid=_selectedOwnTerritoryValid(),owned=civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs.size;if(!valid.length)return;
  if(owned-valid.length<1){showWarNotification('Keep at least one province in your own country','#d06050');return;}
  const name=(document.getElementById('gb-new-state-name')?.value||'').trim().replace(/[<>&]/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const color=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-color')?.value||'#4f9ecf';
  if(name.length<2){showWarNotification('Enter a new state name','#d08050');return;}
  if(civs.some(c=>c&&c.name.toLowerCase()===name.toLowerCase())){showWarNotification('That state name already exists','#d06050');return;}
  const n=_makeCustomCiv(name,color,-1);n.res=civStartRes(valid.length);if(!n.techs)n.techs=new Set();
  _suppressWarContribution=true;try{for(const pid of valid)civClaimProvince(pid,n.id);}finally{_suppressWarContribution=false;}
  const nameEl=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-name');if(nameEl)nameEl.value='';
  _afterOwnTerritoryTransfer();AudioEngine.revolt();showWarNotification(`Created sovereign state ${n.name} with ${valid.length} province${valid.length===1?'':'s'}`,n.color||'#4f9ecf');
}

function _confirmFreeNations(){
  if(!_giveLandMode||_giveLandAction!=='free'||playerCivId<0||!_giveLandSelected.size)return;
  const valid=_selectedFreeNationTerritoryValid(),owned=civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs.size;
  if(!valid.length){showWarNotification('No selected land belongs to a different original nation','#d08050');return;}
  if(owned-valid.length<1){showWarNotification('Keep at least one province in your own country','#d06050');return;}
  const counts=new Map();
  _suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{
    for(const pid of valid){const home=_freeNationHomeOwner(pid);if(home<0)continue;counts.set(home,(counts.get(home)||0)+1);civClaimProvince(pid,home);}
  }finally{_suppressWarContribution=false;}
  const names=[...counts.keys()].map(id=>civs[id]?.name).filter(Boolean);
  const label=names.length<=4?names.join(', '):`${names.slice(0,4).join(', ')} +${names.length-4} more`;
  _afterOwnTerritoryTransfer();AudioEngine.peace();showWarNotification(`Freed ${counts.size} nation${counts.size===1?'':'s'} across ${valid.length} province${valid.length===1?'':'s'}${label?' · '+label:''}`,'#7fcf94');
}

function drawGiveLandOverlay(){
  if(!_giveLandMode)return;
  const tpx=TS*zoom,sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height,hover=_giveLandBrushProvs(_giveLandHoverPid);
  ctx.save();
  const fillSel=_giveLandAction==='new'?'rgba(90,165,255,.30)':_giveLandAction==='free'?'rgba(100,215,145,.30)':'rgba(90,220,120,.30)';
  const strokeSel=_giveLandAction==='new'?'rgba(160,210,255,.98)':_giveLandAction==='free'?'rgba(175,255,200,.98)':'rgba(160,255,180,.95)';
  ctx.fillStyle=fillSel;ctx.strokeStyle=strokeSel;ctx.lineWidth=2;
  const drawProv=(pid,fill=true,stroke=true)=>{const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)return;if(fill)for(const ti of p.tiles){const tx2=ti%WORLD_W,ty2=(ti/WORLD_W)|0,sx=(tx2-camX)*tpx,sy=(ty2-camY)*tpx;if(sx<-tpx||sy<-tpx||sx>sw+tpx||sy>sh+tpx)continue;ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,tpx,tpx);}if(stroke){let segs=_provOutlineCache.get(pid);if(!segs)segs=_buildProvOutline(pid);ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=4){const x1=(segs[i]-camX)*tpx,y1=(segs[i+1]-camY)*tpx,x2=(segs[i+2]-camX)*tpx,y2=(segs[i+3]-camY)*tpx;if((x1<-3&&x2<-3)||(x1>sw+3&&x2>sw+3)||(y1<-3&&y2<-3)||(y1>sh+3&&y2>sh+3))continue;ctx.moveTo(x1,y1);ctx.lineTo(x2,y2);}ctx.stroke();}};
  for(const pid of _giveLandSelected)drawProv(pid,true,true);
  if(hover.length){ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.13)';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,230,145,.92)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;for(const pid of hover)drawProv(pid,true,true);}
  ctx.restore();
}

function _initGiveLandControls(){
  const giveStart=document.getElementById('gb-give-start'),newStart=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-start'),freeStart=document.getElementById('gb-free-nations-start'),confirm=document.getElementById('gb-give-confirm'),newConfirm=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-confirm'),freeConfirm=document.getElementById('gb-free-confirm'),cancel=document.getElementById('gb-give-cancel'),clear=document.getElementById('gb-give-clear'),recipient=document.getElementById('gb-give-recipient'),brush=document.getElementById('gb-give-brush'),nameEl=document.getElementById('gb-new-state-name');
  if(giveStart&&!giveStart.dataset.bound){giveStart.dataset.bound='1';giveStart.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='give')_cancelGiveLandMode(true);else _startGiveLandMode('give');});}
  if(newStart&&!newStart.dataset.bound){newStart.dataset.bound='1';newStart.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='new')_cancelGiveLandMode(true);else _startGiveLandMode('new');});}
  if(freeStart&&!freeStart.dataset.bound){freeStart.dataset.bound='1';freeStart.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(_giveLandMode&&_giveLandAction==='free')_cancelGiveLandMode(true);else _startGiveLandMode('free');});}
  if(confirm&&!confirm.dataset.bound){confirm.dataset.bound='1';confirm.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_confirmGiveLand();});}
  if(newConfirm&&!newConfirm.dataset.bound){newConfirm.dataset.bound='1';newConfirm.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_confirmCreateOwnNewState();});}
  if(freeConfirm&&!freeConfirm.dataset.bound){freeConfirm.dataset.bound='1';freeConfirm.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_confirmFreeNations();});}
  if(cancel&&!cancel.dataset.bound){cancel.dataset.bound='1';cancel.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_cancelGiveLandMode(true);});}
  if(clear&&!clear.dataset.bound){clear.dataset.bound='1';clear.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_giveLandSelected.clear();_renderGiveLandWidget();});}
  if(recipient&&!recipient.dataset.bound){recipient.dataset.bound='1';recipient.addEventListener('change',_renderGiveLandWidget);}
  if(brush&&!brush.dataset.bound){brush.dataset.bound='1';brush.addEventListener('input',e=>{_giveLandBrush=Math.max(1,Math.min(5,parseInt(e.target.value)||1));const v=document.getElementById('gb-give-brush-val');if(v)v.textContent=_giveLandBrush;_renderGiveLandWidget();});}
  if(nameEl&&!nameEl.dataset.bound){nameEl.dataset.bound='1';nameEl.addEventListener('input',_renderGiveLandWidget);nameEl.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&_giveLandAction==='new'){e.preventDefault();_confirmCreateOwnNewState();}});}
  if(!_giveLandMode||!recipient||recipient.options.length===0)_populateGiveLandRecipients();
  const v=document.getElementById('gb-give-brush-val');if(v)v.textContent=_giveLandBrush;_renderGiveLandWidget();
}

// ── ALLIANCE WIDGET BUILDER ────────────────────────────────────────
function buildAllianceWidget(){
  const el=document.getElementById('gb-alliance-widget');
  if(!el) return;
  el.innerHTML='';

  const wrap=document.createElement('div');

  const hdr=document.createElement('div');
  hdr.className='gb-alliance-header';
  hdr.textContent='ALLIANCE';
  wrap.appendChild(hdr);

  if(playerCivId<0){
    const st=document.createElement('div');
    st.className='gb-alliance-status-row';
    st.textContent='No nation selected.';
    wrap.appendChild(st);
    el.appendChild(wrap);
    return;
  }

  const civ=civs[playerCivId];
  const myAlliance=getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
  const canAfford=civ.res.coins>=ALLIANCE_JOIN_COST;

  if(myAlliance){
    const isLeader=myAlliance.leaderId===playerCivId;
    // Badge
    const badge=document.createElement('div');
    badge.className='gb-alliance-badge';
    badge.innerHTML=(isLeader?'<span class="ab-crown">👑</span>':'')+`<span>${myAlliance.name}</span>`;
    badge.style.borderColor=myAlliance.color;
    badge.style.color=myAlliance.color;
    wrap.appendChild(badge);

    const st=document.createElement('div');
    st.className='gb-alliance-status-row';
    st.textContent=isLeader?'You are the Alliance Leader.':'Member of this alliance.';
    wrap.appendChild(st);

    // Divider
    const div2=document.createElement('div'); div2.className='gb-alliance-divider'; wrap.appendChild(div2);

    // Members list with action buttons
    const memHdr=document.createElement('div'); memHdr.className='gb-alliance-members-hdr'; memHdr.textContent='MEMBERS'; wrap.appendChild(memHdr);
    for(const mid of myAlliance.memberIds){
      if(mid<0||mid>=civs.length) continue;
      const mc=civs[mid];
      const item=document.createElement('div'); item.className='gb-alliance-member-item';
      const isSelf = mid === playerCivId;
      const isThisLeader = myAlliance.leaderId === mid;

      // Name + crown
      const nameSpan = document.createElement('span');
      nameSpan.innerHTML = `<span class="gb-alliance-member-dot" style="background:${mc.color}"></span><span class="gb-alliance-member-name">${mc.name}</span>${isThisLeader?'<span class="gb-alliance-member-crown">👑</span>':''}`;
      nameSpan.style.flex = '1';
      nameSpan.style.display = 'flex';
      nameSpan.style.alignItems = 'center';
      nameSpan.style.minWidth = '0';
      item.appendChild(nameSpan);

      if(!isSelf && isLeader){
        // Kick button
        const kickBtn = document.createElement('button');
        kickBtn.className = 'gb-ab-sm kick-btn';
        kickBtn.textContent = '✕ Kick';
        kickBtn.title = 'Kick ' + mc.name + ' from alliance';
        kickBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          kickFromAlliance(mid);
          buildAllianceWidget();
        });
        item.appendChild(kickBtn);

        // Betray button
        const betrayBtn = document.createElement('button');
        betrayBtn.className = 'gb-ab-sm betray-btn';
        betrayBtn.textContent = '⚔ Betray';
        betrayBtn.title = 'Betray and declare war on ' + mc.name;
        betrayBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          betrayAndAttack(mid);
          buildAllianceWidget();
          if(selectedProv >= 0) openPanel(selectedProv);
        });
        item.appendChild(betrayBtn);
      }

      wrap.appendChild(item);
    }

    const div3=document.createElement('div'); div3.className='gb-alliance-divider'; wrap.appendChild(div3);

    if(isLeader){
      // Rename alliance
      const inp=document.createElement('input');
      inp.className='gb-alliance-name-input';
      inp.placeholder='Rename alliance…';
      inp.value=myAlliance.name;
      inp.addEventListener('input', ()=>{ myAlliance.name=inp.value.trim()||'Alliance'; _allianceLabelDirty=true; _allianceLabelImgCache.clear(); updateFlagBanner(); });
      wrap.appendChild(inp);

      // ── Alliance color picker ───────────────────────────────────
      const colorRow = document.createElement('div');
      colorRow.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:7px';
      const colorLabel = document.createElement('span');
      colorLabel.style.cssText = "font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:8px;color:#a08040;letter-spacing:1.2px;flex:1";
      colorLabel.textContent = 'ALLIANCE COLOR';
      const colorPicker = document.createElement('input');
      colorPicker.type = 'color';
      colorPicker.value = myAlliance.color;
      // Horizontally invert (mirror along Y axis) the color picker swatch
      colorPicker.style.cssText = 'width:32px;height:20px;padding:0;border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.3);background:transparent;cursor:pointer;border-radius:3px;transform:scaleX(-1)';
      colorPicker.addEventListener('input', () => {
        myAlliance.color = colorPicker.value;
        _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
        _allianceLabelImgCache.clear();
        badge.style.borderColor = myAlliance.color;
        badge.style.color = myAlliance.color;
        // Mirror the visual preview swatch next to label
        colorPreview.style.background = myAlliance.color;
      });
      // Mirrored live preview swatch
      const colorPreview = document.createElement('span');
      colorPreview.style.cssText = `display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.3);background:${myAlliance.color};transform:scaleX(-1);flex-shrink:0`;
      colorRow.appendChild(colorLabel);
      colorRow.appendChild(colorPreview);
      colorRow.appendChild(colorPicker);
      wrap.appendChild(colorRow);

      // ── Invite nations section ──────────────────────────────────
      const invitableCivs = civs.filter(c =>
        c.id !== playerCivId &&
        c.ownedProvs.size > 0 &&
        !getAllianceOfCiv(c.id)
      );
      if(invitableCivs.length > 0){
        const invDiv3=document.createElement('div'); invDiv3.className='gb-alliance-divider'; wrap.appendChild(invDiv3);
        const invHdr=document.createElement('div'); invHdr.className='gb-alliance-members-hdr'; invHdr.textContent='INVITE NATION'; wrap.appendChild(invHdr);

        const invSel=document.createElement('select'); invSel.className='gb-alliance-join-select';
        for(const c of invitableCivs){
          const opt=document.createElement('option'); opt.value=c.id; opt.textContent=c.name; invSel.appendChild(opt);
        }
        wrap.appendChild(invSel);

        const invBtn=document.createElement('button');
        invBtn.className='gb-ab promote';
        invBtn.textContent='📜 INVITE TO ALLIANCE';
        invBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
          const tId=parseInt(invSel.value);
          inviteToAlliance(myAlliance.id, tId);
          buildAllianceWidget();
        });
        wrap.appendChild(invBtn);
      }

      const div4=document.createElement('div'); div4.className='gb-alliance-divider'; wrap.appendChild(div4);

      // Disband
      const disbandBtn=document.createElement('button');
      disbandBtn.className='gb-ab leave';
      disbandBtn.textContent='✕ DISBAND ALLIANCE';
      disbandBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        disbandAlliance(myAlliance.id);
        _allianceLabelDirty=true;
        buildAllianceWidget();
      });
      wrap.appendChild(disbandBtn);
    } else {
      // Leave
      const leaveBtn=document.createElement('button');
      leaveBtn.className='gb-ab leave';
      leaveBtn.textContent='← LEAVE ALLIANCE';
      leaveBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        leaveAlliance(playerCivId);
        buildAllianceWidget();
      });
      wrap.appendChild(leaveBtn);
    }
  } else {
    // Not in an alliance
    const st=document.createElement('div');
    st.className='gb-alliance-status-row';
    st.textContent='Not in any alliance.';
    wrap.appendChild(st);

    // Create alliance
    const createSection=document.createElement('div'); createSection.style.marginBottom='8px';
    const inp=document.createElement('input');
    inp.className='gb-alliance-name-input';
    inp.placeholder='Alliance name…';
    inp.id='gb-new-alliance-name';
    createSection.appendChild(inp);
    const createBtn=document.createElement('button');
    createBtn.className='gb-ab create';
    createBtn.textContent='⚡ CREATE ALLIANCE (FREE)';
    createBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const name=document.getElementById('gb-new-alliance-name')?.value||'New Alliance';
      createAlliance(playerCivId, name);
      buildAllianceWidget();
    });
    createSection.appendChild(createBtn);
    wrap.appendChild(createSection);

    // Join existing alliance
    const otherAlliances=alliances.filter(a=>!a.memberIds.has(playerCivId));
    if(otherAlliances.length>0){
      const div2=document.createElement('div'); div2.className='gb-alliance-divider'; wrap.appendChild(div2);

      const joinHdr=document.createElement('div'); joinHdr.className='gb-alliance-members-hdr'; joinHdr.textContent='JOIN EXISTING'; wrap.appendChild(joinHdr);

      const sel=document.createElement('select'); sel.className='gb-alliance-join-select';
      for(const a of otherAlliances){
        const opt=document.createElement('option'); opt.value=a.id; opt.textContent=a.name; sel.appendChild(opt);
      }
      wrap.appendChild(sel);

      const cost=document.createElement('div');
      cost.className='gb-alliance-cost'+(canAfford?'':' no');
      cost.textContent=`Cost: ${ALLIANCE_JOIN_COST} 🪙 coins ${canAfford?'(affordable)':'(insufficient)'}`;
      wrap.appendChild(cost);

      const joinBtn=document.createElement('button');
      joinBtn.className='gb-ab join';
      joinBtn.textContent='🤝 JOIN ALLIANCE';
      joinBtn.disabled=!canAfford;
      joinBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const alId=parseInt(sel.value);
        joinAlliance(alId, playerCivId);
        buildAllianceWidget();
      });
      wrap.appendChild(joinBtn);
    }
  }
  el.appendChild(wrap);
}

let _menuMapFrameValid=false,_menuMapW=0,_menuMapH=0,_menuMapCamX=NaN,_menuMapCamY=NaN,_menuMapZoom=NaN;
let _menuMapBorders=null,_menuMapLabels=null,_menuMapView='';
function drawMainMenuWorldMap(){
  const needsRedraw=!_menuMapFrameValid || _menuMapW!==canvas.width || _menuMapH!==canvas.height ||
    _menuMapCamX!==camX || _menuMapCamY!==camY || _menuMapZoom!==zoom ||
    _menuMapBorders!==showBorders || _menuMapLabels!==showLabels || _menuMapView!==viewMode ||
    _worldDirty || _terrBorderDirty || _civLabelDirty ||
    (viewMode==='alliances' && (_alOverlayDirty || _allianceBorderDirty || _allianceLabelDirty));
  if(!needsRedraw) return;
  // Territory paths are rebuilt lazily per visible chunk inside drawTerritoryBorders().
  if(_civLabelDirty){_buildCivLabelCache();_civLabelLastBuild=time;}
  _renderBaseLayer(true);_renderMapLayer(true);_renderLabelLayer(true,true);
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  fxCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);fxCtx.clearRect(0,0,fxCanvas.width,fxCanvas.height);
  _menuMapW=canvas.width;_menuMapH=canvas.height;_menuMapCamX=camX;_menuMapCamY=camY;_menuMapZoom=zoom;
  _menuMapBorders=showBorders;_menuMapLabels=showLabels;_menuMapView=viewMode;_menuMapFrameValid=true;
}

// ── LAYERED MAP COMPOSITOR ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// The old renderer copied the full static map and the full label overlay back onto the
// gameplay canvas every RAF.  The browser now keeps those layers as independent canvases:
//   map-c   : terrain/political colours/borders/alliance tint (redraw only when view changes)
//   c       : dynamic gameplay sprites/highlights/routes/particles
//   label-c : country/city labels (redraw only when view/labels change)
//   fx-c    : top-most peace/notification effects
// This removes full-screen map cache blits from steady animation frames.
let _baseLayerValid=false,_baseLayerKey='';
let _mapLayerValid=false,_labelLayerValid=false,_mapLayerKey='',_labelLayerKey='',_mapLayerViewKey='';
let _mapLayerCamX=NaN,_mapLayerCamY=NaN,_mapLayerZoom=NaN,_mapLayerContentKey='';
let _labelLayerCamX=NaN,_labelLayerCamY=NaN,_labelLayerZoom=NaN,_labelLayerContentKey='';
let _cameraMotionUntil=0,_cameraZoomUntil=0,_lastExactMapCameraRender=0,_lastExactLabelCameraRender=0;
const _cameraCompositorReadyAt=performance.now()+420;
const _CAMERA_SETTLE_MS=90,_MAP_CAMERA_COMMIT_MS=34,_LABEL_CAMERA_COMMIT_MS=34;
function _cameraCompositorReady(){return performance.now()>=_cameraCompositorReadyAt;}
function _markCameraMotion(ms=_CAMERA_SETTLE_MS){const now=performance.now();_cameraMotionUntil=Math.max(_cameraMotionUntil,now+ms);}
function _markCameraZoom(ms=140){const now=performance.now();_cameraZoomUntil=Math.max(_cameraZoomUntil,now+ms);_cameraMotionUntil=Math.max(_cameraMotionUntil,now+ms);}
function _cameraMotionActive(){return performance.now()<_cameraMotionUntil;}
function _cameraZoomActive(){return performance.now()<_cameraZoomUntil;}
function _resetLayerTransform(el){if(el.style.transform)el.style.transform='';if(el.style.willChange)el.style.willChange='';}
function _applyLayerCameraTransform(el,baseX,baseY,baseZoom){
  if(!Number.isFinite(baseX)||!Number.isFinite(baseY)||!Number.isFinite(baseZoom)||baseZoom<=0){_resetLayerTransform(el);return;}
  const scale=zoom/baseZoom,dx=(baseX-camX)*TS*zoom,dy=(baseY-camY)*TS*zoom;el.style.willChange='transform';el.style.transformOrigin='0 0';el.style.transform=`translate3d(${dx}px,${dy}px,0) scale(${scale})`;
}
function _ensureStaticFrameSize(){return baseCanvas.width===canvas.width&&baseCanvas.height===canvas.height;}
function _staticBaseSceneKey(){return _mapSceneKey();}
function _staticOverlaySceneKey(){return _labelSceneKey();}
function _withRenderCtx(target,fn){const prev=ctx;ctx=target;try{return fn();}finally{ctx=prev;}}
function _baseSceneKey(){return [baseCanvas.width,baseCanvas.height,camX,camY,zoom,_worldBitmapGen,_mapContentSceneKey(),_tbSegsVersion,_staticBorderVersion,_alOverlayVersion,_allianceBorderSegVersion].join('|');}
function _mapContentSceneKey(){return [viewMode,showBorders?1:0,editorMode?1:0,playerCivId,_tbSegsVersion,_staticBorderVersion,_alOverlayVersion,_allianceBorderSegVersion].join('|');}
function _labelContentSceneKey(){return [viewMode,showLabels?1:0,showCities?1:0,_civLabelCacheVersion,viewMode==='alliances'?_allianceBorderSegVersion:0].join('|');}
function _mapViewSceneKey(){return [mapCanvas.width,mapCanvas.height,camX,camY,zoom,viewMode,showBorders?1:0,editorMode?1:0,playerCivId].join('|');}
function _mapSceneKey(){return [_mapViewSceneKey(),_tbSegsVersion,_staticBorderVersion,_alOverlayVersion,_allianceBorderSegVersion].join('|');}
function _labelSceneKey(){return [labelCanvas.width,labelCanvas.height,camX,camY,zoom,viewMode,showLabels?1:0,showCities?1:0,_civLabelCacheVersion,viewMode==='alliances'?_allianceBorderSegVersion:0].join('|');}
function _renderBaseLayer(force=false){
  const dirty=_worldDirty||_terrBorderDirty||(viewMode==='alliances'&&(_alOverlayDirty||_allianceBorderDirty||_allianceLabelDirty));
  const preKey=_baseSceneKey();if(!force&&_baseLayerValid&&!dirty&&preKey===_baseLayerKey)return false;
  _ensureWorldTex();if(_worldDirty)_rebuildWorldTex();
  const tpx=TS*zoom,srcW=baseCanvas.width/tpx,srcH=baseCanvas.height/tpx;
  baseCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);baseCtx.globalAlpha=1;baseCtx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';baseCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  baseCtx.drawImage(_worldTex,camX,camY,srcW,srcH,0,0,baseCanvas.width,baseCanvas.height);
  // Borders/territory/alliance tint are static relative to the map and belong behind
  // units, so paint them directly into the same opaque viewport canvas. This removes
  // an entire full-screen alpha-composited DOM canvas from every browser frame.
  _withRenderCtx(baseCtx,()=>{_drawStaticBaseLayers();});
  _baseLayerKey=_baseSceneKey();_baseLayerValid=true;
  _mapLayerKey=_mapSceneKey();_mapLayerViewKey=_mapViewSceneKey();_mapLayerContentKey=_mapContentSceneKey();_mapLayerCamX=camX;_mapLayerCamY=camY;_mapLayerZoom=zoom;_mapLayerValid=true;
  return true;
}
function _drawStaticBaseLayers(){
  drawAllianceOverlay();
  if(editorMode){const savedBorders=showBorders,savedZoom=zoom;showBorders=true;if(zoom<=0.30)zoom=0.31;drawBorderLines();zoom=savedZoom;showBorders=savedBorders;drawSeaZones();drawTerritoryBorders();}
  else{drawBorderLines();drawSeaZones();drawTerritoryBorders();}
}
function _renderMapLayer(force=false){
  // Compatibility stub: map geometry is now composited into baseCanvas by
  // _renderBaseLayer(), eliminating one full-screen transparent browser layer.
  if(mapCanvas.width!==1||mapCanvas.height!==1){mapCanvas.width=1;mapCanvas.height=1;}
  return false;
}
function _renderLabelLayer(force=false,enabled=true){
  if(!enabled||(!showLabels&&!showCities)){_resetLayerTransform(labelCanvas);if(_labelLayerValid||force)labelCtx.clearRect(0,0,labelCanvas.width,labelCanvas.height);_labelLayerValid=false;_labelLayerKey='';return false;}
  if((_civLabelDirty&&time-_civLabelLastBuild>=90)||!_civLabelCache){_buildCivLabelCache();_civLabelLastBuild=time;}
  const now=performance.now(),contentKey=_labelContentSceneKey(),cameraChanged=_labelLayerValid&&(_labelLayerCamX!==camX||_labelLayerCamY!==camY||_labelLayerZoom!==zoom);
  const zoomChanged=cameraChanged&&_labelLayerZoom!==zoom;
  if(!force&&_labelLayerValid&&contentKey===_labelLayerContentKey&&cameraChanged&&_cameraMotionActive()&&_cameraCompositorReady()){
    // Move the existing transparent label layer continuously with the camera between exact
    // redraws. The previous hold-and-snap path left labels stationary for ~34 ms, producing
    // the visible 'click' on every pan. CSS compositor transforms are smooth and allocation-free.
    _applyLayerCameraTransform(labelCanvas,_labelLayerCamX,_labelLayerCamY,_labelLayerZoom);
    if(now-_lastExactLabelCameraRender<_LABEL_CAMERA_COMMIT_MS)return false;
  }
  const key=_labelSceneKey();if(!force&&_labelLayerValid&&key===_labelLayerKey){_resetLayerTransform(labelCanvas);return false;}
  _resetLayerTransform(labelCanvas);labelCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);labelCtx.clearRect(0,0,labelCanvas.width,labelCanvas.height);_withRenderCtx(labelCtx,()=>{if(showLabels){drawLabels();drawSeaZoneLabels();}drawCities();});
  _labelLayerKey=_labelSceneKey();_labelLayerContentKey=contentKey;_labelLayerCamX=camX;_labelLayerCamY=camY;_labelLayerZoom=zoom;_labelLayerValid=true;_lastExactLabelCameraRender=now;return true;
}
function _drawOrReuseStaticBase(){_renderBaseLayer();return _renderMapLayer();}
function _drawOrReuseStaticOverlay(){return _renderLabelLayer(false,true);}

let _idleDynamicValid=false,_idleDynamicW=-1,_idleDynamicH=-1,_idleDynamicCamX=NaN,_idleDynamicCamY=NaN,_idleDynamicZoom=NaN,_idleDynamicBotPhase=-1,_idleDynamicSimPhase=-1,_idleDynamicEditor=-1,_idleDynamicFoxes=-1,_idleDynamicPanel=-9999,_idleDynamicProv=-9999,_idleDynamicHl=-1;
function _idleDynamicFrameUnchanged(){
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height,bp=_botAnimTick>>2,sp=_simTick>>2,ed=editorMode?1:0,fx=showFoxes?1:0,hl=_hlDirty?1:0;
  const same=_idleDynamicValid&&w===_idleDynamicW&&h===_idleDynamicH&&camX===_idleDynamicCamX&&camY===_idleDynamicCamY&&zoom===_idleDynamicZoom&&bp===_idleDynamicBotPhase&&sp===_idleDynamicSimPhase&&ed===_idleDynamicEditor&&fx===_idleDynamicFoxes&&panelCivId===_idleDynamicPanel&&selectedProv===_idleDynamicProv&&hl===_idleDynamicHl;
  _idleDynamicValid=true;_idleDynamicW=w;_idleDynamicH=h;_idleDynamicCamX=camX;_idleDynamicCamY=camY;_idleDynamicZoom=zoom;_idleDynamicBotPhase=bp;_idleDynamicSimPhase=sp;_idleDynamicEditor=ed;_idleDynamicFoxes=fx;_idleDynamicPanel=panelCivId;_idleDynamicProv=selectedProv;_idleDynamicHl=hl;return same;
}
function _idleDynamicSafe(){
  if(_boxSelecting||_divDragging||_defLineDragging||peaceConference||_giveLandMode||_warNoteTimer>0||particles.length)return false;
  if(_blockedIncomingByProv.size)return false;
  for(const civ of _getAliveCivs())if((civ._activeDivCount||0)||(civ._safeMoveNextDue||0)||(civ._damagedDivs?.size||0))return false;
  return true;
}
function draw(){
  _renderBaseLayer();
  _renderMapLayer();
  _renderLabelLayer(false,true);
