  // Stationary fox animation already changes only once every four RAFs. When absolutely
  // nothing gameplay-visible changes, keep the previous transparent gameplay canvas for the
  // three duplicate frames instead of clearing and rebuilding the exact same pixels.
  if(_idleDynamicSafe()){
    if(_idleDynamicFrameUnchanged())return;
  }else _idleDynamicValid=false;
  ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  if(editorMode){
    _edDrawHover();
  } else {
    drawSelectedHighlight();
    drawGiveLandOverlay();
    if(showBuildings)drawBldIcons();
    drawDivisions();
    drawCommittedRoutes();
    drawRoutePreview();
    drawDefenseLine();
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(-camX*TS*zoom,-camY*TS*zoom);ctx.scale(zoom,zoom);drawParticles();ctx.restore();
  }
  // Peace selection geometry belongs on the ordinary dynamic gameplay canvas. Keeping
  // cities/labels on label-c and treaty geometry on c prevents iOS/WebKit from promoting
  // a second full-screen treaty bitmap that can freeze while the camera continues moving.
  if(peaceConference) drawPeaceConferenceOverlay();

  // FX is now notification-only. It is never used as a duplicate map/city compositor.
  const fxNeeded=_warNoteTimer>0;
  if(fxNeeded||_fxHadContent){
    fxCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);fxCtx.clearRect(0,0,fxCanvas.width,fxCanvas.height);
    if(fxNeeded)_withRenderCtx(fxCtx,()=>{drawWarNotification();});
    _fxHadContent=fxNeeded;
  }
}

// ── GAME LOGIC ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ── RESOURCE TICK — all civs ───────────────────────────────────────
let _econAggDirty=true;
const _civEconAgg=[]; // civId -> {food,wood,stone,coins,barracks}
function _markEconomyAggregatesDirty(){_econAggDirty=true;_divCapCache=-1;for(const c of civs)if(c)c._econRateDirty=true;}
function _econAdjustProvince(cid,p,sign){
  if(_econAggDirty||cid<0||cid>=civs.length||!p||p.isSea)return;
  let a=_civEconAgg[cid];if(!a)a=_civEconAgg[cid]={food:0,wood:0,stone:0,coins:0,barracks:0};
  const b=p.bld;a.barracks+=sign*(b.barracks||0);civs[cid]._econRateDirty=true;
  for(const bname of ECON_BLDS){const lv=b[bname];if(!lv)continue;const def=BLDEFS[bname],mul=def.mul[lv];for(const [r,rate] of def._prodPairs)a[r]+=sign*rate*mul*.016;}
}
function _econOwnershipChanged(pid,prev,next){
  _divCapCache=-1;if(_econAggDirty)return;const p=provinces[pid];if(prev>=0)_econAdjustProvince(prev,p,-1);if(next>=0)_econAdjustProvince(next,p,1);
}
function _econBuildingChanged(pid,bname,oldLv,newLv){
  _divCapCache=-1;const cid=provOwner[pid];if(cid===playerCivId)_markUpgradeMetaDirty();if(_econAggDirty||oldLv===newLv)return;if(cid<0)return;let a=_civEconAgg[cid];if(!a)return;
  if(bname==='barracks'){a.barracks+=(newLv-oldLv);return;}
  civs[cid]._econRateDirty=true;
  if(!ECON_BLDS.includes(bname))return;const def=BLDEFS[bname];
  const oldMul=oldLv?def.mul[oldLv]:0,newMul=newLv?def.mul[newLv]:0,delta=newMul-oldMul;
  for(const [r,rate] of def._prodPairs)a[r]+=rate*delta*.016;
}
function _rebuildEconomyAggregates(){
  const n=civs.length;_civEconAgg.length=n;
  for(let cid=0;cid<n;cid++){let a=_civEconAgg[cid];if(!a)a=_civEconAgg[cid]={food:0,wood:0,stone:0,coins:0,barracks:0};else{a.food=0;a.wood=0;a.stone=0;a.coins=0;a.barracks=0;}}
  for(let pid=0;pid<provinces.length;pid++){
    const cid=provOwner[pid];if(cid<0||cid>=n)continue;const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||p.isSea)continue;const a=_civEconAgg[cid],b=p.bld;
    a.barracks+=(b.barracks||0);
    for(const bname of ECON_BLDS){const lv=b[bname];if(!lv)continue;const def=BLDEFS[bname],mul=def.mul[lv];for(const [r,rate] of def._prodPairs)a[r]+=rate*mul*.016;}
  }
  _econAggDirty=false;for(const c of civs)if(c)c._econRateDirty=true;
}
function civFoxCap(civ){
  if(_econAggDirty)_rebuildEconomyAggregates();
  let cap=8+((_civEconAgg[civ.id]?.barracks||0)*5);
  if(civ.id===playerCivId) cap += (techBonus.capBonus || 0);
  else if(civ.techBonus) cap += (civ.techBonus.capBonus || 0);
  return cap;
}
// Current division objects always carry divCount=1; co-located grouping is render-only,
// so the exact existing upkeep/cap result is simply the live fox-object count. Avoid reducing
// the entire army on every resource tick.
function civFoxDivCount(civ){return civ.foxes.length;}
function _damagedSet(civ){return civ._damagedDivs||(civ._damagedDivs=new Set());}
function _syncDamagedDiv(div){
  
  const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(!civ||!div)return;const set=_damagedSet(civ),should=div.hp>0&&div.hp<div.maxHp;
  if(should){if(!set.has(div)){set.add(div);civ._damagedOrderDirty=true;}}else if(set.delete(div))civ._damagedOrderDirty=true;
}
function _untrackDamagedDiv(div){const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(civ?._damagedDivs?.delete(div))civ._damagedOrderDirty=true;}
function _orderedDamaged(civ){
  const set=civ._damagedDivs;if(!set?.size)return null;
  let a=civ._damagedOrdered;if(!a)a=civ._damagedOrdered=[];
  if(civ._damagedOrderDirty||a.length!==set.size){a.length=0;for(const d of set)a.push(d);a.sort((x,y)=>x._foxOrder-y._foxOrder);civ._damagedOrderDirty=false;}
  return a;}

function tickResources(){
  if(_econAggDirty)_rebuildEconomyAggregates();
  for(const civ of _getAliveCivs()){
    const isPlayer = (civ.id === playerCivId);
    const a=_civEconAgg[civ.id];
    // Buildings/ownership/technology change rarely compared with resource ticks. Cache the
    // exact four post-tech production rates at those events instead of recomputing eight
    // multipliers plus branches for every living country on every simulation tick.
    if(a){
      let rate=civ._econFinalRate;
      if(civ._econRateDirty||!rate){
        if(!rate)rate=civ._econFinalRate={food:0,wood:0,stone:0,coins:0};
        const b=isPlayer?techBonus:(civ.techBonus||null),all=b?(b.allMul||0):0;
        // Universal anti-softlock floor: every living nation always produces at least
        // 1 unit/second of EACH core resource (simulation baseline is ~60 ticks/sec).
        const floorPerTick=1/60;
        rate.food=Math.max(floorPerTick,a.food*(1+(b?.foodMul||0)+all));rate.wood=Math.max(floorPerTick,a.wood*(1+(b?.woodMul||0)+all));
        rate.stone=Math.max(floorPerTick,a.stone*(1+(b?.stoneMul||0)+all));rate.coins=Math.max(floorPerTick,a.coins*(1+(b?.coinsMul||0)+all));
        civ._econRateDirty=false;
      }
      civ.res.food+=rate.food;civ.res.wood+=rate.wood;civ.res.stone+=rate.stone;civ.res.coins+=rate.coins;
    }
    civ.res.food=Math.max(0,civ.res.food-civFoxDivCount(civ)*.008);

    // ── Division healing via food ─────────────────────────────────────────
    // Combat/load/grouping keep a sparse damaged-unit Set, so peaceful full-health armies
    // cost O(1) here instead of being rescanned every simulation tick.
    const damaged=_orderedDamaged(civ);
    if(damaged && civ.res.food > 0.5){
      for(const div of damaged){
        if(div.hp<=0||div.hp>=div.maxHp){_untrackDamagedDiv(div);continue;}
        if(civ.res.food <= 0.5) break;
        const moving=div.path.length>0,inCombat=div.combatCooldown>0;
        const healRate=inCombat?0:(moving?0.02:0.05);if(healRate<=0)continue;
        const healAmt=Math.min(healRate,div.maxHp-div.hp),foodCost=healAmt*0.04;
        if(civ.res.food<foodCost)break;div.hp+=healAmt;civ.res.food-=foodCost;
        if(div.hp>=div.maxHp)_untrackDamagedDiv(div);
      }
    }
  }
  tickTreatyEconomy();
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// BOT AI — independent decision-making for every non-player civ
// Three behaviour layers evaluated on staggered per-civ timers:
//   1. Economy  — build/upgrade buildings based on terrain yield
//   2. Expansion — claim best adjacent province
//   3. Military  — barracks/fort investment based on neighbour threat
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Strategic land-border cache. Ownership changes invalidate only the civs touching
// the changed province, so bot planning never rescans an entire country unless required.
let _botThreatMarks=new Uint32Array(0),_botThreatStamp=1;
function _markBotStrategyCivDirty(cid){const c=civs[cid];if(!c)return;c._botBorderDirty=true;c._armyPlanDirty=true;c._botTacticalDirty=true;}
function _markBotStrategyAround(pid,prev=-1,next=-1){
  if(prev>=0)_markBotStrategyCivDirty(prev);if(next>=0)_markBotStrategyCivDirty(next);
  for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const o=provOwner[_adjList[ai]];if(o>=0)_markBotStrategyCivDirty(o);}
}
function _getBotLandBorders(civ){
  if(!civ)return [];if(!civ._botBorderDirty&&civ._botBorderCache)return civ._botBorderCache;
  let out=civ._botBorderCache;if(!out)out=civ._botBorderCache=[];else out.length=0;
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||p.isSea)continue;for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const n=_adjList[ai],np=provinces[n];if(np&&!np.isSea&&provOwner[n]!==civ.id){out.push(pid);break;}}}
  civ._botBorderDirty=false;return out;
}
function _markAllBotStrategyDirty(){for(const c of civs){c._botBorderDirty=true;c._armyPlanDirty=true;c._botTacticalDirty=true;}}

// Province "value score" — higher = bot wants it more
function botProvScore(pid){
  const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p) return 0;
  // Sum best possible output across all econ buildings
  let score=ECON_BLDS.length; // flat value per province
  // Bonus for larger provinces (more tiles = more building slots conceptually)
  score+=p.tiles.length*0.002;
  return score;
}

// Threat level: count how many *different* enemy civs border this civ
function botThreatLevel(civ){
  const borders=_getBotLandBorders(civ),n=civs.length;if(_botThreatMarks.length<n)_botThreatMarks=new Uint32Array(Math.max(n,_botThreatMarks.length*2||256));
  let stamp=(++_botThreatStamp)>>>0;if(stamp===0){_botThreatMarks.fill(0);_botThreatStamp=stamp=1;}let count=0;
  for(const pid of borders)for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const o=provOwner[_adjList[ai]];if(o>=0&&o!==civ.id&&_botThreatMarks[o]!==stamp){_botThreatMarks[o]=stamp;count++;}}
  return count;
}

// Pick best adjacent unclaimed (or weaker-neighbour) province
function botPickExpansionTarget(civ){
  let best=null,bestScore=-Infinity;
  for(const pid of _getBotLandBorders(civ))for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const n=_adjList[ai];if(provOwner[n]>=0||provinces[n]?.isSea)continue;const sc=botProvScore(n);if(sc>bestScore){bestScore=sc;best=n;}}
  return best;
}

// Cost check helper
function canAfford(res, cost){ return res.wood>=cost.w && res.stone>=cost.s; }

const _BOT_ECON_SCORE=Object.create(null);
function _ensureBotEconScores(){
  if(_BOT_ECON_SCORE._ready)return;const pri={food:1.4,wood:1.1,stone:1.0,coins:0.9};
  for(const bname of ECON_BLDS){const def=BLDEFS[bname],arr=_BOT_ECON_SCORE[bname]=new Float64Array(3);for(let lv=0;lv<3;lv++){let sc=0,nextMul=def.mul[lv+1];for(const [r,rate] of def._prodPairs)sc+=rate*nextMul*(pri[r]||1);if(lv>0)sc*=1.3;arr[lv]=sc;}}
  _BOT_ECON_SCORE._ready=true;
}
// Economy decision: find the single best build/upgrade action for this civ
function botEconAction(civ){
  const r=civ.res;_ensureBotEconScores();
  let bestProv=null, bestBld=null, bestScore=-Infinity;

  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){
    const p=provinces[pid];

    for(const bname of ECON_BLDS){
      const lv=p.bld[bname]; if(lv>=3) continue;
      const cost=BLDEFS[bname].costs[lv];
      if(!canAfford(r,cost)) continue;
      const sc=_BOT_ECON_SCORE[bname][lv];
      if(sc>bestScore){bestScore=sc;bestProv=pid;bestBld=bname;}
    }
  }
  if(bestProv!==null){
    const p=provinces[bestProv];
    const lv=p.bld[bestBld];
    const cost=BLDEFS[bestBld].costs[lv];
    civ.res.wood-=cost.w; civ.res.stone-=cost.s;
    p.bld[bestBld]++;_econBuildingChanged(bestProv,bestBld,lv,lv+1);
  }
}

// Military decision: invest in barracks/forts based on threat
function botMilAction(civ){
  const r=civ.res;
  const threat=botThreatLevel(civ);
  if(threat===0) return; // peaceful, skip military
  // Find border province with lowest fort level using the ownership-event cache.
  let target=null, lowestFort=99;
  for(const pid of _getBotLandBorders(civ)){
    const p=provinces[pid];
    if(p.bld.fort<lowestFort&&p.bld.fort<3){
      const cost=BLDEFS.fort.costs[p.bld.fort];
      if(canAfford(r,cost)){lowestFort=p.bld.fort;target=pid;}
    }
  }
  if(target!==null&&threat>=2){
    const p=provinces[target];
    const cost=BLDEFS.fort.costs[p.bld.fort];
    civ.res.wood-=cost.w; civ.res.stone-=cost.s;
    {const old=p.bld.fort;p.bld.fort++;_econBuildingChanged(target,'fort',old,old+1);}
  }
  // Barracks in capital if low
  if(civ.ownedProvs.size>0){
    const capPid=_capitalProvinceForCiv(civ.id);
    const cp=capPid>=0?provinces[capPid]:null;
    if(!cp)return;
    if(cp.bld.barracks<2&&civ.ownedProvs.size>3){
      const cost=BLDEFS.barracks.costs[cp.bld.barracks];
      if(canAfford(r,cost)){
        civ.res.wood-=cost.w; civ.res.stone-=cost.s;
        {const old=cp.bld.barracks;cp.bld.barracks++;_econBuildingChanged(capPid,'barracks',old,old+1);}
      }
    }
  }
}

// ── BOT TECH SYSTEM ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Each AI civ gets its own techs Set and a cached techBonus object.
// _recomputeBotTechBonus mirrors _recomputeTechBonuses but for one civ.
function _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ){
  if(!civ.techs) civ.techs = new Set();
  if(!civ.techBonus) civ.techBonus = {
    foodMul:0, woodMul:0, stoneMul:0, coinsMul:0, allMul:0,
    atkMul:0,  defMul:0,  movMul:0,   dmgReduce:0,
    capBonus:0, fortBonus:0
  };
  const b = civ.techBonus;
  for(const k in b) b[k] = 0;
  for(const tree of Object.values(TECH_TREES)){
    for(const row of tree.rows){
      for(const t of row.techs){
        if(!civ.techs.has(t.id)) continue;
        for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(t.bonus||{})){
          b[k] = (b[k]||0) + v;
        }
      }
    }
  }  civ._moveMul=1+(b.movMul||0);civ._econRateDirty=true;
}

// Bot tech catalog is immutable; flatten it once instead of re-walking nested tree objects
// and recomputing weighted scores for every AI research pulse.
let _BOT_TECH_CATALOG=null;
function _ensureBotTechCatalog(){
  if(_BOT_TECH_CATALOG)return _BOT_TECH_CATALOG;
  const weights={atkMul:3.0,defMul:2.5,dmgReduce:2.5,movMul:2.0,capBonus:2.0,fortBonus:1.5,foodMul:1.0,woodMul:1.0,stoneMul:1.0,coinsMul:1.0,allMul:1.5};
  const out=[];
  for(const tree of Object.values(TECH_TREES))for(const row of tree.rows)for(const t of row.techs){let score=0;for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(t.bonus||{}))score+=v*(weights[k]||1);score/=Math.max(1,t.cost*.001);out.push({id:t.id,cost:t.cost,requires:row.requires||null,score});}
  return _BOT_TECH_CATALOG=out;
}
// Bot tech research — called every 300 frames per civ.
// Picks the cheapest unlocked tech the bot can afford (in coins).
// Prioritises attack > defence > movement (bots are war-focused).
function botTechAction(civ){
  if(!civ.techs)civ.techs=new Set();if(!civ.techBonus)_recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
  const catalog=_ensureBotTechCatalog();let researched=false,safety=0;
  while(safety++<20){
    let best=null,bestScore=-Infinity;
    for(const e of catalog){
      if(civ.techs.has(e.id)||e.cost>civ.res.coins)continue;
      if(e.requires){let ok=true;for(const id of e.requires)if(!civ.techs.has(id)){ok=false;break;}if(!ok)continue;}
      if(e.score>bestScore){bestScore=e.score;best=e;}
    }
    if(!best)break;civ.res.coins-=best.cost;civ.techs.add(best.id);researched=true;
  }
  if(researched){
    _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
    // Bot research only changes this civilization's armour/boot tier. Sprite keys already
    // include those tiers, so flushing every country's raster/bitmap/GL cache here caused
    // a world-wide graphics rebuild for a local tech event. Invalidate only this civ's
    // tier lookup and the composed bot layer; unchanged sprite variants stay reusable.
    civ._foxTierVersion=0;
    if(typeof _invalidateBotSpriteLayers==='function')_invalidateBotSpriteLayers();
  }
}

// Per-civ bot tick counter — stagger evaluation so not all civs run same frame
const botPhase=[];
function initBotPhases(){
  for(let i=0;i<civs.length;i++) botPhase[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*120);
}

// Expansion rate: how many ticks between land grabs (larger civs expand slower)
function botExpRate(civ){const own=civ.ownedProvs.size,key=botDifficulty;if(civ._botExpOwn===own&&civ._botExpDiff===key)return civ._botExpCache;const v=Math.max(50,Math.round(Math.max(120,200-own*2)*_botDiff().think));civ._botExpOwn=own;civ._botExpDiff=key;civ._botExpCache=v;return v;}

const _botWarEnemies=[],_botWarEnemySig=[];
let _botAtWar=new Uint8Array(0),_botWarCacheDirty=true;
let _aliveCivList=[],_botAliveList=[],_botAliveListDirty=true;
// Ownership changes are comparatively rare. Rebuild the compact living-country lists only
// on those events instead of making economy/AI scan every dead country every logical tick.
function _rebuildAliveCivLists(){_aliveCivList.length=0;_botAliveList.length=0;for(const c of civs)if(c.ownedProvs.size){_aliveCivList.push(c);if(c.id!==playerCivId)_botAliveList.push(c);}_botAliveListDirty=false;}
function _getAliveCivs(){if(_botAliveListDirty)_rebuildAliveCivLists();return _aliveCivList;}
function _getAliveBotCivs(){if(_botAliveListDirty)_rebuildAliveCivLists();return _botAliveList;}
function _buildBotWarEnemies(){
  if(!_botWarCacheDirty)return _botWarEnemies;
  _botWarCacheDirty=false;
  const n=civs.length;
  if(_botAtWar.length<n)_botAtWar=new Uint8Array(n); else _botAtWar.fill(0);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let a=_botWarEnemies[i];if(!a)a=_botWarEnemies[i]=[];else a.length=0;}
  for(const key of warPairs){
    const a=(key/10000)|0,b=key%10000;
    if(a<0||a>=n||b<0||b>=n)continue;
    // Preserve the original semantics exactly: any extant war pair suppresses
    // neutral expansion/spawn-idle logic, but movement AI only targets opponents
    // that still own land.
    _botAtWar[a]=1;_botAtWar[b]=1;
    if(civs[b]&&civs[b].ownedProvs.size)_botWarEnemies[a].push(b);
    if(civs[a]&&civs[a].ownedProvs.size)_botWarEnemies[b].push(a);
  }
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)_botWarEnemySig[i]=_botWarEnemies[i].length?_botWarEnemies[i].join(','):'';
  return _botWarEnemies;
}
let _botCadenceKey='',_botCadence=null;
function _getBotCadence(diff){
  const key=botDifficulty;
  if(_botCadenceKey!==key||!_botCadence){
    _botCadenceKey=key;
    _botCadence={
      think:Math.max(1,Math.round(90*diff.think)),
      milThink:Math.max(40,Math.round(200*diff.think)),
      spawnPeace:Math.max(24,Math.round(350*diff.spawn)),
      spawnWar:Math.max(24,Math.round(80*diff.spawn))
    };
  }
  return _botCadence;
}
function tickBots(){
  if(!gameReady) return;
  const warEnemiesByCiv=_buildBotWarEnemies(),diff=_botDiff(),cad=_getBotCadence(diff);
  const think=cad.think,milThink=cad.milThink,spawnPeace=cad.spawnPeace,spawnWar=cad.spawnWar;

  // ── INSANELY RARE RANDOM WARS ─────────────────────────────────────
  // Preserve the exact candidate ordering and two post-trigger RNG draws, but select by
  // rank instead of allocating/filtering two temporary country arrays on every event.
  if(Math.random() < 0.0000139*diff.war){
    const alive=_getAliveCivs();
    if(alive.length>=2){
      let aggressorCount=0;for(let i=0;i<alive.length;i++)if(alive[i].id!==playerCivId)aggressorCount++;
      if(aggressorCount>0){
        let rank=Math.floor(Math.random()*aggressorCount),attacker=null;
        for(let i=0;i<alive.length;i++){const c=alive[i];if(c.id===playerCivId)continue;if(rank--===0){attacker=c;break;}}
        let targetCount=0;for(let i=0;i<alive.length;i++){const c=alive[i];if(c.id!==attacker.id&&!areAtWar(attacker.id,c.id))targetCount++;}
        if(targetCount>0){
          rank=Math.floor(Math.random()*targetCount);let defender=null;
          for(let i=0;i<alive.length;i++){const c=alive[i];if(c.id===attacker.id||areAtWar(attacker.id,c.id))continue;if(rank--===0){defender=c;break;}}
          if(defender)declareWar(attacker.id,defender.id);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for(const civ of _getAliveBotCivs()){

    // Ensure every bot has its tech structures initialised
    if(!civ.techs) civ.techs = new Set();
    if(!civ.techBonus) _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);

    botPhase[civ.id]=(botPhase[civ.id]||0)+1;
    const ph=botPhase[civ.id];

    // Economy cadence scales with difficulty; Easy exactly preserves the old 90-frame rate.
    if(ph%think===0) botEconAction(civ);

    // Tech research: per-civ cooldown counter, guaranteed to fire every 60-120 frames
    if(civ._techCooldown === undefined) civ._techCooldown = (civ.id % 60);
    civ._techCooldown--;
    if(civ._techCooldown <= 0){
      botTechAction(civ);
      civ._techCooldown = Math.max(24,Math.round((60 + (civ.id % 61))*diff.think)); // harder bots research more often
    }

    // Expansion (neutral land grabs): only when not at war.
    const enemies=warEnemiesByCiv[civ.id]||[];
    const atWarWithAnyone=_botAtWar[civ.id]===1;

    if(!atWarWithAnyone && ph%botExpRate(civ)===0){
      if(civ.res.food>30&&civ.res.wood>20){
        const target=botPickExpansionTarget(civ);
        if(target!==null) civClaimProvince(target,civ.id);
      }
    }

    // Military investment cadence scales with difficulty.
    if(ph%milThink===1) botMilAction(civ);

    // Division spawning
    botSpawnDivisions(civ,atWarWithAnyone,atWarWithAnyone?spawnWar:spawnPeace);

    // Complete event-driven own-territory arrivals at the same stage where ordinary
    // division movement has always run (after economy/tech/expansion/military/spawn).
    _processSafeMoves(civ);
    // Division movement & AI: skip the entire country when there is literally no
    // moving army and no strategic/tactical event due this logical tick.
    const activeDivs=civ._activeDivs||_ensureActiveDivSet(civ);
    const planDue=!civ._armyPlanCache||civ._armyPlanDirty||_simTick>=(civ._armyPlanNextTick||0);
    const tacticalDue=enemies.length>0&&(civ._botTacticalDirty||_simTick>=(civ._botNextTacticalTick||0));
    if((civ._activeDivCount||0)||civ._botGroupDirty||civ._botRescueDirty||civ._botRescueInit!==true||planDue||tacticalDue) botTickDivisions(civ,enemies,activeDivs);
  }
}


// Pre-cache HUD element references to avoid repeated getElementById calls
const _hud={};
function _initHudRefs(){
  _hud.wood  =document.getElementById('h-wood');
  _hud.stone =document.getElementById('h-stone');
  _hud.food  =document.getElementById('h-food');
  _hud.coins =document.getElementById('h-coins');
  _hud.pop   =document.getElementById('h-pop');
  _hud.land  =document.getElementById('h-land');
}
let _hudTick=0;
const _hudLast={wood:null,stone:null,food:null,coins:null,pop:null,land:null};
function _hudSet(el,key,v){if(_hudLast[key]===v)return;_hudLast[key]=v;if(el)el.textContent=v;}
function updateHUD(){
  if(++_hudTick<10) return; // retain the existing ~6fps HUD cadence, but write only changed nodes
  _hudTick=0;
  if(!_hud.wood) _initHudRefs();
  if(playerCivId>=0){
    const r=civs[playerCivId].res,c=civs[playerCivId];
    _hudSet(_hud.wood,'wood',Math.floor(r.wood));_hudSet(_hud.stone,'stone',Math.floor(r.stone));
    _hudSet(_hud.food,'food',Math.floor(r.food));_hudSet(_hud.coins,'coins',Math.floor(r.coins));
    _hudSet(_hud.pop,'pop',c.foxes.length);_hudSet(_hud.land,'land',c.ownedProvs.size);
  } else {
    _hudSet(_hud.pop,'pop','—');_hudSet(_hud.land,'land',civs.length+' civs');
  }
}

// ── PROVINCE PANEL ────────────────────────────────────────────────
function buildRateStr(def,lv){
  if(!def.prod)return '';
  const mul=def.mul[lv];
  const icons={food:'🌾',wood:'🪵',stone:'🪨',coins:'🪙'};
  return Object.entries(def.prod).map(([r,rate])=>{
    const actual=(rate*mul).toFixed(1);
    return `${icons[r]} ${actual}/min`;
  }).join(' ');
}

function buildEffectStr(def,lv){if(!def.vals)return '';return def.note.replace('{v}',def.vals[lv]);}

function renderBldRow(bname,lv,canEdit,pid){
  const def=BLDEFS[bname];
  const atMax=lv>=3;
  const cost=atMax?null:def.costs[lv];
  const pr=playerCivId>=0?civs[playerCivId].res:_emptyRes;
  const canUp=!atMax&&canEdit&&cost&&pr.wood>=cost.w&&pr.stone>=cost.s;
  const rateStr=lv>0?(def.prod?buildRateStr(def,lv):buildEffectStr(def,lv)):'Not built';
  const lvDots=[0,1,2].map(i=>`<span class="pp-lv${i<lv?' on':''}"></span>`).join('');
  const upgLabel=atMax
    ?`<span class="pp-maxlv">MAX</span>`
    :`<button class="pp-upg" ${!canUp?'disabled':''} data-pid="${pid}" data-bld="${bname}">Lv${lv+1}<br><small>${cost?cost.w+'W '+cost.s+'S':''}</small></button>`;
  return `<div class="pp-bld"><span class="pp-bic">${def.icon}</span><div class="pp-binfo"><div class="pp-bname">${def.name}</div><div class="pp-brate">${rateStr}</div></div><div class="pp-bright"><div class="pp-lvs">${lvDots}</div>${upgLabel}</div></div>`;
}

// ── RECRUIT TAB — tier picker + slider + max button ───────────────
// State stored on the element between refreshes
function refreshRecruitTab(pid, partialUpdate){
  const el=document.getElementById('pp-recruit'); if(!el) return;
  if(playerCivId<0||provOwner[pid]!==playerCivId){
    el.innerHTML='<div class="pp-locked">Can only recruit in owned provinces</div>';
    return;
  }

  const r=civs[playerCivId].res;
  const cap=civFoxCap(civs[playerCivId]);
  const cur=civs[playerCivId].foxes.length; // player divisions are always individual objects
  const room=Math.max(0,cap-cur);

  // Reset per-province state
  if(el._rcPid!==pid){ el._rcTier=0; el._rcPid=pid; el._rcBuilt=false; }
  const tierIdx=el._rcTier||0;
  const tier=DIV_TIERS[tierIdx];
  const sliderMax=100;
  const count=Math.max(1,Math.min(sliderMax,el._rcCount||1));
  el._rcCount=count;

  const RES_ICONS={food:'🌾',wood:'🪵',stone:'🪨',coins:'🪙'};

  // Cost for N divisions of selected tier
  function totalCost(n){
    return {food:tier.cost.food*n,wood:tier.cost.wood*n,stone:tier.cost.stone*n,coins:tier.cost.coins*n};
  }

  // Max affordable & within cap
  function maxAffordable(){
    if(room<=0) return 0;
    for(let n=room;n>=1;n--){
      const c=totalCost(n);
      if(r.food>=c.food&&r.wood>=c.wood&&r.stone>=c.stone&&r.coins>=c.coins) return n;
    }
    return 0;
  }

  const effectiveCount=Math.min(count,room,100);
  const cost=totalCost(effectiveCount);
  const maxAff=maxAffordable();
  const canAffordThis=room>0&&r.food>=cost.food&&r.wood>=cost.wood&&r.stone>=cost.stone&&r.coins>=cost.coins&&effectiveCount>0;

  // ── Partial update: slider moved — only patch labels/costs/buttons ──
  if(partialUpdate && el._rcBuilt){
    const countEl=el.querySelector('.rc-count');
    if(countEl) countEl.textContent=effectiveCount;
    const strip=el.querySelector('.rc-cost-strip');
    if(strip) strip.innerHTML=['food','wood','stone','coins'].map(res=>
      `<div class="rc-cost-item ${r[res]>=cost[res]?'ok':'no'}">${RES_ICONS[res]} ${cost[res]}</div>`
    ).join('');
    const goB=el.querySelector('#rc-go');
    if(goB){
      goB.disabled=!canAffordThis||room<=0;
      goB.textContent=room<=0?'— AT CAP —':`▶ RECRUIT ${effectiveCount} DIVISION${effectiveCount>1?'S':''}`;
    }
    const mxB=el.querySelector('#rc-max');
    if(mxB){
      mxB.disabled=maxAff<=0;
      mxB.textContent=`⚡ MAX RECRUIT${maxAff>0?' ('+maxAff+')':''}`;
    }
    return; // done — slider element untouched
  }

  // ── Full render (first open, tier change, after recruit) ──
  el._rcBuilt=true;

  const tierBtns=DIV_TIERS.length>1?`<div class="rc-tier-row">${DIV_TIERS.map((t,i)=>`
    <div class="rc-tier${i===tierIdx?' sel':''}" data-tier="${i}">
      <span class="rc-icon">${t.icon}</span>
      <span class="rc-name">${t.name.replace(' Infantry','').replace(' Division','')}</span>
    </div>`).join('')}</div>`:'';

  const activeTierHdr=`<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:4px 2px 4px"><span style="font-size:18px">${tier.icon}</span><div><div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:10px;color:#e0c080;letter-spacing:.5px">${tier.name}</div><div style="font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:10px;color:#8a7050">${tier.desc}</div></div></div>`;

  const costItems=['food','wood','stone','coins'].map(res=>
    `<div class="rc-cost-item ${r[res]>=cost[res]?'ok':'no'}">${RES_ICONS[res]} ${cost[res]}</div>`
  ).join('');

  el.innerHTML=`
    <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:9px;color:#a08040;letter-spacing:1.5px;padding:6px 2px 6px">DIVISIONS: ${cur} / ${cap}${room<=0?' — AT CAP':''}</div>
    <div style="font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-size:10px;color:#8a7050;padding:0 1px 4px;font-style:italic">Recruits will spawn across your nation's provinces</div>
    ${activeTierHdr}
    ${tierBtns}
    <div class="rc-slider-wrap">
      <div class="rc-slider-label">
        <span>Recruit:</span>
        <span class="rc-count">${effectiveCount}</span>
      </div>
      <input type="range" class="rc-slider" id="rc-slider" min="1" max="100" value="${count}" ${room<=0?'disabled':''}>
    </div>
    <div class="rc-cost-strip">${costItems}</div>
    <button class="rc-recruit-btn" id="rc-go" ${(!canAffordThis||room<=0)?'disabled':''}>
      ${room<=0?'— AT CAP —':`▶ RECRUIT ${effectiveCount} DIVISION${effectiveCount>1?'S':''}`}
    </button>
    <button class="rc-max-btn" id="rc-max" ${(maxAff<=0)?'disabled':''}>
      ⚡ MAX RECRUIT${maxAff>0?' ('+maxAff+')':''}
    </button>
    ${room<=0?'<div class="pp-locked" style="margin-top:8px">Build Barracks to raise the cap</div>':''}
  `;

  // Wire tier selector
  el.querySelectorAll('.rc-tier').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      e.stopPropagation();
      el._rcTier=parseInt(btn.dataset.tier);
      el._rcBuilt=false; // force full re-render
      refreshRecruitTab(pid);
    });
  });

  // Wire slider — partial update only so the <input> is never destroyed mid-drag
  const slider=el.querySelector('#rc-slider');
  if(slider){
    slider.addEventListener('input',e=>{
      e.stopPropagation();
      el._rcCount=parseInt(slider.value);
      refreshRecruitTab(pid, true); // partial: update labels only, keep slider alive
    });
  }

  // Wire recruit button — spawns in a random province still owned by player
  const goBtn=el.querySelector('#rc-go');
  if(goBtn) goBtn.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    // Re-derive everything live so slider value is always honoured
    const liveTierIdx  = el._rcTier || 0;
    const liveTier     = DIV_TIERS[liveTierIdx];
    const liveCap      = civFoxCap(civs[playerCivId]);
    const liveCur      = civs[playerCivId].foxes.length;
    const liveRoom     = Math.max(0, liveCap - liveCur);
    const liveCount    = Math.max(1, Math.min(liveRoom, 100, el._rcCount || 1));
    const liveCost     = {
      food:  liveTier.cost.food  * liveCount,
      wood:  liveTier.cost.wood  * liveCount,
      stone: liveTier.cost.stone * liveCount,
      coins: liveTier.cost.coins * liveCount
    };
    const liveR        = civs[playerCivId].res;
    const liveCanAfford= liveRoom > 0
      && liveR.food  >= liveCost.food
      && liveR.wood  >= liveCost.wood
      && liveR.stone >= liveCost.stone
      && liveR.coins >= liveCost.coins
      && liveCount   > 0;
    if(!liveCanAfford || liveRoom <= 0) return;
    const n = Math.min(liveCount, liveRoom);
    liveR.food  -= liveCost.food;
    liveR.wood  -= liveCost.wood;
    liveR.stone -= liveCost.stone;
    liveR.coins -= liveCost.coins;
    // Pick a random owned province to spawn in
    const ownedArr = Array.from(civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const spawnPid = ownedArr[Math.floor(Math.random()*ownedArr.length)];
      const d=new Division(playerCivId, spawnPid);
      d.size=liveTier.size; d.divCount=1;
      civs[playerCivId].foxes.push(d);_indexAddDiv(d);
      if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);
    }
    if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)tickPlayerArmyPlans(true);
    const spawnPid2 = ownedArr[Math.floor(Math.random()*ownedArr.length)];
    burst(tcx(provinces[spawnPid2].centerTx),tcy(provinces[spawnPid2].centerTy),'#f0c060');
    AudioEngine.recruit(n);
    el._rcBuilt=false;
    refreshRecruitTab(pid);
  });

  // Wire max button — same live-read pattern, spawns in random provinces
  const maxBtn=el.querySelector('#rc-max');
  if(maxBtn) maxBtn.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const liveTierIdx  = el._rcTier || 0;
    const liveTier     = DIV_TIERS[liveTierIdx];
    const liveCap      = civFoxCap(civs[playerCivId]);
    const liveCur      = civs[playerCivId].foxes.length;
    const liveRoom     = Math.max(0, liveCap - liveCur);
    const liveR        = civs[playerCivId].res;
    // Find max affordable
    let n = 0;
    for(let k=liveRoom; k>=1; k--){
      if(liveR.food  >= liveTier.cost.food  * k
      && liveR.wood  >= liveTier.cost.wood  * k
      && liveR.stone >= liveTier.cost.stone * k
      && liveR.coins >= liveTier.cost.coins * k){ n=k; break; }
    }
    if(n <= 0) return;
    liveR.food  -= liveTier.cost.food  * n;
    liveR.wood  -= liveTier.cost.wood  * n;
    liveR.stone -= liveTier.cost.stone * n;
    liveR.coins -= liveTier.cost.coins * n;
    // Spawn each division in a random owned province
    const ownedArr2 = Array.from(civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const spawnPid = ownedArr2[Math.floor(Math.random()*ownedArr2.length)];
      const d=new Division(playerCivId, spawnPid);
      d.size=liveTier.size; d.divCount=1;
      civs[playerCivId].foxes.push(d);_indexAddDiv(d);
      if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);
    }
    if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)tickPlayerArmyPlans(true);
    const spawnPid2 = ownedArr2[Math.floor(Math.random()*ownedArr2.length)];
    burst(tcx(provinces[spawnPid2].centerTx),tcy(provinces[spawnPid2].centerTy),'#f0c060');
    AudioEngine.recruit(n);
    el._rcBuilt=false;
    refreshRecruitTab(pid);
  });
}

function _totalCost(tier,count){
  return {food:tier.cost.food*count,wood:tier.cost.wood*count,stone:tier.cost.stone*count,coins:tier.cost.coins*count};
}
function recruitDivisions(pid,tierIdx,count){
  if(playerCivId<0) return;
  const tier=DIV_TIERS[tierIdx]; if(!tier) return;
  const r=civs[playerCivId].res;
  const tot=_totalCost(tier,count);
  if(r.food<tot.food||r.wood<tot.wood||r.stone<tot.stone||r.coins<tot.coins) return;
  const cap=civFoxCap(civs[playerCivId]);
  const room=cap-civs[playerCivId].foxes.length;
  const actual=Math.min(count,room); if(actual<=0) return;
  const actCost=_totalCost(tier,actual);
  r.food-=actCost.food; r.wood-=actCost.wood; r.stone-=actCost.stone; r.coins-=actCost.coins;
  for(let i=0;i<actual;i++){const d=new Division(playerCivId,pid);d.size=tier.size;civs[playerCivId].foxes.push(d);_indexAddDiv(d);if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);}
  if(playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)tickPlayerArmyPlans(true);
  burst(tcx(provinces[pid].centerTx),tcy(provinces[pid].centerTy),'#f0c060');
  refreshRecruitTab(pid);
}

function recruitDivision(pid,tierIdx){recruitDivisions(pid,tierIdx,1);}

function openPanel(pid){
  selectedProv=pid;
  const panel=document.getElementById('pp');
  const p=provinces[pid];
  const ownerCivId=provOwner?provOwner[pid]:-1;
  const isPlayerOwned=ownerCivId>=0&&ownerCivId===playerCivId;
  const isOtherCiv=ownerCivId>=0&&ownerCivId!==playerCivId;
  const alreadyAtWar=isOtherCiv&&playerCivId>=0&&areAtWar(playerCivId,ownerCivId);

  // Set selected civ for country highlight
  if(ownerCivId>=0){ panelCivId=ownerCivId; }
  else { panelCivId=-2; } // neutral — use province fallback
  _hlDirty=true; // force highlight canvas rebuild for new selection

  // Determine display info (use civ if owned, else country data)
  const ownerCiv=ownerCivId>=0?civs[ownerCivId]:null;
  const co=countries[p.countryId];
  const civColor=ownerCiv?ownerCiv.color:(co?co.color:'#888');
  const civName=ownerCiv?ownerCiv.name:(co?co.name:'Unknown');

  // ── Header
  document.getElementById('pp-cdot').style.background=civColor;
  document.getElementById('pp-name').textContent=civName;
  document.getElementById('pp-ctry').textContent=
    (isPlayerOwned?'YOUR NATION':isOtherCiv?'FOREIGN NATION':'NEUTRAL TERRITORY');

  // ── Status badge
  const badge=document.getElementById('pp-badge');
  badge.style.color=''; badge.style.borderColor='';
  if(isPlayerOwned){
    badge.textContent='YOURS'; badge.className='pp-badge owned';
  } else if(alreadyAtWar){
    badge.textContent='AT WAR'; badge.className='pp-badge enemy';
    badge.style.color='#ff4040'; badge.style.borderColor='#ff4040';
  } else if(isOtherCiv){
    badge.textContent='FOREIGN'; badge.className='pp-badge enemy';
  } else {
    badge.textContent='NEUTRAL'; badge.className='pp-badge neutral';
  }

  // Hide per-province owner row (we're showing the country directly)
  document.getElementById('pp-owner').style.display='none';

  // ── Declare War / Claim button
  const clm=document.getElementById('pp-clm');
  const warWrap=document.getElementById('pp-war-wrap');
  const warSelect=document.getElementById('pp-war-type');
  const alreadyAllied = isOtherCiv && playerCivId >= 0 && areAllied(playerCivId, ownerCivId);

  if(gameMode==='sandbox'){
    clm.style.display='';if(warWrap)warWrap.style.display='flex';if(warSelect)warSelect.style.display='none'; clm.textContent='⚡ Claim'; clm.disabled=false; clm.style.background='';
  } else if(isOtherCiv && playerCivId >= 0){
    clm.style.display='';if(warWrap)warWrap.style.display='flex';if(warSelect)warSelect.style.display=alreadyAtWar?'none':'';
    if(alreadyAtWar){
      clm.textContent='⚔ At War'; clm.disabled=true; clm.style.background='rgba(80,10,10,.8)';
    } else if(alreadyAllied){
      // Can't declare war directly on an ally — must betray first
      clm.style.display='none';if(warWrap)warWrap.style.display='none';
    } else {
      if(warSelect)warSelect.style.display='';clm.textContent='⚔ Declare War'; clm.disabled=false; clm.style.background='rgba(100,20,20,.8)';
    }
  } else if(ownerCivId<0&&playerCivId>=0&&(isAdjToOwned(pid)||ownedProvs().size===0)){
    clm.style.display='';if(warWrap)warWrap.style.display='flex';if(warSelect)warSelect.style.display='none'; clm.textContent='🏴 Claim'; clm.disabled=false; clm.style.background='';
  } else {
    clm.style.display='none';if(warWrap)warWrap.style.display='none';
  }

  // ── Dynamic alliance action buttons (cleared and rebuilt every open) ──
  const ppSrow = warWrap?.parentElement || clm.parentElement;
  // Remove any previously injected buttons
  ppSrow.querySelectorAll('.pp-dyn-btn').forEach(b => b.remove());

  if(isOtherCiv && playerCivId >= 0){
    const myAl = getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
    const targetAl = getAllianceOfCiv(ownerCivId);
    const isAllyMember = myAl && myAl.memberIds.has(ownerCivId);
    const isLeader = myAl && myAl.leaderId === playerCivId;

    function makePPBtn(id, text, bg, borderCol, textCol){
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.id = id; b.className = 'pp-clmbtn pp-dyn-btn';
      b.style.cssText = `background:${bg};border-color:${borderCol};color:${textCol};margin-left:6px`;
      b.textContent = text;
      return b;
    }

    if(isAllyMember){
      // ── Viewing an alliance member's territory ──

      if(isLeader){
        // Kick button
        const kickBtn = makePPBtn('pp-kick-btn','✕ Kick','rgba(60,30,10,.85)','rgba(200,100,30,.5)','#e08040');
        kickBtn.title = 'Kick '+civName+' from your alliance';
        kickBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          kickFromAlliance(ownerCivId);
          buildAllianceWidget();
          openPanel(pid);
        });
        ppSrow.appendChild(kickBtn);
      }

      // Betray button (any member can betray)
      const betrayBtn = makePPBtn('pp-betray-btn','⚔ Betray','rgba(80,10,10,.85)','rgba(200,40,40,.5)','#e05050');
      betrayBtn.title = 'Betray '+civName+' — remove from alliance and declare war';
      betrayBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        betrayAndAttack(ownerCivId);
        buildAllianceWidget();
        openPanel(pid);
      });
      ppSrow.appendChild(betrayBtn);

    } else if(!targetAl && myAl && isLeader && !alreadyAtWar){
      // ── Viewing a non-allied foreign nation — offer invite if leader ──
      const invBtn = makePPBtn('pp-invite-alliance','📜 Invite','rgba(30,60,100,.8)','rgba(80,140,240,.5)','#88aaee');
      invBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        inviteToAlliance(myAl.id, ownerCivId);
        buildAllianceWidget();
        openPanel(pid);
      });
      ppSrow.appendChild(invBtn);
    }
  }

  // ── Compute income for a civ (per-minute rates)
  function calcIncome(civ){
    const rates={food:0,wood:0,stone:0,coins:0};
    if(!civ) return rates;
    for(const pid2 of civ.ownedProvs){
      const prov=provinces[pid2];
      for(const bname of ECON_BLDS){
        const lv=prov.bld[bname]; if(!lv) continue;
        const def=BLDEFS[bname],mul=def.mul[lv];
        for(const[r,rate] of def._prodPairs) rates[r]+=rate*mul*0.016*60;
      }
    }
    return rates;
  }

  // ── Nation stats HTML
  function buildStatsHtml(civ, showTechIntel){
    if(!civ) return '<div class="pp-locked">Neutral territory — no owner</div>';
    const income=calcIncome(civ);
    const icons={food:'🌾',wood:'🪵',stone:'🪨',coins:'🪙'};
    const incomeLines=Object.entries(income)
      .filter(([,v])=>v>0.01)
      .map(([r,v])=>`<span style="margin-right:10px">${icons[r]} ${v.toFixed(1)}/min</span>`)
      .join('');
    const troopCount=civFoxDivCount(civ);
    const provCount=civ.ownedProvs.size;

    // ── Tech intel block (for foreign/bot civs) ──────────────────────────
    let techHtml = '';
    if(showTechIntel){
      const techs = civ.id===playerCivId ? playerTechs : (civ.techs || new Set());
      const b = civ.techBonus || {};

      // Work out econ tier — highest fully completed tier
      let econTier = 0;
      for(let i=0; i<_ECON_TIERS.length; i++){
        if(_ECON_TIERS[i].every(id => techs.has(id))) econTier = i+1;
        else break;
      }
      // Military display tier follows the actual branch tier numbers (I..XII).
      // Use the highest researched tier present in any military branch so new X/XI/XII
      // technologies are visible immediately in nation intelligence instead of being capped at XI.
      let milTier = 0;
      for(const id of techs){const m=/^(?:arm|gun|bot|spc|air|wmd)(\d+)$/.exec(id);if(m)milTier=Math.max(milTier,Math.min(12,+m[1]));}

      // Distinct late-game tier colours, matching the tech-tree Tier X/XI/XII palette.
      const tierColor = t => t >= 12 ? '#ff63b8' : t >= 11 ? '#c778ff' : t >= 10 ? '#55c8ff' : t >= 9 ? '#e040ff' : t >= 7 ? '#e87070' : t >= 4 ? '#e8b060' : t >= 2 ? '#a0c870' : '#7a8090';
      const econCol = tierColor(econTier);
      const milCol  = tierColor(milTier);

      // Key combat bonuses — only show non-zero
      const bonusItems = [];
      if(b.atkMul)    bonusItems.push(`<span style="color:#e08060">⚔ +${Math.round(b.atkMul*100)}% ATK</span>`);
      if(b.defMul)    bonusItems.push(`<span style="color:#60a0e0">🛡 +${Math.round(b.defMul*100)}% DEF</span>`);
      if(b.dmgReduce) bonusItems.push(`<span style="color:#80c080">🩹 -${Math.round(b.dmgReduce*100)}% DMG</span>`);
      if(b.movMul)    bonusItems.push(`<span style="color:#c0a060">🏃 +${Math.round(b.movMul*100)}% MOV</span>`);
      if(b.allMul)    bonusItems.push(`<span style="color:#b0b060">📈 +${Math.round(b.allMul*100)}% PROD</span>`);
      const bonusLine = bonusItems.length
        ? bonusItems.join('<span style="color:#b09060;margin:0 3px">·</span>')
        : '<span style="color:#b09060;font-style:italic">No bonuses researched</span>';

      // Total techs researched + exact researched names
      const totalTechs = techs.size;
      const nameById=new Map();
      for(const tree of Object.values(TECH_TREES||{}))for(const row of (tree.rows||[]))for(const t of (row.techs||[]))nameById.set(t.id,t.name);
      const econIds=new Set(_ECON_TIERS.flat());
      const econNames=[...techs].filter(id=>econIds.has(id)).map(id=>nameById.get(id)||id);
      // Do not use the historical starting-tech preset table as the intelligence ceiling.
      // Actual military branch IDs continue through Tier XII.
      const milNames=[...techs].filter(id=>/^(?:arm|gun|bot|spc|air|wmd)(?:[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(id)).map(id=>nameById.get(id)||id);
      const techChips=(names,empty)=>names.length?names.map(n=>`<span style="display:inline-block;padding:2px 5px;border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.16);border-radius:4px;background:rgba(20,14,5,.55);color:#c9ad72;margin:1px 2px 1px 0">${n}</span>`).join(''):`<span style="color:#7e6a49;font-style:italic">${empty}</span>`;

      techHtml = `
        <div class="pp-sect">${civ.id===playerCivId?'NATIONAL TECHNOLOGY':'INTELLIGENCE REPORT'}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;margin-bottom:6px">
          <div style="flex:1;background:rgba(20,14,5,.6);border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.12);border-radius:6px;padding:7px 9px">
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:7px;color:#8a7050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:4px">🌾 ECON LEVEL</div>
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:${econCol}">LEVEL ${econTier}<span style="font-size:9px;color:#9a8050"> / 12</span></div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1;background:rgba(20,14,5,.6);border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.12);border-radius:6px;padding:7px 9px">
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:7px;color:#8a7050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:4px">⚔ MILITARY LEVEL</div>
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:${milCol}">LEVEL ${milTier}<span style="font-size:9px;color:#9a8050"> / 12</span></div>
          </div>
          <div style="flex:1;background:rgba(20,14,5,.6);border:1px solid rgba(200,140,50,.12);border-radius:6px;padding:7px 9px">
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:7px;color:#8a7050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:4px">🔬 TECHS</div>
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#a0a0c0">${totalTechs}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-size:11px;flex-wrap:wrap;display:flex;gap:4px;padding:0 1px 6px">${bonusLine}</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:7px;color:#8a7050;letter-spacing:1.2px;margin:4px 0 3px">🌾 ECONOMY TECHS (${econNames.length})</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-size:10px;line-height:1.45;margin-bottom:5px">${techChips(econNames,'No economic technologies yet')}</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:7px;color:#8a7050;letter-spacing:1.2px;margin:4px 0 3px">⚔ MILITARY TECHS (${milNames.length})</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-size:10px;line-height:1.45">${techChips(milNames,'No military technologies yet')}</div>`;
    }

    return `
      <div class="pp-sect">NATION OVERVIEW</div>
      <div style="padding:8px 4px 4px;font-family:'Crimson Pro',serif;font-size:12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:9px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:8px;color:#9a8050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:3px">💰 INCOME (per min)</div>
          <div style="color:#a0b860;flex-wrap:wrap;display:flex">${incomeLines||'<span style="color:#8a7050">No production buildings</span>'}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:20px">
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:8px;color:#9a8050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:3px">⚔ TROOPS</div>
            <div style="color:#e09060;font-size:14px;font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-weight:600">${troopCount} <span style="font-size:10px;color:#7a5030">div${troopCount!==1?'s':''}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-size:8px;color:#9a8050;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:3px">🗺 PROVINCES</div>
            <div style="color:#70a8d0;font-size:14px;font-family:'Cinzel',serif;font-weight:600">${provCount}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${techHtml}`;
  }

  const econEl=document.getElementById('pp-econ');
  const milEl=document.getElementById('pp-mil');
  const recEl=document.getElementById('pp-recruit');
  const tabsEl=panel.querySelector('.pp-tabs');

  // Always show stats in econ section; pass intel flag for foreign civs
  econEl.innerHTML=buildStatsHtml(ownerCiv, isOtherCiv || isPlayerOwned);
  milEl.innerHTML='';

  if(isPlayerOwned){
    // Show tabs with econ (stats) and recruit; hide mil tab
    tabsEl.style.display='flex';
    // Reset tab state to econ
    document.querySelectorAll('.pp-tab').forEach(t=>{
      t.classList.remove('on');
      // Hide mil tab for country view
      t.style.display=(t.dataset.tab==='mil')?'none':'';
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.pp-content').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('on'));
    panel.querySelector('.pp-tab[data-tab="econ"]').classList.add('on');
    econEl.classList.add('on');
    // Populate recruit tab (uses random province logic)
    refreshRecruitTab(pid);
  } else {
    // No tabs for foreign/neutral — just show stats
    tabsEl.style.display='none';
    document.querySelectorAll('.pp-content').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('on'));
    econEl.classList.add('on');
  }

  panel.classList.add('open');
}

function closePanel(){selectedProv=-1;panelCivId=-1;_hlDirty=true;document.getElementById('pp').classList.remove('open');if(isMobile&&_hlCanvas){_hlCanvas.width=1;_hlCanvas.height=1;_hlCanvas=null;_hlCtx=null;_hlCivId=-999;_hlPath2D=null;}}

function upgradeBuilding(pid,bname){
  if(playerCivId<0) return;
  const p=provinces[pid];
  const lv=p.bld[bname];if(lv>=3)return;
  const cost=BLDEFS[bname].costs[lv];
  const r=civs[playerCivId].res;
  if(r.wood<cost.w||r.stone<cost.s)return;
  r.wood-=cost.w;r.stone-=cost.s;
  p.bld[bname]++;_econBuildingChanged(pid,bname,lv,lv+1);
  AudioEngine.industry();
  burst(tcx(p.centerTx),tcy(p.centerTy),BLDEFS[bname].tab==='econ'?'#6ad060':'#e05050');
  buildGenBar();
  // Refresh the country stats if panel is open for player's own nation
  if(selectedProv>=0&&panelCivId===playerCivId) openPanel(selectedProv);
}


// Player upgrade summary. Building/ownership changes are rare; resources change often.
// Keep exact level counts per building so ordinary HUD ticks are O(number of building types)
// rather than O(player provinces × building types).
let _upgradeMetaDirty=true,_upgradeMetaCivId=-999,_upgradeMetaVersion=0;
const _upgradeMeta=Object.create(null),_upgradeUiRows=Object.create(null);
let _upgradeUiWood=-1,_upgradeUiStone=-1,_upgradeUiMetaVersion=-1,_upgradeAllBtn=null;
function _markUpgradeMetaDirty(){_upgradeMetaDirty=true;_upgradeUiMetaVersion=-1;}
function _rebuildUpgradeMeta(){
  if(playerCivId<0){_upgradeMetaCivId=playerCivId;_upgradeMetaDirty=false;_upgradeMetaVersion++;return;}
  const civ=civs[playerCivId],all=[...ECON_BLDS,...MIL_BLDS];
  for(const bname of all){let m=_upgradeMeta[bname];if(!m)m=_upgradeMeta[bname]={c0:0,c1:0,c2:0,c3:0,n:0,sum:0,best:Infinity,cw:Infinity,cs:Infinity};m.c0=m.c1=m.c2=m.c3=m.n=m.sum=0;m.best=Infinity;m.cw=m.cs=Infinity;}
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){const b=provinces[pid]?.bld;if(!b)continue;for(let i=0;i<all.length;i++){const name=all[i],m=_upgradeMeta[name],lv=b[name]||0;m.n++;m.sum+=lv;if(lv<=0)m.c0++;else if(lv===1)m.c1++;else if(lv===2)m.c2++;else m.c3++;if(lv<3){const c=BLDEFS[name].costs[lv],z=c.w+c.s;if(z<m.best){m.best=z;m.cw=c.w;m.cs=c.s;}}}}
  _upgradeMetaCivId=playerCivId;_upgradeMetaDirty=false;_upgradeMetaVersion++;
}
function _upgradeSummary(bname){
  if(_upgradeMetaDirty||_upgradeMetaCivId!==playerCivId)_rebuildUpgradeMeta();
  const m=_upgradeMeta[bname]||{c0:0,c1:0,c2:0,c3:0,n:0,sum:0,cw:Infinity,cs:Infinity},def=BLDEFS[bname];
  const counts=[m.c0,m.c1,m.c2],r=playerCivId>=0?civs[playerCivId].res:null;
  let can=false;
  for(let lv=0;lv<3;lv++){if(!counts[lv])continue;const c=def.costs[lv];if(r&&r.wood>=c.w&&r.stone>=c.s){can=true;break;}}
  return {m,can,allMax:m.n>0&&m.c3===m.n,cw:m.cw,cs:m.cs};
}
function _refreshGenBarAffordability(){
  if(playerCivId<0)return;
  if(_upgradeMetaDirty||_upgradeMetaCivId!==playerCivId)_rebuildUpgradeMeta();
  let anyAll=false;
  for(const bname of [...ECON_BLDS,...MIL_BLDS]){
    const row=_upgradeUiRows[bname];if(!row)continue;
    const s=_upgradeSummary(bname),btn=row.btn,sub=row.sub,def=BLDEFS[bname];
    btn.disabled=!s.can;if(s.can)anyAll=true;
    const txt=s.allMax?'ALL MAX':`${s.cw}🪵${s.cs}🪨`;if(sub&&sub.textContent!==txt)sub.textContent=txt;
    btn.title=s.allMax?`${def.name} fully upgraded`:`Mass upgrade ${def.name}`;
  }
  if(!_upgradeAllBtn)_upgradeAllBtn=document.getElementById('gb-mass-all-btn');
  if(_upgradeAllBtn)_upgradeAllBtn.disabled=!anyAll;
}
function _refreshUpgradeUiLive(){
  if(playerCivId<0)return;
  if(_upgradeMetaCivId!==playerCivId)_markUpgradeMetaDirty();
  if(_upgradeMetaDirty)_rebuildUpgradeMeta();
  const r=civs[playerCivId].res,w=Math.floor(r.wood),st=Math.floor(r.stone);
  if(w===_upgradeUiWood&&st===_upgradeUiStone&&_upgradeUiMetaVersion===_upgradeMetaVersion)return;
  _upgradeUiWood=w;_upgradeUiStone=st;_upgradeUiMetaVersion=_upgradeMetaVersion;_refreshGenBarAffordability();
}

let _flagBannerSig='';
function _refreshFlagBannerIfChanged(){if(playerCivId<0)return updateFlagBanner();const c=civs[playerCivId],sig=c.id+'|'+c.name+'|'+c.color;if(sig!==_flagBannerSig){_flagBannerSig=sig;updateFlagBanner();}}

let panelRefreshTimer=0;
let _genBarTimer=0;
let _allianceWidgetTimer=0;
function tickPanel(){
  _refreshUpgradeUiLive();
  if(++_genBarTimer>=120){_genBarTimer=0;if(playerCivId>=0)_refreshFlagBannerIfChanged();}
  // Refresh alliance widget every 3 seconds when General tab is active,
  // but NEVER while the user has a dropdown or input focused inside it —
  // rebuilding destroys the DOM and closes the native select popup.
  if(++_allianceWidgetTimer>=180){
    _allianceWidgetTimer=0;
    const genTab=document.getElementById('gb-tab-general');
    if(genTab&&genTab.classList.contains('active')){
      const widgetEl=document.getElementById('gb-alliance-widget');
      const focused=document.activeElement;
      const userInteracting = widgetEl && focused && widgetEl.contains(focused);
      if(!userInteracting) buildAllianceWidget();
    }
  }
  if(selectedProv<0)return;
  if(++panelRefreshTimer<30)return;
  panelRefreshTimer=0;
  // Refresh recruit tab if it's active (own country panel only)
  const recTab=document.getElementById('pp-recruit');  if(recTab&&recTab.classList.contains('on')) refreshRecruitTab(selectedProv, true);
}

// Province panel tabs
document.querySelectorAll('.pp-tab').forEach(tab=>{
  tab.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const t=tab.dataset.tab;
    document.querySelectorAll('.pp-tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
    tab.classList.add('on');
    document.getElementById('pp-econ').classList.toggle('on',t==='econ');
    document.getElementById('pp-mil').classList.toggle('on',t==='mil');
    document.getElementById('pp-recruit').classList.toggle('on',t==='recruit');
    if(t==='recruit'&&selectedProv>=0) refreshRecruitTab(selectedProv);
  });
});
document.getElementById('pp-cls').addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();closePanel();});
document.getElementById('pp-clm').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  e.stopPropagation();
  if(selectedProv<0) return;
  if(playerCivId<0&&gameMode!=='sandbox') return;
  const ownerCivId=provOwner[selectedProv];
  if(gameMode==='sandbox'){
    // Sandbox: instantly claim
    civClaimProvince(selectedProv,playerCivId>=0?playerCivId:0);
    openPanel(selectedProv);
    return;
  }
  if(ownerCivId<0){
    // Neutral — claim if adjacent or first province
    const canClaim=isAdjToOwned(selectedProv)||ownedProvs().size===0;
    if(canClaim&&playerCivId>=0){ civClaimProvince(selectedProv,playerCivId); openPanel(selectedProv); }
  } else if(ownerCivId!==playerCivId){
    // Foreign — declare war on ANY country (international wars — no adjacency required)
    if(playerCivId>=0&&!areAtWar(playerCivId,ownerCivId)){
      declareWar(playerCivId, ownerCivId, document.getElementById('pp-war-type')?.value||'normal');
    }
    openPanel(selectedProv);
  }
});

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// BUILT-IN WORLD WAR I — 4 AUGUST 1914
// Province ownership is rebuilt one land province at a time from the untouched
// modern base map. Historical border splits use each deterministic province's
// geographic centre + its built-in historical province name, so this remains
// stable across reloads without shipping a second giant owner-array save.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const WW1_1914_NAMES = {
  'W. Sahara':'Spanish Sahara',
  'Sudan':'Anglo-Egyptian Sudan',
  'Chad':'French Equatorial Africa',
  'Russia':'Russian Empire',
  'Norway':'Kingdom of Norway',
  'France':'French Republic',
  'Senegal':'French West Africa',
  'Algeria':'French Algeria',
  'Tunisia':'French Protectorate of Tunisia',
  'United Arab Emirates':'Trucial States',
  'Qatar':'Qatar',
  'Kuwait':'Sheikhdom of Kuwait',
  'Oman':'Sultanate of Muscat and Oman',
  'Afghanistan':'Emirate of Afghanistan',
  'Uzbekistan':'Emirate of Bukhara',
  'Iran':'Qajar Persia',
  'Sweden':'Kingdom of Sweden',
  'Germany':'German Empire',
  'Austria':'Austria-Hungary',
  'Romania':'Kingdom of Romania',
  'Bulgaria':'Kingdom of Bulgaria',
  'Greece':'Kingdom of Greece',
  'Turkey':'Ottoman Empire',
  'Albania':'Principality of Albania',
  'Switzerland':'Swiss Confederation',
  'Belgium':'Kingdom of Belgium',
  'Netherlands':'Kingdom of the Netherlands',
  'Portugal':'Portuguese Republic',
  'Spain':'Kingdom of Spain',
  'Italy':'Kingdom of Italy',
  'Denmark':'Kingdom of Denmark',
  'United Kingdom':'United Kingdom',
  'Eritrea':'Italian Eritrea',
