  'Saudi Arabia':'Emirate of Nejd and Hasa',
  'Morocco':'French Protectorate of Morocco',
  'Egypt':'Khedivate of Egypt (British Occupation)',
  'Libya':'Italian Libya',
  'Serbia':'Kingdom of Serbia',
  'Montenegro':'Kingdom of Montenegro'
};
const WW1_1914_COLORS = {
  'W. Sahara':'#b8864b','Sudan':'#8b574a','Chad':'#6d86a8','Russia':'#5f806e',
  'Norway':'#785151','France':'#4c6f9f','Senegal':'#627da3','Algeria':'#57779e','Tunisia':'#6683a6',
  'United Arab Emirates':'#806f4f','Qatar':'#835f54','Kuwait':'#7e714d','Oman':'#7f6a4c','Afghanistan':'#69714f',
  'Uzbekistan':'#7d6740','Iran':'#607a5c','Sweden':'#806233','Germany':'#55584f','Austria':'#b39867',
  'Romania':'#b99431','Bulgaria':'#4d8b3c','Greece':'#5c9bc8','Turkey':'#8a6240','Albania':'#874359',
  'Switzerland':'#a8a8a8','Belgium':'#c1aa38','Netherlands':'#c26b32','Portugal':'#39705c','Spain':'#af7e38',
  'Italy':'#7e965e','Denmark':'#9c6b5c','United Kingdom':'#842c32','Eritrea':'#728d58','Yemen':'#8b6450',
  'Saudi Arabia':'#7b7940','Morocco':'#657fa1','Egypt':'#8f674b','Libya':'#728c58','Serbia':'#5c6990','Montenegro':'#466c5b'
};
const WW1_1914_FULL_TRANSFER = Object.freeze({
  'Kazakhstan':'Russia','Kyrgyzstan':'Russia','Turkmenistan':'Russia',
  'Belarus':'Russia','Moldova':'Russia','Latvia':'Russia','Estonia':'Russia','Finland':'Russia',
  'Armenia':'Russia','Azerbaijan':'Russia','Georgia':'Russia',
  'Hungary':'Austria','Czechia':'Austria','Slovakia':'Austria','Slovenia':'Austria','Croatia':'Austria','Bosnia and Herz.':'Austria',
  'Ireland':'United Kingdom','Iceland':'Denmark','Cyprus':'United Kingdom','N. Cyprus':'United Kingdom',
  'Israel':'Turkey','Lebanon':'Turkey','Jordan':'Turkey','Iraq':'Turkey','Syria':'Turkey',
  'Kosovo':'Serbia','Mali':'Senegal','Mauritania':'Senegal','Niger':'Senegal'
});
const WW1_POLAND_GERMAN_NAMES=Object.freeze(['silesia','jelenia','greater poland','poznań','poznan','zielona','pomerelia','gdańsk','gdansk','warmia','mazuria','olsztyn']);
const WW1_POLAND_AUSTRIAN_NAMES=Object.freeze(['lesser poland','rzeszów','rzeszow','sub-carpathia']);
const WW1_TECH_ADVANCED_NAMES=new Set(['German Empire','United Kingdom','French Republic','Austria-Hungary','Kingdom of Italy','Kingdom of Belgium','Kingdom of the Netherlands','Kingdom of Sweden','Swiss Confederation']);
const WW1_TECH_MAJOR_NAMES=new Set(['Russian Empire','Ottoman Empire','Kingdom of Romania','Kingdom of Bulgaria','Kingdom of Greece','Kingdom of Serbia','Kingdom of Norway','Kingdom of Denmark','Kingdom of Spain','Portuguese Republic','Qajar Persia']);

function _ww1Geo(p){
  if(!p||!proj||typeof proj.invert!=='function') return [0,0];
  const g=proj.invert([p.centerTx,p.centerTy]);
  return g&&Number.isFinite(g[0])&&Number.isFinite(g[1])?g:[0,0];
}
function _ww1NameHas(p,...parts){
  const n=(p?.name||'').toLowerCase();
  return parts.some(x=>n.includes(String(x).toLowerCase()));
}

function _ww1ResolveOwner(source,p,lon,lat,customIds){
  // Colonial/dependent units intentionally remain separate where the map has
  // enough territory to label them; continental empires are consolidated.
  const fullTarget=WW1_1914_FULL_TRANSFER[source];
  if(fullTarget) return fullTarget;

  if(source==='Russia'){
    // East Prussia / Königsberg: the modern Kaliningrad exclave was German
    // territory in 1914. Keep this as an explicit geographic exception because
    // the untouched modern base map correctly supplies these cells as Russia.
    if(lon>19.0&&lon<23.0&&lat>53.8&&lat<55.5) return 'Germany';
    return 'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='France'){
    // Reichsland Elsaß-Lothringen: Alsace + Moselle/Metz sector.
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'lower alsace','upper alsace','moselle')) return 'Germany';
    if(lon>6.0&&lat>47.4&&lat<49.7&&_ww1NameHas(p,'alsace')) return 'Germany';
    return 'France';
  }
  if(source==='Poland'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,...WW1_POLAND_GERMAN_NAMES)) return 'Germany';
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,...WW1_POLAND_AUSTRIAN_NAMES)) return 'Austria';
    // Geographic fallback for generic cells not matched to a historical label.
    if((lon<18.25)||(lat>53.55&&lon<22.8)||(lon<19.25&&lat<51.55)) return 'Germany';
    if(lat<50.25&&lon>18.25&&lon<23.8) return 'Austria';
    return 'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='Ukraine'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'galicia','lviv','ternopil','ivano-frankivsk','drohobych','transcarpathia','bukovyna','bukovina')) return 'Austria';
    return 'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='Romania'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'transylvania','hunedoara','crișana','crisana','maramureș','maramures','cluj','deva','bistrița','bistrita','suceava')) return 'Austria';
    return 'Romania';
  }
  if(source==='Serbia'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'vojvodina','novi sad')||lat>44.95) return 'Austria';
    return 'Serbia';
  }
  if(source==='Italy'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'south tyrol','trentino')) return 'Austria';
    // Trieste/Gorizia sector is represented by the easternmost Friuli cell.
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'friuli')&&lon>13.1) return 'Austria';
    return 'Italy';
  }
  if(source==='Lithuania'){
    // Memel/Klaipėda district belonged to the German Empire.
    if((lon<21.75&&lat>55.15)||_ww1NameHas(p,'memel','klaip')) return 'Germany';
    return 'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='Turkey'){
    // Kars/Ardahan were in the Russian Empire in 1914; keep Erzurum/Van Ottoman.
    if(lon>42.0&&lat>39.05) return 'Russia';
    return 'Turkey';
  }
  if(source==='Bulgaria'){
    // Southern Dobruja had passed to Romania in 1913.
    if(lon>27.25&&lat>43.45) return 'Romania';
    return 'Bulgaria';
  }
  if(source==='Greece'){
    // Western Thrace was Bulgarian after the 1913 Treaty of Bucharest.
    if(lon>24.55&&lat>40.55) return 'Bulgaria';
    return 'Greece';
  }
  if(source==='Macedonia'){
    // Vardar Macedonia was mainly Serbian after 1913; only the narrow upper
    // Strumica sector remained Bulgarian, so do not hand Bulgaria the whole east.
    return (lon>22.52&&lat>41.10&&lat<41.72)?'Bulgaria':'Serbia';
  }
  if(source==='Morocco'){
    // Spain had both the northern Rif/Yebala zone and the detached Cape Juby/Tarfaya
    // southern zone; the remainder of Morocco is the French protectorate.
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'nador')||(lat>35.0&&lon>-4.5)||(lat<29.75&&lon<-8.4)) return 'Spain';
    return 'Morocco';
  }
  if(source==='Uzbekistan'){
    // The 1914 Atlas of Asiatic Russia distinguishes direct Russian Turkestan
    // (Syr-Darya, Samarkand, Fergana) from the Khiva/Bukhara protectorates.
    if(lon<61.8&&lat>39.0) return customIds.khiva;
    if((lon>66.0&&lat>38.55)||(lat>40.45&&lon>62.0)) return 'Russia';
    return 'Uzbekistan'; // Emirate of Bukhara
  }
  if(source==='Tajikistan'){
    // Khujand/northern Fergana and the eastern Pamir administration fall under
    // direct Russian Turkestan; western/central territory remains Bukhara.
    if(lat>39.15||lon>71.0) return 'Russia';
    return 'Uzbekistan';
  }
  if(source==='Saudi Arabia'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'asir','jizan')) return customIds.idrisid;
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'hejaz','medina','mecca','tabuk','al-bahah')) return 'Turkey';
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'al-jawf')||(lat>26.2&&lon>40.0&&lon<45.0)) return customIds.jabal;
    return 'Saudi Arabia';
  }
  if(source==='Yemen'){
    // Ottoman Yemen in the north/west; British Aden protectorates in the south/east.
    return (lat>14.25&&lon<46.5)?'Turkey':'Yemen';
  }
  return source;
}

function _ww1TechProfile(name){
  if(WW1_TECH_ADVANCED_NAMES.has(name)) return 3;
  if(WW1_TECH_MAJOR_NAMES.has(name)) return 2;
  return 1;
}
function _ww1TechForName(name){
  // Hand-picked from the existing game tree to stop 1914 starts inheriting
  // anachronistic Kevlar, blitzkrieg, strategic bombing, nuclear or AI techs.
  const profile=_ww1TechProfile(name);
  const econBase=['farm1','wood1','stone1','trade1','farm2','wood2','stone2','trade2','farm3','surplus','banking'];
  const econIndustrial=['steam1','rail1','steel1','mfg1'];
  const econAdvanced=['oil1','elec1','chem1','tele1'];
  const milBase=['gun1','gun2','gun3','arm1','arm2','bot1','bot2','spc1','spc2','spc3'];
  const milIndustrial=['gun4','spc4'];
  const milAviation=['air1'];
  const out=[...econBase,...milBase];
  if(profile>=2) out.push(...econIndustrial,...milIndustrial);
  if(profile>=3) out.push(...econAdvanced,...milAviation);
  return [...new Set(out)];
}
function _ww1ResourceScale(name){
  if(['German Empire','United Kingdom','French Republic'].includes(name)) return 2.15;
  if(['Russian Empire','Austria-Hungary','Kingdom of Italy'].includes(name)) return 1.80;
  if(['Ottoman Empire','Kingdom of Belgium','Kingdom of the Netherlands','Kingdom of Sweden','Swiss Confederation'].includes(name)) return 1.45;
  if(/French |Italian |Spanish Sahara|Protectorate|Sudan|Occupation/.test(name)) return 0.90;
  return 1.12;
}
function _ww1SeedBuildings(){
  for(const p of provinces){
    if(!p||p.isSea) continue;
    p.bld={farm:0,sawmill:0,mine:0,market:0,barracks:0,fort:0,tower:0};
    const owner=provOwner[p.id],c=owner>=0?civs[owner]:null;
    if(!c) continue;
    const prof=_ww1TechProfile(c.name),rng=makeRng(hashStr('ww1-1914|'+c.name+'|'+p.id));
    p.bld.farm=prof>=2?1+(rng()<0.34?1:0):(rng()<0.55?1:0);
    p.bld.sawmill=rng()<(prof===3?0.62:prof===2?0.42:0.24)?1:0;
    p.bld.mine=rng()<(prof===3?0.58:prof===2?0.38:0.20)?1:0;
    p.bld.market=rng()<(prof===3?0.66:prof===2?0.43:0.22)?1:0;
    p.bld.barracks=rng()<(prof===3?0.40:prof===2?0.30:0.20)?1:0;
    const frontier=[...(provAdj.get(p.id)||[])].some(n=>!provinces[n]?.isSea&&provOwner[n]>=0&&provOwner[n]!==owner);
    if(frontier&&rng()<(prof===3?0.34:0.22)) p.bld.fort=1;
  }
}

function _applyWW11914Map(){
  // Always called immediately after initCivs(), while names are still modern.
  const baseByName={};
  for(const c of civs) baseByName[c.name]=c.id;
  const required=['Russia','Germany','Austria','Turkey','United Kingdom','France','Serbia'];
  if(required.some(n=>baseByName[n]===undefined)) throw new Error('WW1 1914 map cannot find required base nations.');

  // A few 1914 political units have no modern-country slot. Create them in a
  // deterministic order so their IDs are identical in preview and real start.
  const khiva=_makeCustomCiv('Khanate of Khiva','#8b744c',baseByName['Uzbekistan']??-1);
  const jabal=_makeCustomCiv('Emirate of Jabal Shammar','#916438',baseByName['Saudi Arabia']??-1);
  const idrisid=_makeCustomCiv('Idrisid Emirate of Asir','#7b6c3f',baseByName['Saudi Arabia']??-1);
  const customIds={khiva:'@'+khiva.id,jabal:'@'+jabal.id,idrisid:'@'+idrisid.id};

  const owners=new Int16Array(provinces.length).fill(-1);
  let landCount=0,assignedCount=0;
  for(let pid=0;pid<provinces.length;pid++){
    const p=provinces[pid];
    if(!p||p.isSea) continue;
    landCount++;
    const source=countries[p.countryId]?.name||'';
    const [lon,lat]=_ww1Geo(p);
    const target=_ww1ResolveOwner(source,p,lon,lat,customIds);
    let targetId=-1;
    if(typeof target==='string'&&target.startsWith('@')) targetId=+target.slice(1);
    else targetId=baseByName[target];
    if(targetId===undefined||targetId<0) targetId=baseByName[source];
    if(targetId!==undefined&&targetId>=0){owners[pid]=targetId;assignedCount++;}
  }
  if(assignedCount!==landCount) throw new Error(`WW1 1914 ownership incomplete (${assignedCount}/${landCount} land provinces assigned).`);

  // Bulk replace ownership: no capture particles, no war contribution, and no
  // O(N²) transfer churn. This is the same fresh-map approach used by TNO.
  _fillBaseWorldColors(ownedColorBuf);tileCivBuf.fill(-1);
  _provDivIndexDirty=true;_provDivIndex.clear();
  for(const civ of civs){civ.ownedProvs.clear();civ.territory.resetProvinces?.([]);_clearCivRenderMovers(civ);civ.foxes.length=0;civ._damagedDivs?.clear();civ.isPlayer=false;}
  provOwner.fill(-1);
  for(let pid=0;pid<owners.length;pid++){
    const civId=owners[pid]; if(civId<0) continue;
    const p=provinces[pid]; provOwner[pid]=civId;civs[civId].ownedProvs.add(pid);
    civs[civId].territory.addProvince?.(pid);
    bakeProvOwnedColor(pid,civId);
  }

  // Historical display names + palette. Empty absorbed modern civs remain in
  // the backing array for save compatibility but disappear from the country bar.
  for(const [modern,historical] of Object.entries(WW1_1914_NAMES)){
    const id=baseByName[modern];if(id!==undefined)civs[id].name=historical;
  }
  for(const [modern,col] of Object.entries(WW1_1914_COLORS)){
    const id=baseByName[modern],c=id===undefined?null:civs[id];if(!c)continue;
    c.color=col;c.terrAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(col,.20));c.borderAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(col,.35));
    c._cd={base:col,baseD:darken(col,.22),helm:darken(col,.38),helmD:darken(darken(col,.38),.12),lite:lighten(col,.50)};
  }

  // Historical-ish game balance: resources and industrial buildings are fresh,
  // deterministic and period-scaled — never imported from a progressed save.
  for(const civ of civs){
    const base=civStartRes(civ.ownedProvs.size),scale=_ww1ResourceScale(civ.name);
    civ.res={wood:Math.round(base.wood*scale),stone:Math.round(base.stone*scale),food:Math.round(base.food*scale),coins:Math.round(base.coins*scale)};
    civ.techs=new Set(_ww1TechForName(civ.name));
    _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);civ._techCooldown=civ.id%60;
  }
  _ww1SeedBuildings();
  // Colors changed after ownership bake — rebake owned tiles once with final palette.
  for(const civ of civs) for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs) bakeProvOwnedColor(pid,civ.id);

  _terrBorderDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;
  _civLabelDirty=true;_civOutlineCache.clear();_civOutlinePathCache.clear();_clearLabelImgCache();_clearFoxSpriteCache();
  _markWorldFullDirty();_hlDirty=true;
  return true;
}



// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ADDITIONAL REALISTIC HISTORICAL STARTS — 1917 / 1933 / 1936 / 1939 / 1942 / 1945
// These are deterministic province-level reconstructions from the same modern
// source geography used by the game. Border exceptions are resolved from each
// province's geographic centre and its stable generated/historical name.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _histBaseByModernName(){const o={};for(const c of civs){const n=countries[c.countryId]?.name;if(n)o[n]=c.id;}return o;}
function _histCustom(name,color,sourceId,bag,key){const c=_makeCustomCiv(name,color,sourceId??-1);bag[key]=c.id;return c.id;}
function _histResolveId(target,base,custom){if(typeof target==='number')return target;if(typeof target==='string'&&target[0]==='@')return custom[target.slice(1)]??-1;return base[target]??-1;}
function _histInstallOwners(owners){
  _fillBaseWorldColors(ownedColorBuf);tileCivBuf.fill(-1);
  _provDivIndexDirty=true;_provDivIndex.clear();
  for(const c of civs){c.ownedProvs.clear();c.territory.resetProvinces?.([]);_clearCivRenderMovers(c);c.foxes.length=0;c._damagedDivs?.clear();c.isPlayer=false;}
  provOwner.fill(-1);
  for(let pid=0;pid<owners.length;pid++){const cid=owners[pid];if(cid<0||!civs[cid])continue;const p=provinces[pid];provOwner[pid]=cid;civs[cid].ownedProvs.add(pid);civs[cid].territory.addProvince?.(pid);bakeProvOwnedColor(pid,cid);}
}
function _histNameColor(base,modern,name,color){const id=base[modern],c=id===undefined?null:civs[id];if(!c)return;if(name)c.name=name;if(color){c.color=color;c.terrAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(color,.20));c.borderAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(color,.35));c._cd={base:color,baseD:darken(color,.22),helm:darken(color,.38),helmD:darken(darken(color,.38),.12),lite:lighten(color,.50)};}}
function _historicalTechSet(year,name){
  if(year<=1917){const x=_ww1TechForName(name);if(['German Empire','United Kingdom','French Republic','Russian Republic','Kingdom of Italy'].includes(name))x.push('air2','gun4');return [...new Set(x)];}
  let e=4,m=3;
  if(year>=1936)m=4;
  if(year>=1939)e=5;
  if(year>=1942)m=5;
  if(year>=1954){e=5;m=5;}       // mature industrial / early jet-nuclear era
  if(year>=1967){e=5;m=6;}       // missiles, nuclear weapons, advanced aircraft
  if(year>=1989){e=6;m=6;}       // digital/biotech age; no far-future tiers
  const major=/Germany|Soviet|United Kingdom|French|France|Italy|Polish|Czechoslovak|Yugoslav|United States|China|Japan/.test(name);
  if(major&&year>=1939&&year<1954)m=Math.min(6,m+1);
  if(major&&year>=1967)m=Math.min(7,m+1);
  return _buildTechSet(e,m);
}
function _historicalResourceScale(name,year){let s=1.05;if(/Germany|United Kingdom|French|Soviet/.test(name))s=1.85;else if(/Italy|Polish|Czechoslovak|Yugoslav|Romania|Hungary/.test(name))s=1.45;if(year===1942&&/Germany/.test(name))s*=1.12;return s;}
function _historicalSeedBuildings(year){for(const p of provinces){if(!p||p.isSea)continue;p.bld={farm:0,sawmill:0,mine:0,market:0,barracks:0,fort:0,tower:0};const cid=provOwner[p.id],c=cid>=0?civs[cid]:null;if(!c)continue;const rng=makeRng(hashStr('hist|'+year+'|'+c.name+'|'+p.id));const industrial=/Germany|United Kingdom|French|Soviet|Italy|Czechoslovak/.test(c.name);p.bld.farm=1+(rng()<(industrial?.42:.28)?1:0);p.bld.sawmill=rng()<(industrial?.55:.30)?1:0;p.bld.mine=rng()<(industrial?.52:.28)?1:0;p.bld.market=rng()<(industrial?.58:.34)?1:0;p.bld.barracks=rng()<(industrial?.42:.28)?1:0;const frontier=[...(provAdj.get(p.id)||[])].some(n=>!provinces[n]?.isSea&&provOwner[n]>=0&&provOwner[n]!==cid);if(frontier&&rng()<(year>=1939?.36:.22))p.bld.fort=1;}}
function _historicalFinish(year){for(const c of civs){const b=civStartRes(c.ownedProvs.size),s=_historicalResourceScale(c.name,year);c.res={wood:Math.round(b.wood*s),stone:Math.round(b.stone*s),food:Math.round(b.food*s),coins:Math.round(b.coins*s)};c.techs=new Set(_historicalTechSet(year,c.name));_recomputeBotTechBonus(c);c._techCooldown=c.id%60;}_historicalSeedBuildings(year);for(const c of civs)for(const pid of c.ownedProvs)bakeProvOwnedColor(pid,c.id);_terrBorderDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;_civLabelDirty=true;_civOutlineCache.clear();_civOutlinePathCache.clear();_clearLabelImgCache();_clearFoxSpriteCache();_markWorldFullDirty();_hlDirty=true;}

function _interwarOwner(year,source,p,lon,lat,base,custom){
  // Soviet successor territory, with interwar western exceptions.
  if(source==='Russia'){if(year<1945&&lon>19&&lon<23&&lat>53.8&&lat<55.5)return 'Germany';return 'Russia';}
  if(['Kazakhstan','Kyrgyzstan','Turkmenistan','Uzbekistan','Tajikistan','Armenia','Azerbaijan','Georgia'].includes(source))return 'Russia';
  if(source==='Belarus'){
    if(year===1942)return 'Germany';
    if(year===1945)return 'Russia';
    return lon<27.2?'Poland':'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='Ukraine'){
    if(year===1942)return lon<40.5?'Germany':'Russia';
    if(year===1945)return 'Russia';
    if((lon<23.6&&lat<49.25)||_ww1NameHas(p,'transcarpath','zakarp'))return year>=1939?'Hungary':'Czechia';
    if((lon<26.6&&lat<49.2)||_ww1NameHas(p,'cherniv','bukovin'))return 'Romania';
    if((lon<25.8&&lat>48.2)||(lon<27.0&&lat>50.0)||_ww1NameHas(p,'lviv','ternopil','volhyn','ivano-frank'))return 'Poland';
    return 'Russia';
  }
  if(source==='Moldova')return year===1945?'Russia':'Romania';
  if(['Estonia','Latvia','Lithuania'].includes(source)){
    if(year===1942)return 'Germany';
    if(year===1945)return 'Russia';
    if(source==='Lithuania'&&((lon<25.9&&lat<55.2)||_ww1NameHas(p,'vilnius','wilno')))return 'Poland';
    if(source==='Lithuania'&&year>=1939&&((lon<21.45&&lat>55.2)||_ww1NameHas(p,'klaip','memel')))return 'Germany';
    return source;
  }
  if(source==='Poland'){
    if(year===1942)return 'Germany';
    if(year===1945)return 'Poland';
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'gdańsk','gdansk','danzig'))return year>=1939?'Germany':'@danzig';
    // 1919-1939 German territories now inside Poland: east of Oder-Neisse was not all Polish yet.
    if(lon<15.4||(lat<51.5&&lon<18.1)||((lat>53.4&&lon>19.0&&lon<22.2)&&!_ww1NameHas(p,'gdańsk','gdansk')))return 'Germany';
    return 'Poland';
  }
  if(source==='Czechia'){
    if(year>=1939&&year<=1942){
      // Sudeten border districts were annexed to Germany in 1938; the Czech
      // interior became the Protectorate in March 1939.
      if(_ww1NameHas(p,'sudeten','karlovy','liberec','ústí','usti')||lon<13.35||lat>50.45||(lon>16.15&&lat>49.8))return 'Germany';
      return '@bohemia';
    }
    return 'Czechia';
  }
  if(source==='Slovakia'){
    if(year===1933||year===1936||year===1945)return 'Czechia';
    if((year===1939||year===1942)&&lat<48.25)return 'Hungary';
    return 'Slovakia';
  }
  if(source==='Austria')return (year===1939||year===1942)?'Germany':'Austria';
  if(['Croatia','Bosnia and Herz.'].includes(source))return year===1942?'@croatia':'Serbia';
  if(source==='Slovenia'){if(year===1942)return (lon>14.7&&lat<46.25)?'Italy':'Germany';return 'Serbia';}
  if(source==='Montenegro')return year===1942?'Italy':'Serbia';
  if(source==='Kosovo')return year===1942?'Italy':'Serbia';
  if(source==='Macedonia')return year===1942?(lon>21.5?'Bulgaria':'Italy'):'Serbia';
  if(source==='Serbia')return year===1942?'Germany':'Serbia';
  if(source==='Albania')return (year===1939||year===1942)?'Italy':'Albania';
  if(source==='Romania'){
    if(year===1942&&(_ww1NameHas(p,'transylvania','cluj','maramure')||lat>46.45&&lon<25.5))return 'Hungary';
    if(year>=1942&&lon>27.25&&lat>43.45)return 'Bulgaria';
    return 'Romania';
  }
  if(source==='France'&&year===1942)return lat<46.25?'@vichy':'Germany';
  if(['Belgium','Netherlands','Norway','Denmark','Luxembourg'].includes(source)&&year===1942)return 'Germany';
  if(source==='Greece'&&year===1942){if(lon>24.5&&lat>40.55)return 'Bulgaria';return lon<22.0?'Italy':'Germany';}
  if(source==='Spain'&&year===1936)return (lon<-5.0||lat>42.2)?'@nationalist':'Spain';
  if(source==='Syria'&&year<1939&&_ww1NameHas(p,'hatay','iskenderun','alexandretta'))return 'Syria';
  if(source==='Syria'&&year>=1939&&_ww1NameHas(p,'hatay','iskenderun','alexandretta'))return 'Turkey';
  return source;
}


// ── FULL-WORLD HISTORICAL OVERLAY (v0.4) ─────────────────────────
// The old historical engine was deliberately Europe-centric.  This layer keeps
// every old European rule, then reconciles the rest of the modern Natural Earth
// geography into the political units that existed in the selected year.
const HIST_POSTWAR_USSR = new Set(['Ukraine','Belarus','Moldova','Estonia','Latvia','Lithuania','Kazakhstan','Kyrgyzstan','Turkmenistan','Uzbekistan','Tajikistan','Armenia','Azerbaijan','Georgia']);
const HIST_YUGOSLAV_SUCCESSORS = new Set(['Croatia','Bosnia and Herz.','Slovenia','Montenegro','Kosovo','Macedonia']);
const HIST_GERMAN_EAST_NAMES = Object.freeze([
  'brandenburg','lower lusatia','upper lusatia','mecklenburg','western pomerania','saxony','vogtland','thuringia',
  'halle','dresden','leipzig','rügen','rugen','prignitz','magdeburg','rostock'
]);
const HIST_EAST_GERMANY_POLY = Object.freeze([
  [10.58,53.92],[10.95,54.55],[13.95,54.60],[14.70,53.65],[14.78,52.55],
  [14.90,51.05],[13.55,50.45],[12.10,50.20],[11.25,50.33],[10.15,50.18],
  [10.02,51.25],[10.55,52.35]
]);
function _histPointInPoly(lon,lat,poly){
  let inside=false;
  for(let i=0,j=poly.length-1;i<poly.length;j=i++){
    const [xi,yi]=poly[i],[xj,yj]=poly[j];
    if(((yi>lat)!==(yj>lat)) && lon < (xj-xi)*(lat-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-12)+xi) inside=!inside;
  }
  return inside;
}
function _histEastGermany(p,lon,lat){
  if(_ww1NameHas(p,...HIST_GERMAN_EAST_NAMES)) return true;
  // Generic-cell fallback follows the five Soviet-zone Länder much more closely
  // than the old single-longitude cut (which incorrectly captured parts of Bavaria/Hesse).
  return _histPointInPoly(lon,lat,HIST_EAST_GERMANY_POLY);
}
function _histSouthYemen(p,lon,lat){
  return lon>45.05 || lat<14.45 || _ww1NameHas(p,'aden','hadram','mahra','shab','abyan','lahij');
}
function _histSetupWorldCustoms(year,base,custom){
  if([1945,1954,1967,1989].includes(year)){
    const nm=year===1945?'Soviet Occupation Zone (Germany)':'German Democratic Republic';
    _histCustom(nm,year===1945?'#8c5c5c':'#7c4a4a',base.Germany,custom,'eastgermany');
    const berlinName=year===1945?'Berlin (Four-Power Occupation)':'West Berlin (Allied special status)';
    _histCustom(berlinName,'#7187a0',base.Germany,custom,'berlin');
  }
  if(year===1954||year===1967){
    _histCustom(year===1954?'State of Vietnam':'Republic of Vietnam','#c9a642',base.Vietnam,custom,'southvietnam');
  }
  if(year>=1933&&year<=1989){
    const nm=year<=1967?'Aden / South Arabian Territories':'People’s Democratic Republic of Yemen';
    _histCustom(nm,'#6d7960',base.Yemen,custom,'southyemen');
  }
}
const HIST_UK_DEP_1954=new Set(['Ghana','Nigeria','Sierra Leone','Gambia','Kenya','Uganda','Tanzania','Botswana','Lesotho','Eswatini','Zambia','Malawi','Zimbabwe','Guyana','Belize','Jamaica','Trinidad and Tobago','Bahamas','Malaysia','Singapore','Fiji','Solomon Is.']);
const HIST_FR_DEP_1954=new Set(['Senegal','Mauritania','Mali','Guinea','Ivory Coast','Burkina Faso','Benin','Niger','Chad','Central African Rep.','Congo','Gabon','Madagascar','Djibouti','Togo','Cameroon','Morocco','Tunisia','Algeria','New Caledonia']);
const HIST_PT_DEP_1967=new Set(['Angola','Mozambique','Guinea-Bissau','Timor-Leste']);
const HIST_ES_DEP_1967=new Set(['Eq. Guinea','W. Sahara']);
const HIST_UK_DEP_1967=new Set(['Belize','Bahamas','Fiji','Solomon Is.','Eswatini']);
function _histDependencyOwner(year,source){
  // Overseas dependencies/colonies are owned by the administering state while
  // their original province geometry remains untouched. Dominions and major
  // quasi-state entities (e.g. British Raj) stay separate for gameplay/readability.
  if(year<=1954){
    if(HIST_UK_DEP_1954.has(source)) return 'United Kingdom';
    if(HIST_FR_DEP_1954.has(source)) return 'France';
    if(source==='Dem. Rep. Congo'||source==='Rwanda'||source==='Burundi') return 'Belgium';
  }
  if(year<=1967){
    if(HIST_PT_DEP_1967.has(source)) return 'Portugal';
    if(HIST_ES_DEP_1967.has(source)) return 'Spain';
    if(source==='Papua New Guinea') return 'Australia';
    if(source==='Suriname') return 'Netherlands';
    if(HIST_UK_DEP_1967.has(source)) return 'United Kingdom';
    if(source==='Djibouti') return 'France';
  }
  if(source==='New Caledonia') return 'France';
  if(source==='Puerto Rico') return 'United States of America';
  if(source==='Falkland Is.') return 'United Kingdom';
  if(year<=1989&&source==='Namibia') return 'South Africa';
  return null;
}

function _histWorldOwner(year,source,p,lon,lat,base,custom,prior){
  // Requested German split: occupation zones in 1945, sovereign FRG/GDR later.
  if([1945,1954,1967,1989].includes(year)&&source==='Germany'){
    if(_ww1NameHas(p,'berlin')) return '@berlin';
    return _histEastGermany(p,lon,lat)?'@eastgermany':'Germany';
  }

  // Postwar composite states that modern source geography splits apart.
  if(year>=1945&&year<=1989&&HIST_POSTWAR_USSR.has(source)) return 'Russia';
  if([1945,1954,1967,1989].includes(year)&&source==='Slovakia') return 'Czechia';
  if([1945,1954,1967,1989].includes(year)&&HIST_YUGOSLAV_SUCCESSORS.has(source)) return 'Serbia';
  if(year<=1989&&source==='S. Sudan') return 'Sudan';

  // British India and the later Pakistan/East-Pakistan configuration.
  if(year<=1945&&['India','Pakistan','Bangladesh'].includes(source)) return 'India';
  if((year===1954||year===1967)&&source==='Bangladesh') return 'Pakistan';

  // East Asia: Japanese empire before surrender; then the Cold-War divisions.
  if(year<=1942&&['North Korea','South Korea'].includes(source)) return 'Japan';
  if(year<=1945&&source==='Taiwan') return 'Japan';

  // French Indochina is consolidated before the Geneva settlement.  From 1954
  // through 1967 Vietnam is split at the provisional military line near 17°N.
  if(year<=1945&&['Vietnam','Laos','Cambodia'].includes(source)) return 'Vietnam';
  if((year===1954||year===1967)&&source==='Vietnam') return lat>=17.05?'Vietnam':'@southvietnam';

  // Palestine / Arab-Israeli territorial control.  1954 represents the armistice
  // era (West Bank under Jordan, Gaza under Egypt); 1967 is immediately after
  // the Six-Day War, so Sinai, West Bank, Gaza and Golan use the controlling side.
  if(year<=1945&&['Israel','Palestine'].includes(source)) return 'Israel';
  if(year===1954&&source==='Palestine') return lon<34.9?'Egypt':'Jordan';
  if((year===1967||year===1989)&&source==='Palestine') return 'Israel';
  if(year===1967&&source==='Egypt'&&lon>32.25&&lat<31.55) return 'Israel';
  if((year===1967||year===1989)&&source==='Syria'&&lon>35.55&&lat>32.72&&lat<33.45) return 'Israel';

  // North/South Yemen / Aden.  The June-1967 scenario intentionally predates
  // South Yemen's 30 Nov independence, hence the colonial-era southern label.
  if(source==='Yemen'&&year>=1933&&year<=1989)
    return _histSouthYemen(p,lon,lat)?'@southyemen':'Yemen';

  // 1953–63 Federation of Rhodesia and Nyasaland.
  if(year===1954&&['Zambia','Malawi'].includes(source)) return 'Zimbabwe';

  // Ruanda-Urundi and Somali unification.
  if(year<=1954&&source==='Burundi') return 'Rwanda';
  if(year>=1967&&year<=1989&&source==='Somaliland') return 'Somalia';

  // Eritrea: British administration in 1945; federation/annexation with Ethiopia
  // from 1952 until its 1993 independence.
  if(year>=1954&&year<=1989&&source==='Eritrea') return 'Ethiopia';

  // Indonesian control of East Timor in 1989.
  if(year===1989&&source==='Timor-Leste') return 'Indonesia';

  const dependencyOwner=_histDependencyOwner(year,source);
  if(dependencyOwner) return dependencyOwner;

  return prior;
}
function _histWorldNames(year,base,custom){
  const rn=(modern,name,color)=>_histNameColor(base,modern,name,color);

  if([1945,1954,1967,1989].includes(year)){
    rn('Germany',year===1945?'Western Allied Occupation Zones (Germany)':'Federal Republic of Germany',year===1945?'#5f7082':'#596f86');
    rn('Russia','Soviet Union','#7e3d3d');
    rn('Serbia',year===1945?'Democratic Federal Yugoslavia':'Socialist Federal Republic of Yugoslavia','#596a8f');
    rn('Czechia',year===1945?'Czechoslovak Republic':'Czechoslovak Socialist Republic','#789179');
  }

  // South / East Asia
  if(year<=1945) rn('India','British Raj','#9b7653'); else rn('India','Republic of India','#c98a46');
  if(year===1954) rn('Pakistan','Dominion of Pakistan','#447557');
  else if(year>=1967) rn('Pakistan','Islamic Republic of Pakistan','#447557');
  if(year<=1945) rn('Sri Lanka','Ceylon (British Crown Colony)','#8b6f59');
  else if(year<1972) rn('Sri Lanka','Dominion of Ceylon','#8b6f59');
  if(year<=1945) rn('Myanmar','British Burma','#8b7355'); else if(year<=1989) rn('Myanmar','Union of Burma','#6f7756');
  if(year<=1942) rn('Indonesia','Netherlands East Indies','#9a6848');
  else if(year===1945) rn('Indonesia','Republic of Indonesia (independence proclaimed)','#8f6d45');
  else rn('Indonesia','Republic of Indonesia','#8f6d45');
  if(year<=1945) rn('Vietnam','French Indochina','#697e9f');
  else if(year===1954||year===1967) rn('Vietnam','Democratic Republic of Vietnam','#8b4a45');
  else if(year===1989) rn('Vietnam','Socialist Republic of Vietnam','#8b4a45');
  if(year<=1945){rn('Laos','French Laos','#7185a3');rn('Cambodia','French Cambodia','#7185a3');}
  else {rn('Laos',year===1954?'Kingdom of Laos':'Laos','#6f7655');rn('Cambodia',year===1954?'Kingdom of Cambodia':year===1967?'Kingdom of Cambodia':'People’s Republic of Kampuchea','#756852');}
  if(year<=1945) rn('Philippines','Commonwealth of the Philippines (U.S.)','#6c7896'); else rn('Philippines','Republic of the Philippines','#657a9c');
  if(year<=1945) rn('Japan','Empire of Japan','#8e5a58'); else rn('Japan','Japan','#8e5a58');
  if(year<=1942){rn('North Korea','Korea (Japanese rule)','#77575b');rn('South Korea','Korea (Japanese rule)','#77575b');}
  if(year===1945){rn('North Korea','Soviet Occupation Zone (Korea)','#7c4a4a');rn('South Korea','U.S. Occupation Zone (Korea)','#587699');}
  if(year>=1954){rn('North Korea','Democratic People’s Republic of Korea','#8a4a4a');rn('South Korea','Republic of Korea','#52769a');}
  if(year<=1945) rn('China','Republic of China','#8b6f45'); else rn('China','People’s Republic of China','#8b4545');
  if(year>=1954) rn('Taiwan','Republic of China (Taiwan)','#596f91');
  if(year>=1933&&year<=1989) rn('Mongolia','Mongolian People’s Republic','#72514d');
  if(year===1954) rn('Malaysia','Federation of Malaya (British)','#776b54');
  else if(year>=1967) rn('Malaysia','Malaysia','#60785a');
  if(year<=1954) rn('Singapore','Singapore (British Colony)','#776b54');
  else if(year>=1967) rn('Singapore','Republic of Singapore','#5b6f82');
  if(year<=1967) rn('Brunei','Brunei (British Protected State)','#7f714e');
  if(year<=1967) rn('Papua New Guinea','Territory of Papua and New Guinea (Australia)','#7b6f62');

  // Middle East
  if(year<=1945) rn('Israel','Mandatory Palestine','#81745e'); else rn('Israel','State of Israel','#55779a');

  if(year<1975) rn('W. Sahara','Spanish Sahara','#aa8252'); else if(year===1989) rn('W. Sahara','Western Sahara (disputed)','#9d8355');

  if(year<1962){rn('Jamaica','Jamaica (British Colony)','#776e55');rn('Trinidad and Tobago','Trinidad and Tobago (British Colony)','#776e55');}

  // Additional colonial administrations required for a genuinely global 1945/1954 map.
  if(year<=1954){
    rn('Senegal','Senegal (French West Africa)','#667e9e');
    rn('Mauritania','Mauritania (French West Africa)','#667e9e');

function _applyInterwarWW2Map(year){
  const base=_histBaseByModernName(),custom={};
  if(year===1933||year===1936)_histCustom('Free City of Danzig','#a17f5a',base.Poland,custom,'danzig');
  if(year===1936)_histCustom('Nationalist Spain','#6d5f4d',base.Spain,custom,'nationalist');
  if(year===1939||year===1942)_histCustom('Protectorate of Bohemia and Moravia','#777064',base.Czechia,custom,'bohemia');
  if(year===1942){_histCustom('Vichy France','#6f7f9f',base.France,custom,'vichy');_histCustom('Independent State of Croatia','#886b58',base.Croatia,custom,'croatia');}
  _histSetupWorldCustoms(year,base,custom);
  const owners=new Int16Array(provinces.length).fill(-1);let land=0,assigned=0;
  for(let pid=0;pid<provinces.length;pid++){
    const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||p.isSea)continue;land++;
    const source=countries[p.countryId]?.name||'';const [lon,lat]=_ww1Geo(p);
    let target=year<=1945?_interwarOwner(year,source,p,lon,lat,base,custom):source;
    target=_histWorldOwner(year,source,p,lon,lat,base,custom,target);
    let id=_histResolveId(target,base,custom);if(id<0)id=base[source]??-1;
    if(id>=0){owners[pid]=id;assigned++;}
  }
  if(assigned!==land)throw new Error(`Historical ${year} ownership incomplete (${assigned}/${land}).`);
  _histInstallOwners(owners);
  _histNameColor(base,'Russia','Soviet Union','#7e3d3d');
  _histNameColor(base,'Germany',year>=1933&&year<=1942?'German Reich':'Germany','#545454');
  _histNameColor(base,'Serbia',year===1945?'Democratic Federal Yugoslavia':'Kingdom of Yugoslavia','#596a8f');
  _histNameColor(base,'Czechia',year===1945?'Czechoslovak Republic':'Czechoslovakia','#789179');
  _histNameColor(base,'Poland','Second Polish Republic','#a75c72');
  _histNameColor(base,'Italy','Kingdom of Italy','#78915e');
  _histNameColor(base,'Romania','Kingdom of Romania','#b58e31');
  _histNameColor(base,'Hungary','Kingdom of Hungary','#a76048');
  _histNameColor(base,'Bulgaria','Kingdom of Bulgaria','#568346');
  _histNameColor(base,'Greece','Kingdom of Greece','#5f90bd');
  _histNameColor(base,'Albania','Albanian Kingdom','#7c4452');
  _histNameColor(base,'Spain',year===1933?'Second Spanish Republic':year===1936?'Spanish Republic':'Spanish State','#a47b3e');
  _histNameColor(base,'Portugal','Portuguese Republic','#4e765f');
  _histNameColor(base,'France',year===1942?'French State (occupied)':'French Republic','#506f9f');
  _histNameColor(base,'United Kingdom','United Kingdom','#7d3038');
  _histNameColor(base,'Turkey','Republic of Turkey','#8b6045');
  _histNameColor(base,'Iran',year===1933?'Imperial State of Persia':'Imperial State of Iran','#55765d');
  _histNameColor(base,'Egypt','Kingdom of Egypt','#8b6849');
  _histNameColor(base,'Iraq','Kingdom of Iraq','#536e45');
  _histNameColor(base,'Jordan','Emirate of Transjordan','#8b6b4f');
  _histNameColor(base,'Israel','Mandatory Palestine','#81745e');
  _histNameColor(base,'Syria',year>=1945?'Syrian Republic':'French Mandate for Syria','#68748f');
  _histNameColor(base,'Lebanon',year>=1945?'Lebanese Republic':'Greater Lebanon (French Mandate)','#6f855f');
  _histNameColor(base,'Morocco','French Protectorate of Morocco','#667e9e');
  _histNameColor(base,'Tunisia','French Protectorate of Tunisia','#7084a1');
  _histNameColor(base,'Algeria','French Algeria','#57779e');
  _histNameColor(base,'Libya','Italian Libya','#748b5f');
  _histNameColor(base,'Sudan','Anglo-Egyptian Sudan','#895d50');
  _histNameColor(base,'W. Sahara','Spanish Sahara','#aa8252');
  if(year===1933)_histNameColor(base,'Austria','Republic of Austria','#999999');
  if(year===1936)_histNameColor(base,'Austria','Federal State of Austria','#999999');
  if(year===1945)_histNameColor(base,'Germany','Allied-occupied Germany','#666666');
  _histWorldNames(year,base,custom);
  _historicalFinish(year);return true;
}

function _applyWW11917Map(){
  _applyWW11914Map();
  const base=_histBaseByModernName(),custom={};
  const polId=_histCustom('Kingdom of Poland (Central Powers)','#9a6670',base.Poland??base.Russia,custom,'poland1917');
  const owners=Int16Array.from(provOwner);
  const german=base.Germany,austria=base.Austria,russia=base.Russia,bulgaria=base.Bulgaria;
  for(let pid=0;pid<provinces.length;pid++){const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||p.isSea)continue;const source=countries[p.countryId]?.name||'';const [lon,lat]=_ww1Geo(p);
    if(source==='Poland'&&owners[pid]===russia)owners[pid]=polId;
    else if(source==='Lithuania')owners[pid]=german;
    else if(source==='Latvia'&&lon<24.7)owners[pid]=german;
    else if(source==='Belarus'&&lon<27.2)owners[pid]=german;
    else if(source==='Romania'&&lat<46.1&&lon<26.6)owners[pid]=german;
    else if(source==='Serbia')owners[pid]=lon>21.4?bulgaria:austria;
    else if(source==='Montenegro')owners[pid]=austria;
    else if(source==='Macedonia')owners[pid]=bulgaria;
  }
  _histInstallOwners(owners);
  const rus=civs[russia];if(rus)rus.name='Russian Republic';
  _historicalFinish(1917);return true;
}

function _isTnoScenario(scenId){ return /^tno(?:1962|1947|1967|1975|1984)$/.test(scenId||''); }
function _isLegacyTnoScenario(scenId){ return /^tno(?:1947|1967|1975|1984)$/.test(scenId||''); }

function _ensureTnoCountryIdMaps(){
  if(_tnoLegacyToWorldCountryId&&_tnoWorldToLegacyCountryId)return true;
  // Full-world loadMap deliberately keeps every original legacy-region country at
  // the SAME numeric countryId, then appends the rest of the world.  Do not make
  // this stable ID relationship depend on _worldGeoFeatures remaining resident:
  // potato-mode is allowed to release raw GeoJSON after the fixed mesh is built.
  // The legacy template itself is sufficient to recover the size of that prefix.
  let legacyCount=0;
  for(const cd of TNO_CIV_TEMPLATE)if(!cd.customNation&&Number.isInteger(cd.countryId)&&cd.countryId>=0)legacyCount=Math.max(legacyCount,cd.countryId+1);
  if(!legacyCount||!countries?.length||countries.length<legacyCount)return false;
  const fwd=new Int32Array(legacyCount),rev=new Int32Array(countries.length);rev.fill(-1);
  for(let cid=0;cid<legacyCount;cid++){fwd[cid]=cid;rev[cid]=cid;}
  _tnoLegacyToWorldCountryId=fwd;_tnoWorldToLegacyCountryId=rev;return true;
}

function _legacyTnoValidBounds(legacyProj){
  // Exact old validity condition, converted from per-pixel proj.invert() checks
  // into monotonic Mercator pixel bounds. This is equivalent to:
  // lon ∈ [-27,89.5], lat ∈ [14.2,71.8].
  const xa=legacyProj([-27,40])[0],xb=legacyProj([89.5,40])[0];
  const ya=legacyProj([0,71.8])[1],yb=legacyProj([0,14.2])[1];
  return {
    x0:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Math.min(xa,xb)-1e-9)),
    x1:Math.min(WORLD_W-1,Math.floor(Math.max(xa,xb)+1e-9)),
    y0:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Math.min(ya,yb)-1e-9)),
    y1:Math.min(WORLD_H-1,Math.floor(Math.max(ya,yb)+1e-9))
  };
}

// Exact nearest-seed accelerator used only by the one-time legacy TNO rebuild.
// It preserves the original "first seed wins equal distance" rule but avoids the
// O(tiles × seeds) inner scan once countries have more than a small handful of seeds.
function _legacyTnoSeedNearestFactory(xs,ys){
  const k=xs.length;
  if(k<20)return (x,y)=>{let best=0,bd=Infinity;for(let i=0;i<k;i++){const dx=x-xs[i],dy=y-ys[i],d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bd){bd=d;best=i;}}return best;};
  const build=(ids,depth)=>{
    if(!ids.length)return null;const axis=depth&1;
    ids.sort((a,b)=>((axis?ys[a]-ys[b]:xs[a]-xs[b])||a-b));
    const m=ids.length>>1,id=ids[m];
    return {id,axis,left:build(ids.slice(0,m),depth+1),right:build(ids.slice(m+1),depth+1)};
  };
  const root=build(Array.from({length:k},(_,i)=>i),0);
  return (x,y)=>{
    let best=0,bd=Infinity;
    const visit=node=>{if(!node)return;const id=node.id,dx=x-xs[id],dy=y-ys[id],d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bd||(d===bd&&id<best)){bd=d;best=id;}
      const diff=node.axis?dy:dx,near=diff<=0?node.left:node.right,far=diff<=0?node.right:node.left;visit(near);if(diff*diff<=bd)visit(far);};
    visit(root);return best;
  };
}
function _legacyTnoNearestCountryTile(cx,cy,cid,legacyCountry,minX,maxX,minY,maxY,fallback){
  const x0=Math.floor(cx),y0=Math.floor(cy),maxR=Math.max(Math.abs(x0-minX),Math.abs(x0-maxX),Math.abs(y0-minY),Math.abs(y0-maxY))+2;
  let best=fallback,bestD=Infinity,bestTi=fallback;
  const test=(x,y)=>{if(x<minX||x>maxX||y<minY||y>maxY)return;const ti=y*WORLD_W+x;if(legacyCountry[ti]!==cid)return;const dx=x-cx,dy=y-cy,d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bestD||(d===bestD&&ti<bestTi)){bestD=d;bestTi=ti;best=ti;}};
  for(let r=0;r<=maxR;r++){
    if(r===0)test(x0,y0);else{
      const ya=y0-r,yb=y0+r,xa=x0-r,xb=x0+r;
      for(let x=xa;x<=xb;x++){test(x,ya);test(x,yb);}
      for(let y=ya+1;y<yb;y++){test(xa,y);test(xb,y);}
    }
    if(bestD<Infinity&&bestD<=r*r)break;
  }
  return best;
}
function _legacyTnoLloydRelax(seedTxArr,seedTyArr,tiles,iterations,cid,legacyCountry,minX,maxX,minY,maxY){
  const k=seedTxArr.length,sX=seedTxArr.slice(),sY=seedTyArr.slice();
  for(let iter=0;iter<iterations;iter++){
    const nearest=_legacyTnoSeedNearestFactory(sX,sY),sumX=new Float64Array(k),sumY=new Float64Array(k),cnt=new Int32Array(k);
    for(let tii=0;tii<tiles.length;tii++){
      const ti=tiles[tii],tx=ti%WORLD_W,ty=(ti/WORLD_W)|0,best=nearest(tx,ty);sumX[best]+=tx;sumY[best]+=ty;cnt[best]++;
    }
    for(let si=0;si<k;si++)if(cnt[si]>0){sX[si]=sumX[si]/cnt[si];sY[si]=sumY[si]/cnt[si];}
  }
  // Original code snaps every relaxed centroid back onto the nearest country tile.
  // Ring search is exact on the integer raster and normally touches only a tiny area.
  for(let si=0;si<k;si++){
    const best=_legacyTnoNearestCountryTile(sX[si],sY[si],cid,legacyCountry,minX,maxX,minY,maxY,tiles[0]);
    seedTxArr[si]=best%WORLD_W;seedTyArr[si]=(best/WORLD_W)|0;
  }
}
function _tnoProvinceLayoutFingerprint(){
  let h=2166136261>>>0;
  for(let i=0;i<provinces.length;i++){
    const p=provinces[i];if(!p)continue;
    h=Math.imul(h^((p.countryId+2)&0xffff),16777619)>>>0;
    h=Math.imul(h^(Math.round(p.centerTx*16)&0xffff),16777619)>>>0;
    h=Math.imul(h^(Math.round(p.centerTy*16)&0xffff),16777619)>>>0;
    h=Math.imul(h^((p.tiles?.length||0)&0xffff),16777619)>>>0;
  }
  return h.toString(36);
}
function _tnoRemapStorageKey(){return `ws_tno_remap_v6_${WORLD_W}x${WORLD_H}_${provinces.length}_${_tnoProvinceLayoutFingerprint()}`;}
function _loadStoredTnoRemap(){
  try{
    const raw=localStorage.getItem(_tnoRemapStorageKey());if(!raw)return null;
    const parts=raw.split(',');if(parts.length!==provinces.length)return null;
    const a=new Int32Array(parts.length);let mapped=0;const unique=new Set();
    for(let i=0;i<parts.length;i++){const v=+parts[i];if(!Number.isInteger(v)||v<-1||v>=_TNO_LEGACY_LAND_COUNT)return null;a[i]=v;if(v>=0){mapped++;unique.add(v);}}
    if(mapped<Math.min(500,provinces.length*0.03)||unique.size<Math.min(450,Math.floor(_TNO_LEGACY_LAND_COUNT*.30)))return null;return a;
  }catch(e){return null;}
}
function _storeTnoRemap(a){try{localStorage.setItem(_tnoRemapStorageKey(),Array.from(a).join(','));}catch(e){}}

async function _buildTnoLegacyProvinceRemap(){
  if(_tnoLegacyProvinceRemap&&_tnoLegacyProvinceRemap.length===provinces.length)return _tnoLegacyProvinceRemap;
  if(!_ensureTnoCountryIdMaps())throw new Error('TNO compatibility cannot map original country IDs: world geography is unavailable.');
  const stored=_loadStoredTnoRemap();if(stored){_tnoLegacyProvinceRemap=stored;setLoad(82,'TNO geography cache loaded…');return stored;}
  if(!_worldGeoFeatures?.length)throw new Error('TNO compatibility cannot rebuild the original map: world geography is unavailable.');

  const legacyFeats=_worldGeoFeatures.filter(inLegacyRegion);
  const legacyProj=d3.geoMercator().fitSize([WORLD_W,WORLD_H],PROJ_BBOX);
  const bounds=_legacyTnoValidBounds(legacyProj),N=WORLD_W*WORLD_H;
  const legacyCountry=new Int16Array(N);legacyCountry.fill(-1);
  const cTiles=Array.from({length:legacyFeats.length},()=>[]);
  const legacySeedMaps=Array.from({length:legacyFeats.length},()=>null);
  const off=(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')?new OffscreenCanvas(WORLD_W,WORLD_H):document.createElement('canvas');
  off.width=WORLD_W;off.height=WORLD_H;
  const oc=off.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true}),path=d3.geoPath(legacyProj,oc);

  // Recreate the old regional country raster in exactly the same feature order.
  // Bounding-box reads are pixel-identical to the old full-canvas read, but avoid
  // scanning 1.7M transparent pixels for every country.
  for(let cid=0;cid<legacyFeats.length;cid++){
    const feat=legacyFeats[cid],bb=path.bounds(feat);
    let x0=Math.max(bounds.x0,Math.floor(bb[0][0])-2),y0=Math.max(bounds.y0,Math.floor(bb[0][1])-2);
    let x1=Math.min(bounds.x1+1,Math.ceil(bb[1][0])+2),y1=Math.min(bounds.y1+1,Math.ceil(bb[1][1])+2);
    const w=Math.max(0,x1-x0),h=Math.max(0,y1-y0);
    if(w&&h){
      oc.clearRect(x0,y0,w,h);oc.beginPath();path(feat);oc.fillStyle='#fff';oc.fill();
      const img=oc.getImageData(x0,y0,w,h).data;
